### [1] [Detecting Einstein metrics on products of spheres](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00066)
*Anna Siffert*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该论文在四个乘积流形上构造了无穷多个互不等距的实解析爱因斯坦度量，这些度量非乘积、非经典Böhm度量，通过对Ricci-平坦极限的振荡相位分析及内外匹配论证满足闭合条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在给定乘积流形$S^5\times S^2, S^5\times S^3, S^5\times S^4, S^7\times S^2$上构造非同质、非乘积的无穷多个Einstein度量，避开经典两和项Böhm度量的范畴。

Method: 度量形式为$dt^2+a(t)^2g_{S^p}+b(t)^2g_{S^p}+c(t)^2g_{S^q}$，$p,q\ge2$，$2p+q\le8$。从单奇异轨道两个非坍缩半径极小的解出发，放大该区域使Einstein方程逼近Ricci-平坦问题，其解以衰减振荡趋于锥。提取两个响应量：其一确定公共振荡相位，去除相位后第二闭合条件化为标量符号变化，再用一致的内外匹配论证将极限符号变化转移到精确Einstein方程。当初始尺度趋于零，相位任意多次旋转，闭合条件无穷多次满足。

Result: 构造出无穷多个互不等距的实解析Einstein度量；除有限多个成员外，均不同距或同位于Riemannian乘积或经典两和项Böhm度量。

Conclusion: 通过将闭合条件归结为Ricci-平坦极限中提取的响应量与振荡相位，并借助内–外匹配，证明了在初始尺度趋于零时闭合条件无穷多次满足，从而得到无穷多个非平凡Einstein度量。

Abstract: On \[ S^5\times S^2,\qquad S^5\times S^3,\qquad S^5\times S^4,\qquad S^7\times S^2, \] we construct infinitely many pairwise non-isometric real-analytic Einstein metrics. After discarding finitely many members, none is isometric or homothetic to a Riemannian product or to a classical two-summand Böhm metric. The metrics have the form \[ dt^2+a(t)^2g_{S^p}+b(t)^2g_{S^p}+c(t)^2g_{S^q}, \qquad p,q\ge2,\qquad 2p+q\le8. \]
  The construction starts with solutions for which the two non-collapsing radii at one singular orbit are very small. After magnifying this region, the Einstein equations approach a Ricci-flat problem whose solutions tend to a cone by a decaying oscillation. The main point is to determine exactly which part of this oscillation can still be seen by the closing conditions at the other end of the manifold. Rather than following the full, increasingly oscillatory shooting map, we extract two response quantities from the Ricci-flat limit. One determines the common oscillatory phase; after that phase has been removed, the second closing condition is reduced to a scalar sign change. A uniform inner--outer matching argument transfers this limiting sign change to the exact Einstein equations. As the initial scale tends to zero, the phase makes arbitrarily many turns, and the closing conditions are therefore met infinitely many times.

</details>


### [2] [Gradient Ricci Solitons on PTR-Submanifolds of Complex Space Forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00077)
*Jae Won Lee*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究复空间形式中部分全实（PTR）子流形上的梯度Ricci孤立子，在非零全纯截曲率下建立Gauss-孤立子方程，将切向Kähler算子的平方P²由Hessian自同态与外在Ricci算子重构，从而用内在孤立子数据与第二基本形式刻画PTR切向谱。对紧致极小正曲率PTR孤立子给出λ≤c(n−1)/4及相对零化条件下的严格版本，并证明紧致平凡性准则、无迹各向异性恒等式、平行数据刚性定理与紧致实超曲面上的不存在性定理，最后以平坦多级高斯模型与非平坦Segre CR-乘积作一致性检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在将梯度Ricci孤立子理论推广到复空间形式中的PTR子流形，刻画外在几何（第二基本形式）与内在孤立子数据如何共同决定切向Kähler算子的谱结构，并建立相应的曲率估计、刚性、平凡性与不存在性结果。

Method: 在非零环境全纯截曲率下推导Gauss-孤立子方程，用Hessian自同态与外在Ricci算子重构切向Kähler算子平方P²；结合PTR切向谱分析、相对零化条件与无迹各向异性恒等式；通过平坦多级高斯模型与非平坦Segre CR-乘积进行显式一致性检验。

Result: Gauss-孤立子方程成功重构P²并以内在孤立子数据加第二基本形式编码PTR切向谱；对紧致极小正曲率PTR孤立子得到λ≤c(n−1)/4，相对零化条件下为严格不等式；同时证明紧致平凡性准则、精确无迹各向异性恒等式、平行数据刚性定理和紧致实超曲面不存在性定理；两类显式模型验证了重构公式并刻画出其极限情形。

Conclusion: 梯度Ricci孤立子在PTR子流形上的几何受外在第二基本形式与内在孤立子数据的联合约束；所建立的重构公式与谱编码为研究复空间形式子流形的孤立子刚性、曲率界与不存在性提供了统一框架。

Abstract: We study gradient Ricci solitons on partially totally real (PTR) submanifolds of complex space forms. In nonzero ambient holomorphic sectional curvature, the Gauss--soliton equation reconstructs the square $P^2$ of the tangential Kähler operator from the Hessian endomorphism and an extrinsic Ricci operator. Consequently, the PTR tangency spectrum is encoded by intrinsic soliton data together with the second fundamental form. For compact minimal PTR solitons with positive ambient curvature we obtain $λ\le c(n-1)/4$, with a strict version under a relative-nullity condition. We also prove compact triviality criteria, an exact trace-free anisotropy identity, a parallel-data rigidity result, and a nonexistence theorem for compact real hypersurfaces. A flat multi-level Gaussian model and a nonflat Segre $CR$-product provide explicit consistency checks and identify the limiting cases of the reconstruction formulas.

</details>


### [3] [A Quantitative Reeb-Form Obstruction for Real Hypersurfaces of Strict Nearly Kähler Six-Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00078)
*Jae Won Lee,Chul Woo Lee*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 证明六维严格近Kähler流形的五维定向实超曲面不存在Reeb一形式闭的情形，即(dη)_p≠0处处成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究六维严格近Kähler流形中超曲面的诱导近接触结构，考察是否可能出现dη=0（即接触化退化）的局部情形，并探讨其对Yano半对称度量联络曲率的影响。

Method: 局部方法：利用超曲面恒等式∇_Xξ=φAX−G(X,N)及六维严格近Kähler代数。假设(dη)_p=0推出φA+Aφ=2G_N，导出Hopf条件；在接触超平面D_p=kerη_p上证明φA+Aφ与φ交换，而G_N反交换且G_N^2=−αI（α>0），产生矛盾。

Result: 证明点wise障碍(dη)_p≠0对每个p∈M成立；作为联络推论，对与ξ相关的标准Yano半对称度量联络∇̂_XY=∇_XY+η(Y)X−g(X,Y)ξ，在五维下Skew Ric^{∇̂}=−3/2 dη，故Ric^{∇̂}处处非对称；该障碍排除了任何定义条件含dη=0的局部诱导近接触类。

Conclusion: 六维严格近Kähler流形的五维实超曲面上的诱导Reeb一形式必不闭，dη无处为零；Yano半对称度量联络的Ricci曲率无处对称；结果在标准齐性严格近Kähler空间上得到验证。

Abstract: Let $M^5$ be an oriented real hypersurface of a six-dimensional strict nearly Kähler manifold $(\widetilde M^6,\widetilde g,J)$, and let $ξ=-JN$ and $η=g(\,\cdot\,,ξ)$ denote the induced Reeb field and Reeb one-form. We prove the pointwise obstruction \[ (dη)_p\neq0\qquad\text{for every }p\in M. \] The proof is local and uses only the hypersurface identity \[ \nabla_Xξ=φAX-G(X,N) \] together with the six-dimensional strict nearly Kähler algebra. If $(dη)_p=0$, then \[ φA+Aφ=2G_N,\qquad G_NX=G(X,N), \] which forces the Hopf condition. On the contact hyperplane $\mathcal D_p=\kerη_p$, the endomorphism $φA+Aφ$ then commutes with $φ$, whereas $G_N$ anti-commutes with $φ$ and satisfies \[ G_N^2=-αI_{\mathcal D_p},\qquad α>0. \] This contradiction proves the obstruction. As a connection-theoretic consequence, for the standard Yano semi-symmetric metric connection associated with $ξ$, \[ \widehat\nabla_XY=\nabla_XY+η(Y)X-g(X,Y)ξ, \] one has in dimension five \[ \operatorname{Skew}\operatorname{Ric}^{\widehat\nabla}=-\frac32\,dη, \] and hence $\operatorname{Ric}^{\widehat\nabla}$ is nowhere symmetric. The obstruction also rules out any local induced almost contact class whose defining conditions include $dη=0$. We illustrate the result on the standard homogeneous strict nearly Kähler spaces.

</details>


### [4] [A counterexample to Yau's bounded-mean-curvature embedding question](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00153)
*Haoxuan Cheng*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文否定了Yau关于完备Ricci有界、单射半径正流形可等距嵌入欧氏空间且具界平均曲率的猜想，构造出R^4上Ricci曲率有界、单射半径正但全曲率无界的反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Yau提出问题：完备Ricci曲率有界且单射半径正的黎曼流形是否总能等距嵌入某欧氏空间并具有界平均曲率。该问题关乎曲率控制与等距嵌入之间的定量联系，作者旨在给出反例以否定这一猜想。

Method: 在R^4上构造光滑完备度量，由彼此不交的曲率"峰"拼装而成，使Ricci收缩一致有界而全曲率无界；利用短测地曲率估计和Jacobi方程证明单射半径有一致下界；再用Gauss方程论证任何C^2等距浸入到有限维欧氏空间必导致平均曲率无界。构造可推广至任意维数≥4，且在4维时Ricci曲率可趋于无穷远处为零。

Result: 得到R^4上完备光滑度量：Ricci曲率有界、单射半径正，但全曲率无界。Gauss方程迫使该度量的任何C^2等距浸入到有限维欧氏空间都具有无界平均曲率，从而构成Yau猜想的反例。该构造在维数≥4均成立，4维时Ricci曲率可趋于零。

Conclusion: Yau的猜想不成立：Ricci曲率有界与单射半径正并不能保证存在具有界平均曲率的等距嵌入。反例在维数≥4普遍存在，说明需要更强曲率控制（如全曲率有界）才能获得此类嵌入。

Abstract: Yau asked whether every complete Riemannian manifold with bounded Ricci curvature and positive injectivity radius admits an isometric embedding into some Euclidean space with bounded mean curvature. We construct a counterexample: a smooth complete metric on $\mathbb{R}^4$ with bounded Ricci curvature, positive injectivity radius, and unbounded full curvature. The Gauss equation then forces every $C^2$ isometric immersion of this metric into a finite-dimensional Euclidean space to have unbounded mean curvature. The metric is assembled from disjoint curvature peaks whose Ricci contractions stay uniformly bounded. A short-geodesic curvature estimate and the Jacobi equation give the uniform injectivity-radius bound. The construction works in every dimension at least four, and in dimension four the Ricci curvature may be chosen to tend to zero at infinity.

</details>


### [5] [Minimality and Nonlinear Spectral Rigidity of Hypersurfaces in Real Space Forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00214)
*Zihao Wang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 研究欧氏、球面与双曲空间中超曲面的非线性平均曲率谱方程，证明谱参数σ≥nc时的极小性与σ<nc时的严格平均曲率界，涵盖幂函数及正混合响应，进而解决Chen猜想与双调和极小性，并给出尖锐性与定量刚性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究多局限于特定几何或特定响应函数（如双调和、p-调和），缺乏统一处理欧氏、球面、双曲三类空间的一般非线性平均曲率谱方程的理论框架，且Chen猜想与常负曲率下的双调和极小性等基本问题尚未在无需完备性/完整性的假设下得到解决。

Method: 对容许解析响应F与环境截面曲率c，采用实解析延拓与焦痕（focal-trace）论证，统一处理三种几何；对σ≥nc证明极小性，对σ<nc在梯度点处建立严格平均曲率界；通过显式脐点模型验证谱极小范围的尖锐性；用定量方法证明紧致非极小测地球与等半径Clifford超曲面的局部刚性。

Result: 当σ≥nc时解为极小；当σ<nc时梯度点处平均曲率满足严格界。该类响应包含所有幂F(t)=t^{p-1}(p>1)及满足显式宽度条件的正混合。由此证明欧氏余维一的Chen猜想及常负曲率中的双调和极小性，无需完备性或完整性假设。显式脐点模型表明无条件谱极小范围是尖锐的。此外，证明了三种几何中紧致非极小测地球（模环境等距）及球面中真等半径Clifford超曲面在模型振幅附近对满足F,F'>0的光滑律的定量局部刚性。

Conclusion: 该工作为三类常曲率空间中的非线性平均曲率谱方程提供了统一框架，明确了谱参数阈值σ=nc的极小/非极小分界并证明其尖锐性，同时获得若干刚性结果，推广并解决了Chen猜想与双调和极小性等经典问题。

Abstract: We study nonlinear mean-curvature spectral equations for hypersurfaces in Euclidean, spherical and hyperbolic space. For an admissible analytic response $F$ and ambient sectional curvature $c$, we prove minimality when the spectral parameter satisfies $σ\ge nc$, and strict mean-curvature bounds at gradient points when $σ<nc$. The class includes every power $F(t)=t^{p-1}$, $p>1$, and positive mixtures satisfying an explicit width condition. In particular, the result proves Chen's conjecture in Euclidean codimension one and biharmonic minimality in constant negative curvature, without completeness or holonomicity. A common real-analytic continuation and focal-trace argument treats all three geometries. Explicit umbilical models show that the unconditional spectral minimality range is sharp. Independently, we prove quantitative local rigidity, modulo ambient isometries, of compact nonminimal geodesic spheres in all three geometries and proper equal-radius Clifford hypersurfaces in spheres, for smooth laws with $F,F'>0$ near the model amplitude.

</details>


### [6] [Minimal equatorial fillings of low index](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00247)
*Jacob Bernstein,Daniel Ketover*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究 $\mathbb{S}^3$ 中极小子流形作为赤道面填充的几何与拓扑限制，聚焦于最低可能 Morse 指数的情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解三维球面中极小曲面填充赤道曲线的几何与拓扑约束，特别是最小 Morse 指数这一极端情形下的刚性现象。

Method: 运用极小曲面理论中的 Morse 指数分析与变分方法，对 $\mathbb{S}^3$ 中填充赤道曲线的极小曲面进行研究。

Result: 得到了最低 Morse 指数的极小赤道填充在几何与拓扑上的若干限制性结论。

Conclusion: 最低 Morse 指数的极小赤道填充受到显著的几何与拓扑约束，体现出该类极小子流形的刚性特征。

Abstract: We obtain restrictions on the geometry and topology of minimal equatorial fillings in $\mathbb{S}^3$ of the lowest possible Morse index.

</details>


### [7] [An exact hypergeometric Weierstrass representation for Smyth's free-boundary minimal disk in the regular tetrahedron](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00434)
*Steven Finch*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 对正则四面体，显式给出Smyth所证三张正交极小圆盘的Weierstrass数据：Gauss映射为角(π/4, π/2, πβ/2)、β=arccos(-1/3)/π的Schwarz三角函数，超几何参数关于β线性、无附属参数与自由参数；并给出κ的Γ函数闭式、g'的Wronskian闭式、总曲率2π(2β-1)、数据对称平方结构及非代数性刚性结论，数值验证至28位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Smyth(1984)仅证明了任意四面体内存在三张与边界正交的嵌入极小圆盘（作为斜四边形的Plateau解之共轭面）这一存在性结果，但未给出具体解析数据。本文旨在对正则四面体这一特殊情形给出完全显式的Weierstrass表示，并借此揭示其代数性质与刚性特征。

Method: 以正则四面体为对象，构造斜四边形Q（边取为c_iν_i，c_i为面面积、ν_i为外法向）上的Plateau解并取共轭面。显式写出Weierstrass数据：Gauss映射取角为(π/4, π/2, πβ/2)、β=arccos(-1/3)/π的Schwarz三角（圆-弧三角）函数；确定超几何参数（关于β线性）并证明无附属参数、无自由参数；进一步给出κ的Γ函数闭式、g'的Wronskian闭式，分析数据的对称平方结构，并用单值群性质论证刚性。

Result: 获得正则四面体的完全显式Weierstrass数据：超几何参数关于β线性，且无附属参数、无自由参数；给出κ的Γ值闭式与g'的Wronskian闭式；精确总曲率为2π(2β-1)；揭示数据的对称平方结构；并证明因β/2无理，射影单值群稠密，数据不可能为代数的。数值方面，表示可延展至28位有效数字，Area/ℓ²=0.2172341554075574483760009126…，与Ken Brakke 2013年Surface Evolver计算相符。

Conclusion: 本文为Smyth的存在性定理在正则四面体情形提供了完整显式解，表明其极小圆盘数据由Schwarz三角函数严格确定、无自由参数，且本质为非代数（因单值群稠密）。精确总曲率2π(2β-1)与高精度数值结果验证了Surface Evolver计算，凸显该几何对象的刚性。

Abstract: Smyth (1984) proved that every tetrahedron $T\subset\mathbb{R}^3$ contains three embedded minimal disks meeting $\partial T$ orthogonally, obtained as conjugate surfaces of Plateau solutions in a skew quadrilateral $Q$ whose edges are $c_iν_i$, where $\varphi_1,\varphi_2,\varphi_3,\varphi_4$ are faces of $T$, $c_i=\operatorname{Area}(\varphi_i)$ and $ν_i$ are outward normal vectors. For the $\textit{regular}$ tetrahedron we give the Weierstrass data explicitly. The Gauss map is a Schwarz triangle function for a circular-arc triangle with angles $(π/4,\ π/2,\ πβ/2)$, where $β=\arccos(-1/3)/π$; the hypergeometric parameters are linear in $β$ and there is $\textit{no accessory parameter and no free parameter}$. We record a closed form for $κ$ in $Γ$-values, a Wronskian closed form for $g'$, the exact total curvature $2π(2β-1)$, the symmetric-square structure of the data, and a rigidity statement: since $β/2$ is irrational, the projective monodromy is dense and the data $\textit{cannot}$ be algebraic. Numerically the representation extends to 28 digits -- $\operatorname{Area}/\ell^{2}=0.2172341554075574483760009126\ldots$ -- confirming a 2013 Surface Evolver computation of Ken Brakke.

</details>


### [8] [The Wasserstein space of the circle is not flat](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00625)
*André Magalhães de Sá Gomes,Christian S. Rodrigues,Luiz A. B. San Martin*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 圆的Wasserstein空间不是平坦的，其截面曲率由Fourier系数上的辛形式控制，曲率在n阶Fourier模态平面上以3n²为界且无界；该结果纠正了"底流形一维则Wasserstein空间平坦"的普遍预期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前普遍认为Wasserstein空间的平坦性与其底流形的维数相关，实数轴与区间的Wasserstein空间均平坦，因此自然预期一维底流形（如圆）的Wasserstein空间亦平坦。本文旨在检验并纠正这一预期，深入刻画圆上Wasserstein空间的曲率结构。

Method: 采用Otto的形式Riemannian结构计算曲率，并从W_2定义直接验证。在由Laplacian特征函数（Lott观察到其存在）诱导的P^∞(M)切丛全局标架下进行运算，证明该标架是每个切空间的Schauder基，并写出Otto度量及其Levi-Civita联络。在圆的情形给出P^∞(S^1)度量与联络系数的闭形式（密度的Fourier系数的有限组合）。

Result: 圆的Wasserstein空间非平坦。其截面曲率由Fourier系数上的辛形式支配：某平面平坦当且仅当它对该形式是各向同性的；由第n阶Fourier模态张成的平面上曲率上界为3n²，等号恰在均匀测度处取得，故曲率无界。从W_2定义直接验证还表明，P_2(S^1)中均匀测度的任何邻域都不是CAT(0)。

It is therefore unbounded. We compute it inside the formal Riemannian structure of Otto and confirm it directly from the definition of $W_2$, which also shows that no neighbourhood of the uniform measure in $P_2(S^1)$ is CAT(0). The result corrects the widely stated expectation that a Wasserstein space is flat whenever its base manifold is one-dimensional.

Abstract: We study complete translating solitons for the mean curvature flow in arbitrary codimension under quadratic pinching of the second fundamental form. We prove a codimension reduction theorem at the Andrews-Baker threshold: if a complete $n$-dimensional connected translator isometrically immersed in $(\mathbb{R}^{n+p},\langle\cdot,\cdot\rangle)$ satisfies \[ |B|^2\leq \left(\frac{4}{3n}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)|H|^2 \] for some $\varepsilon_{0}>0$, then either it is an affine $n$-plane or $|H|>0$ everywhere and the translator is contained in an $(n+1)$-dimensional affine subspace. In particular, this improves in low dimensions the pinching range previously obtained from codimension reduction results for ancient mean curvature flows. Our proof is purely elliptic: viewing translators as weighted minimal submanifolds, we combine Simons-type identities for the drift Laplacian with refined gradient estimates and an Omori-Yau maximum principle. As a consequence, under the stronger pinching condition \[ |B|^2\leq (c_n-\varepsilon_{0})|H|^2,\qquad c_n=\min\left\{\frac{4}{3n},\frac{1}{n-2}\right\} \] for $n\geq3$, with $c_n=2/3$ when $n=2$, we obtain a rigidity theorem: the translator is either an affine plane or a bowl soliton contained in an $(n+1)$-dimensional affine subspace. </details>

Abstract: Hessian structure on a manifold is a geometric structure consisting of a pair $(\nabla,g)$ of an affine connection $\nabla$ and a Riemannian metric $g$ satisfying certain compatibility conditions. It is also known as a dually flat structure in information geometry. Hessian geometry is considered to be a real analogue of Kähler geometry; in particular, Hessian sectional curvature corresponds to holomorphic sectional curvature in Kähler geometry. The standard models of Hessian manifolds with constant Hessian sectional curvature were classified by Furuhata and Kurose (2013) in the nonpositive curvature case, whereas the positive curvature case is open. In this paper, we classify the standard models in the positive curvature case. For this end, we introduce Hessian structure on quotient manifolds of Kähler manifolds equipped with suitable Hamiltonian actions of abelian Lie groups and realizing the standard models as such quotient manifolds.

Abstract: We construct the first examples of quasi-contact metric structures that are not contact metric in every odd dimension greater than or equal to $5$. This yields compact, homogeneous examples on $S^5$. Abstract: For integer $b\ge3$, let $\Sig\subset\Ball$ be an embedded free boundary minimal surface (FBMS) of genus zero with $b$ boundary components. Suppose that $\Sig$ is invariant under the prismatic group of order $4b$, the rotation of order $b$ permutes the boundary components cyclically, and the reflection in the equatorial plane preserves each of them. We show that the Morse index of $\Sig$ is $2b$ and its nullity is three; moreover, we determine the negative space of the index form as a representation of the symmetry group. Examples include the $b$-noids of Karpukhin, Kusner, McGrath, and Stern and, for large $b$, the genus-zero surfaces of Folha, Pacard, and Zolotareva. To our knowledge, apart from the equatorial disc and the critical catenoid, these are the first FBMS in the ball whose Morse indices are known precisely. There are also lower bounds for the index of surfaces with rotational or polyhedral symmetry. The main new ingredient is an equivariant comparison, using the representation theory of finite groups, between area and energy, in which the Teichmüller directions are counted by symmetry type using circle domains.

Abstract: The Ahlfors--Weill construction explicitly extends a univalent function $f$ with Schwarzian norm less than $1/2$ to a quasiconformal homeomorphism of the Riemann sphere, an extension that can be understood geometrically through Epstein surfaces. For asymptotically conformal curves, the uniformization map can be extended to a larger disk, and Loewner theory can be used to verify that this extension is quasiconformal. In this paper, we construct a local quasireflection with such an extension. Using this local quasireflection, we modify classical estimates of Ahlfors and show that domains bounded by asymptotically conformal curves have asymptotically similar hyperbolic and quasihyperbolic metrics. Furthermore, by combining these estimates with a geometric reinterpretation of the extension through Epstein surfaces, we show that the Epstein-Poincaré surfaces of the complementary domains are arbitrarily close to one another near the curve.

Abstract: We prove stability of concentration in the Paley-Wiener space for each fixed time-bandwidth product. Abstract: Baxter's classical theorem for orthogonal polynomials on the unit circle establishes that the linear Fourier coefficients of a measure are in $\ell^1$ if and only if the nonlinear coefficients, i.e., the so-called Verblunsky coefficients, are in $\ell^1$. Abstract: Prescribed positive bidiagonal factorizations of semi-infinite banded matrices reveal spectral, approximation, and integrable structures for arbitrary lower and upper bandwidths. Every cyclic permutation of the factors admits a normalized lower--upper positive factorization. Under suitable degree conditions, factor transfers induce matrix Christoffel transformations, linking the cyclic Darboux orbit to mixed-type Favard theory.
  Christoffel words determine minimum-height positive refactorizations and the cyclic products that reach them. The associated matrices have two block displacements, reducing to two diagonals for coprime bandwidths. Their finite nonzero spectra lie on a star, and planar networks yield positive radial Stieltjes moment sequences. In the compact radial case star-supported representing measures are characterized by conditions at the origin and negative fractional moments. For coprime bandwidths, a radial moment deformation gives determinant solutions of a sparse Lax hierarchy and synchronizes the cyclic Toda flows.
  Retaining the prescribed factor order yields matrix continued fractions with explicit Padé-type contact, denominator factorizations, backward evaluation, and error bounds. For bounded nonnegative factors, the convergents are monotone and converge to the least nonnegative solution in the admissible domain. Under the Favard identification, they approximate the mixed-type Weyl matrix. Unbounded factorizations retain the formal approximation; analytic convergence requires a closed realization and stability hypotheses, with an additional identification for measure-defined Weyl matrices. Piñeiro and Jacobi-like systems give applications, including global positive coefficientwise integrable solutions under the stated positivity and AT hypotheses.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弦-切线运算的结合律是椭圆曲线群结构的核心，但传统几何或代数证明往往依赖特定特征假设（如避免特征2、3）或较繁重的代数几何工具。作者希望给出一个在任意特征下都成立的、以初等多项式运算为主的证明。

Result: 证明了两条括号方式（先P⊕Q再加R与先Q⊕R再加P）由普通或Hermite插值确定的同一二次多项式给出，从而得到相同的最终点，结合律成立。对短Weierstrass形式另给一个简洁证明，将q等同于Zwegers构造中的插值抛物线。

Conclusion: 弦-切线运算的结合律可在任意特征下用多项式恒等式与插值统一证明，避免了对特征的限制，并揭示了其与Zwegers插值抛物线构造的联系。

Abstract: Let $K$ be a field and let $E/K$ be a nonsingular projective curve in general Weierstrass form. We prove associativity of its chord--tangent operation by combining polynomial factorization, formal differentiation, and quadratic interpolation. The main identity eliminates the two nonvertical lines $Y=\ell(X)$ and $Y=m(X)$ occurring in the successive additions $S=P\oplus Q$ and $T=S\oplus R$. There are $c\in K$ and a monic quadratic polynomial $h\in K[X]$ such that \[
  \ell+m+\Apol=c(X-x_S),
  \qquad
  \Gpol+\ell m=(X-x_S)h, \] and \[
  (Y-\ell)(Y-m)
  =(X-x_S)(h-cY)
  +Y^2+\Apol(X)Y-\Gpol(X). \] When $c\ne0$, the graph of $q=h/c$ contains $P,Q,R$, and $-T$. The two bracketings determine the same quadratic by ordinary or Hermite interpolation and therefore yield the same final point. The proof applies in every characteristic, since tangency is expressed through the formal partial derivatives of the Weierstrass equation. For the short Weierstrass form, a separate concise proof identifies $q$ with the interpolating parabola in Zwegers's construction.

</details>

### [31] [An explicit family of twists of the Carlitz module with unbounded analytic rank](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00379)
*David Niedbala Giraudin*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: Grishkov-Logachev提出的Carlitz模扭动的解析秩是否有界的猜想被否定：对任意q≥3，解析秩无界，并给出了明确的图论刻画与公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回答Grishkov与Logachev在其综述Problem 1.4中提出的问题：对固定的有限域F_q，Carlitz模的扭动的解析秩是否有界。

Method: 对q=p^s、n≥1取P_{q,n}=(theta^{q^n}-theta)^{q-2}，研究Grishkov-Logachev矩阵M(P_{q,n},1,k)的有向图，给出其回路的显式描述：两两不交、不涉及含t的项，且与E^n在循环旋转下的轨道一一对应（E为[0,q-2]中使p不整除l+1的l构成的集合）。

Result: L(C_P,t,T)不依赖于t，按每条轨道分解为因子1-wt(nu)T^{per(nu)}的乘积，解析秩等于所有权重为1的轨道的p^{v_p(per(nu))}之和。当n为p的幂时该值等于(q/p)^n(p-1)^{n-1}，故无界。该论证同时回答了综述中的问题3.6.1至3.6.4。

Conclusion: 对每个q≥3，Carlitz模扭动的解析秩无界，从而给出Grishkov-Logachev问题的否定答案，并进一步解决了该综述3.6.1-3.6.4的相关问题。

Abstract: Grishkov and Logachev have asked (Problem 1.4 of their recent survey) whether, for a fixed finite field F_q, the analytic ranks of the twists of the Carlitz module are bounded. We answer this in the negative for every q >= 3. For q = p^s and n >= 1 put P_{q,n} = (theta^(q^n) - theta)^(q-2). We show that the directed graph of the matrix M(P_{q,n},1,k) of Grishkov-Logachev has a completely explicit set of circuits: they are pairwise disjoint, none of them meets an entry involving t, and they are in bijection with the orbits of E^n under cyclic rotation, where E is the set of l in [0,q-2] with p not dividing l+1. Consequently L(C_P, t, T) does not depend on t, it factors as a product of one term 1 - wt(nu) T^per(nu) per orbit, and the analytic rank equals the sum of p^(v_p(per(nu))) over the orbits of weight 1. When n is a power of p this equals (q/p)^n (p-1)^(n-1), which is unbounded. The argument also answers questions 3.6.1 to 3.6.4 of the survey for this family.

</details>

Abstract: The integer Chebyshev constant of [0,1] has been bracketed by 0.4213 <= t_Z([0,1]) <= 0.422685 since the work of Pritsker (2005) and of Flammang (2014). We prove
  0.4222286000 <= t_Z([0,1]) <= 0.4226846975,
  narrowing the interval from 1.39e-3 to 4.56e-4. Both bounds are established by explicit certificates. The upper bound is carried by an integer polynomial with 92 factors and explicit integer exponents; the lower bound by a discrete measure on 947 cells together with a library of 206 irreducible polynomials, through an inequality in which the terms paying for divisibility are made explicit. Two self-contained scripts, included as ancillary files, re-derive both bounds from the certificates alone.

</details>

Abstract: A longstanding open problem concerning Ramanujan-Sato series is given by determining an expression for the coefficients $c_{11}(n)$ associated with Ramanujan-Sato series of level 11 with a fixed, nested, finite binomial sum for all nonnegative integers $n$, by direct analogy with the known formulas for the coefficients for lower-level Ramanujan-Sato series (and this is clarified and made explicit in our paper). This problem seems to have remained open. We succeed in solving this problem, by introducing and proving a double binomial sum formula for $c_{11}(n)$ that is directly analogous to the coefficient formulas for lower-level Ramanujan-Sato series.

Conclusion: 平面结果在至多差一个对数因子的意义下是最优的，表明所得下界在量级上基本不可改进。

Abstract: Let $p$ be an odd prime, let $m\geq1$ be an integer, and let $Q$ be a nondegenerate quadratic form on $\mathbb{F}_p^{2m}$ with Witt index $m-1$. For a nonempty set $E\subseteq\mathbb{F}_p^{2m}$, write $Δ_Q(E)=\{Q(x-y):x,y\in E\}$. We prove that, whenever $|E|\geq p^m$, $$ |Δ_Q(E)|\gg \frac{p}{\log\bigl(2+p^{m+1}/|E|\bigr)}, $$ with an absolute implied constant independent of $p$, $m$ and $Q$. In the planar case $m=1$, we also prove that $$ |Δ_Q(E)|\gg\frac{|E|}{\log(2|E|)}, \qquad (1\leq |E|\leq p), $$ which is optimal up to a logarithmic factor.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

We further construct a genuinely nonunit recurrent four-cycle with infinitely many positive integral points and show that its dynamics admits a uniform Pell-type linearization. If, however, all the rows and all the columns of the aforementioned square of squares add up to a common sum, but the two diagonals do not, the original magic square is called pseudo-bimagic. Abstract: We construct indefinite theta representations for a family of signed generalized Nahm sums of arbitrary rank \(r\geq2\). Abstract: In this paper, we study the number of points of $\mathbb{Z}$-schemes $X$ of finite type over the finite rings $\mathbb{Z}/p^m\mathbb{Z}$ for positive integers $m$ and prime numbers $p$. To prove our estimate, beside using the usual tools such as resolutions of singularities and Denef's formula for Igusa local zeta functions, we also use the Hermit interpolation formula to study the numerators of Igusa local zeta functions. By the same method, we are able to approach Igusa's conjecture for exponential sums in the case of rational singularities. Abstract: We bound the error term in the counting function of cubic extensions of $\mathbb{Q}$ by $\mathcal{O}_ε\left(X^{θ+ε}\right)$, with $θ< 2/3$, improving upon results due to Bhargava, Taniguchi, and Thorne. The proof proceeds by relating a certain discriminant-reducing sieve, due to Bhargava, Shankar, and Tsimerman, to averages of Artin characters associated with the Artin $L$-function $ζ_K(s)/ζ(s)$, with $K$ a cubic field. Our arguments can also handle finitely many splitting conditions.

This also proves that at least $75\%$ of these $L$-functions are non-vanishing at the central point, conditionally improving upon results of Shankar, Södergren and Templier.
  Finally, one may study similar questions over a rational function field $\mathbb{F}_q(T)$, with $q$ coprime to $2$ and $3$. Here, we obtain a proportion of non-vanishing of at least $75\%$ for the analogous family of $L$-functions. Furthermore, we obtain a bound $\mathcal{O}(X^{1/2+ε})$ for the error term in the counting function of cubic function fields, improving previous results of the author. Conclusion: 该工作完整刻画了D_k(X)对每个k的增长阶，验证了D_6(X)≫X的猜想，并在附录中给出了构造完全m次幂的一般性结果。

Erdos and Graham asked for the order of growth of $D_k(X)$ for $3\le k\le6$, and conjectured that $D_6(X)\gg X$. Together with classical facts, this determines the order of growth of $D_k(X)$ for every $k$. The exponent $2/5$ comes from balancing a uniform bound for Pell equations against Gallagher's larger sieve, with residue restrictions supplied by the Weil bound. For five and six factors we restrict to integers with a prime factor exceeding $X^{1-α}$, where $α>0$ is small and fixed. We exclude shorter representations by combining an equidistribution estimate for primes of Matomaki, Radziwill, Shao, Tao and Teravainen with the large sieve. In an appendix we use a zero-sum theorem for finite abelian groups to construct, for every $m\ge2$, perfect $m$-th powers that are products of a bounded number of distinct factorials with arguments given by fixed affine functions.

Abstract: Supersingular abelian surfaces are essential in isogeny-based cryptography. Despite this, we have no efficient algorithm to verify if a given abelian surface is supersingular. Main category: math.NT

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Determining whether an algebraic number field admits a power integral basis is a classical problem, but it can be difficult for fields defined by polynomial compositions and dynamical iterates. In this paper, we study the monogeneity of compositions $f(h(x))$, where $f(x)$ and $h(x)$ are monic polynomials over an arbitrary Krull valuation ring and $h(x)$ is a trinomial. We derive explicit formulas for the discriminant of the composition and use them to characterize when $f(h(x))$ generates a monogenic field. In particular, we relate the monogeneity of the composition to that of $f(x)$ and to explicit square-free conditions on the critical values of $h(x)$. We further extend the results to polynomial iteration and obtain a criterion for the monogeneity of binomial iterates, yielding new infinite families of monogenic fields. Finally, we give quantitative results and illustrate our criteria with several examples.

Main category: math.AP

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 通过trace-determinant变量将系统化为两个分离的谱根标量抛物方程并带反对称二次耦合；利用Euler-Poisson-Darboux单侧截断并以混合熵稳定化，得到边界De Giorgi振荡衰减与正时刻Hölder正则性；再由混合熵Hessian给出局部对称化子，利用余法向Schauder估计获得一致C^{1+a}控制，冻结极大L_p正则性给出终端trace一致界以排除有限时刻爆破；相同摩擦情形退化为三角标量抛物系统。

Result: 在任意正二元摩擦系数与相容非负初值下，无需小性条件即证明了系统全局强适定性；具有正守恒质量的每个分量在正时刻严格为正。

Conclusion: 该工作去除了Maxwell-Stefan扩散系统全局适定性中的小性限制，并建立了正性保持性质，将结果推广至一般摩擦系数与有限域情形。

Abstract: We prove global strong well-posedness for the normalized isothermal, isobaric ternary Maxwell-Stefan diffusion system on bounded $C^{2+α_0}$ domains, for arbitrary positive constant binary frictions and compatible nonnegative initial data, without a smallness condition. For distinct frictions, trace--determinant variables produce two separated spectral roots satisfying scalar parabolic equations with antisymmetric quadratic coupling. Euler-Poisson-Darboux one-sided truncations, stabilized by the mixing entropy, yield boundary De Giorgi oscillation decay and positive-time Hölder regularity. The mixing-entropy Hessian then provides a local symmetrizer; conormal Schauder estimates give uniform $C^{1+a}$ control, and frozen maximal $L_p$-regularity yields a uniform terminal trace bound excluding finite-time breakdown. Repeated frictions reduce to triangular scalar parabolic systems. Every component with positive conserved mass is strictly positive for positive time.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 方法上，采用 Stieltjes 坐标变换将 Maxwell–Stefan 摩擦算子对角化到固定特征标架，结合 De Giorgi 迭代建立正则性；针对仿射 Darken 类（组成依赖标量系数、示踪扩散系数一致正）证明全局极大 $L^p$ 强适定性。对一般光滑一致正 Darken 示踪扩散系数，推导直接约束通量形式与精确混合熵对称化，给出 Fisher 耗散结构及组成一致的常法向椭圆性估计，并由此得到局部强适定性。此外，通过把具有相同倍增因子的组分分组，处理常数倍摩擦（含重复因子）情形。

Conclusion: 本文表明 core-diagonal 闭包所对应的仿射 Darken 类可在无小性假设下实现全局强适定性，并刻画其为保持 Stieltjes 特征标架的最大类；同时为更一般的 Darken 示踪扩散系数建立了从约束通量、熵对称化、Fisher 耗散到局部适定性的完整理论。解的一致边界分离、瞬时经典化与指数收敛进一步揭示了该类 Maxwell–Stefan 系统的正则性与渐近稳定性，常数倍摩擦情形也被覆盖。

Abstract: The core-diagonal closure of Bothe and Druet identifies multicomponent Darken diffusion as a thermodynamically consistent Maxwell-Stefan model in which the constitutive data are species tracer diffusivities. Building on the Stieltjes-coordinate and De Giorgi regularity framework introduced in our recent preprint for additive Maxwell-Stefan frictions, we prove global maximal-$L^p$ strong well-posedness, without smallness assumptions, for the affine Darken class with composition-dependent scalar coefficients and uniformly positive tracer diffusivities. We show that this is exactly the maximal Darken class preserving the fixed Stieltjes eigenframe underlying the global argument. For general smooth, uniformly positive Darken tracer diffusivities we also derive the direct constrained flux form, exact mixing-entropy symmetrization, Fisher dissipation, composition-uniform normal ellipticity, and local strong well-posedness. Solutions in the affine class remain uniformly separated from the simplex boundary, become classical for positive times, and converge exponentially to equilibrium. Constant multiplicative frictions, including repeated factors, are covered by grouping species with equal factors.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Stieltjes/测度驱动抛物理论主要针对前向状态或已有Aubin-Lions框架，而测度原子处解的后向演化需要隐式变分预解。作者希望填补后向能量空间下微积分与能量恒等式缺失的空白，并说明原有Aubin-Lions定理在此不自动适用且在线性强制情形下并不必要。

Result: 证明了Banach值侧向乘积公式、弱-强重构定理与Stieltjes型Lions-Magenes能量恒等式；能量恒等式显式包含正跳跃贡献(1/2)Σ_r||u(r+)-u(r)||_H^2；两种构造方法均给出存在性；并论证了现有Aubin-Lions定理对后向能量空间不自动适用但在此线性强制设定下无需使用。

Conclusion: 该工作将Stieltjes微积分推广到后向状态演化问题，建立了完整能量恒等式与存在性理论，并以Stieltjes时间热方程为模型应用。作者将其定位为对既有Stieltjes抛物方程、一般测度驱动演化及算子值测度抛物系统的补充，未主张测度驱动抛物理论或Galerkin逼近的优先权。

Abstract: We develop a Banach-valued lateral Stieltjes calculus adapted to linear evolution equations on a Gelfand triple \[
  V\hookrightarrow H\simeq H^*\hookrightarrow V^*. \] The evolution problem is formulated with the posterior state, \[
  D_g u(t)+A(t) S_g u(t)=f(t),\qquad u(a)=u_0. \] This choice is decisive at atoms of the Stieltjes measure: if $δ=Δ_g(r)>0$, the equation yields the implicit variational resolvent \[
  (I+δA(r))u(r^+)=u(r)+δf(r). \] We prove a Banach-valued lateral product formula, a weak--strong reconstruction theorem, and a Stieltjes analogue of the Lions--Magenes energy identity. In the separable Hilbert setting considered here, the stable projection chain used in the proof is shown to exist automatically by a spectral-band construction. The latter contains the exact positive jump contribution \[
  \frac12\sum_r\|u(r^+)-u(r)\|_H^2. \] Existence is constructed independently by Galerkin approximation and by a Rothe scheme adapted to the Stieltjes mass and its atoms. The Rothe construction does not use spatial projections. We also explain why the available Aubin--Lions theorem for anterior Stieltjes--Bochner evolution graphs is not automatically applicable to the posterior energy space and, in the present linear coercive setting, is not needed. A heat equation with Stieltjes time is included as a model application. The claims are positioned relative to earlier Stieltjes parabolic equations, general measure-driven evolution, and operator-valued-measure parabolic systems; no priority is claimed for measure-driven parabolic theory or Galerkin approximation in general.

</details>

*Xi Chen*

Main category: math.AP

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚焦非线性Schrödinger方程在欧氏空间中的孤子（基态）是稳定、非集中的；而在紧致流形（如环面）上存在周期镜像，通常认为缺乏集中机制。本文旨在证明即使在周期设定下，也可以构造出时间趋于无穷时于一点集中的解，揭示周期镜像间的指数小相互作用可作为集中机制，并刻画其精确渐近行为与唯一性。

Method: 在二维方环面上构造性分析聚焦三次NLS：以移位的相周期化欧氏基态为轮廓，利用轮廓与自身周期镜像之间指数小的相互作用作为驱动机制；通过精确的质量与能量约束选取集中分支（质量取欧氏基态质量、能量为零）；在动约束空间上利用正的能量控制每个固定正则阶的终值问题。

Result: 构造出双时间方向全局、于正无穷处单点集中的光滑解，质量等于欧氏基态质量、能量为零；集中尺度满足 L/λ(t)=2log t+(11/2)log log t+O(1)；解在任意固定Sobolev空间中趋近相位的周期化基态，且 H^s 范数渐近于显式常数乘 (log t)^s；并在构造所用的定量单轮廓渐近类中证明了至多相差常数相位与时间平移的唯一性。

Conclusion: 结果表明：紧致环面上的周期镜像相互作用足以驱动集中现象，给出精确的对数修正集中律与 H^s 范数增长律；精确质量-能量约束选出集中分支，动约束空间上的正能量控制终值问题，从而实现逐阶正则性的全局构造与唯一性。

Abstract: We construct a smooth solution of the focusing cubic nonlinear Schrödinger equation on a square two-dimensional torus which is global in both time directions and concentrates at one point as time tends to positive infinity. Its mass is the mass of the Euclidean ground state and its energy is zero. If $λ(t)$ denotes its concentration scale and $L$ the period, then $L/λ(t)=2\log t+\frac{11}{2}\log\log t+O(1)$. The solution approaches a phased, periodized ground state with an error tending to zero in every fixed Sobolev space. In particular, its $H^s$ norm is asymptotic to an explicit constant times $(\log t)^s$ for every $s>0$. We also prove uniqueness, up to constant phase and time translation, within the quantitative single-profile asymptotic class used in the construction. The mechanism is the exponentially small interaction of the profile with its periodic images. Exact mass and energy constraints select the concentrating branch, while a positive energy on the moving constrained space controls the terminal-value problem at every fixed regularity order.

</details>

*Han Cheng,Changxing Miao,Xiaohua Yao*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究 $\mathbb R^n$（$n\ge3$）上薛定谔算子低能波算子的端点映射性质，重点分析零能共振与零能本征值对 $L^1$ 及 $L^p$ 有界性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画低能波算子在端点空间（如 $L^1$、$L^\infty$）上的有界性，并厘清零能共振与零能本征值这两种临界谱现象对映射性质的阻碍或分类作用。

Method: 在衰减条件 $|V(x)|\lesssim\langle x\rangle^{-\beta}$ 下，分别针对四维零能共振（$\beta>10$）与一般维数零能本征值（$\beta>n+4$）情形，通过识别有限秩障碍并分析本征值修正项的临界渐近剖面来证明结论。

Result: 四维时零能共振（无论是否同时为本征值）会阻止 $L^1$ 有界性；无共振的零能本征值情形下，对任意 $n\ge3$ 得到完整的低能 $L^p$ 分类，其中 $L^\infty$ 有界性等价于对每个零能本征函数 $\psi$，$V\psi$ 的第零、第一及调和第二阶矩均为零。

Conclusion: 零能共振是端点 $L^1$ 有界性的本质障碍；本征值修正项无法抵消其临界渐近剖面，完整分类由矩条件刻画。当相应高能界可用时，结论同样适用于完整波算子。

Abstract: We study endpoint mapping properties of low-energy wave operators for Schrödinger operators $H=-Δ+V$ on $\mathbb R^n$, $n\ge3$. In dimension four, we prove that a zero-energy resonance prevents $L^1$ boundedness, whether or not zero is also an eigenvalue, under $|V(x)|\lesssim\langle x\rangle^{-β}$ with $β>10$. For a zero-energy eigenvalue without a resonance, we obtain a complete low-energy $L^p$ classification in every dimension $n\ge3$ under $β>n+4$. In particular, $L^\infty$ boundedness is equivalent to the vanishing of the zeroth, first, and harmonic second moments of $Vψ$ for every zero-energy eigenfunction $ψ$. The proof identifies the finite-rank obstruction and shows that the remaining eigenvalue correction cannot cancel its critical asymptotic profiles. The same conclusions hold for the full wave operators when the corresponding high-energy bounds are available.

</details>


### [61] [Global Well-Posedness of the Kuramoto--Sivashinsky Equation in One and Two Dimensions under a Codimension-One Constraint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00248)
*Harsh Tiwari,Manil T. Mohan*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究带余维一约束的KS方程在一、二、三维的适定性：一维全局适定，二维在无小性假设下得到全局强解，三维受维数相关障碍限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无约束KS方程的强解全局适定性在二维及以上一般仍是公开问题，作者希望通过引入保持L²范数的约束获得一致先验界，从而突破这一困难。

Method: 对带约束KS方程：一维用Fourier–Galerkin逼近与紧性论证；二、三维用解析半群理论与Banach不动点定理证局部强解；二维结合保持的L²范数、Agmon不等式与高阶能量估计控制非线性项。

Conclusion: 约束使二维KS方程获得无小性假设下的全局适定性，与无约束问题形成对比；同时揭示三维存在本质的维数相关障碍，说明该全局正则性论证不能直接推广到三维。

Abstract: We study the Kuramoto--Sivashinsky (KS) equation in one, two, and three spatial dimensions subject to a codimension-one constraint. The constraint preserves the \(L^2\)-norm of the solution and consequently provides a uniform a priori bound that plays a central role in the higher-order energy estimates. In one spatial dimension, we establish global well-posedness by means of a Fourier--Galerkin approximation and compactness arguments. In two and three dimensions, we first prove the existence and uniqueness of local strong solutions using analytic semigroup theory and the Banach fixed-point theorem. In two dimensions, the preserved \(L^2\)-norm, combined with the Agmon inequality and suitable higher-order energy estimates, allows us to control the nonlinear terms and obtain global-in-time a priori bounds. Consequently, we establish the existence and uniqueness of global strong solutions for arbitrary sufficiently regular initial data, without any smallness assumption. This provides a global well-posedness result for the constrained two-dimensional KS equation, in contrast with the corresponding unconstrained problem, for which global well-posedness of strong solutions remains open in general. In three dimensions, although the constraint yields a uniform \(L^2\)-bound, the available interpolation and Agmon estimates are insufficient to close the higher-order energy estimate. This identifies a genuine dimension-dependent obstruction to extending the global regularity argument to three dimensions.

</details>


### [62] [Vanishing orders of Dirichlet solutions to the Schrödinger equation in dimensions three and higher](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01615)
*Shuowen Feng,Zaihui Gan,Renjin Jiang,Fanghua Lin*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该论文在三维球上构造了具有高阶零点但势函数范数受控的实值 Dirichlet 解，证明消失阶数的最优上界为 C(1+‖V‖_∞^{2/3})，并指出 Kukavica 与 Kenig 独立提出的猜想界一般不可达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

This geometric projection yields a constrained evolution system of finite codimension, ensuring that the state remains on $\mathcal{M}$ for all times. Using a Faedo-Galerkin approximation technique, we establish the global existence and uniqueness of strong solutions to the constrained Navier-Stokes equations with external controls $\boldsymbol{U} \in \mathrm{L}^2(0,T;\mathbb{H})$, together with uniform energy estimates. The associated optimal control problem, minimizing a quadratic cost functional subject to the manifold constraint, is shown to admit at least one optimal pair $(\boldsymbol{u}^*,\boldsymbol{U}^*)$. We derive the first-order necessary optimality conditions by formulating an optimal control system whose state-control pair $(\boldsymbol{u}^*,\boldsymbol{U}^*)$ evolves on the tangent bundle $T\mathcal{M}$ of the constraint manifold $\mathcal{M}$. In this setting, the control satisfies $\boldsymbol{U}^*(t)\in T_{\boldsymbol{u}^*(t)}\mathcal{M}$ for all $t\in[0,T]$, ensuring that the dynamics remain confined to the tangent bundle $T\mathcal{M}$. Furthermore, we establish the Fréchet differentiability of the control-to-state mapping $\boldsymbol{U}\mapsto \boldsymbol{u}_{\boldsymbol{U}}$ in $\mathrm{C}([0,T];\mathbb{H})\cap\mathrm{L}^2(0,T;\mathbb{V})$, with the corresponding linearized state characterized as the unique weak solution of a linearized constrained Navier-Stokes system preserving the tangency condition. These results provide a rigorous analytical foundation for the optimal control of fluid flows constrained to nonlinear manifolds.

Abstract: The one-dimensional planar compressible magnetohydrodynamic system is studied on unbounded domains. The specific volume and temperature possess time-independent upper and lower bounds. Moreover, for each admissible viscosity exponent, the solution converges uniformly in space to the equilibrium state as time tends to infinity.

Abstract: We develop a measure-theoretic framework for small-order limits of nonlocal energies with metric-valued maps. For globally $L^p$ maps, we consider interaction measures whose marginals have uniformly bounded $L^\infty$ densities $a_α$ and $b_α$. If these densities converge weakly-* to $a_\infty$ and $b_\infty$, the normalized energies converge to the $L^p$ energy weighted by $a_\infty+b_\infty$ if and only if the interaction measures escape every bounded rectangle. For maps that are only locally $L^p$, we introduce target laws describing the distribution of the values sampled at infinity. Convergence of their $p$-distance profiles yields the limiting interaction energy for every $1\leq p<\infty$ and arbitrary Polish targets, with integrated $p$-Wasserstein convergence as a sufficient criterion. The arguments include the endpoint $p=1$ and require no linear structure on the target space. Applications include directional and anisotropic Maz'ya--Shaposhnikova formulas, a Bernoulli-law interpretation of fractional perimeters, and an Abelian principle for heat-semigroup energies.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: We develop an intermediate-derivative theory for Banach-valued evolution classes with Morrey control in time. Let $E_0\hookrightarrow E$ be Banach spaces, $m\ge2$, $1<p<\infty$, and $0\leλ<1$. If $u\in\mathcal M^{p,λ}(E_0)$ and $u^{(m)}\in\mathcal M^{p,λ}(E)$, then every intermediate derivative satisfies \[ u^{(j)}\in\mathcal M^{p,λ}\bigl((E_0,E)_{j/m,\infty}\bigr),\qquad 0<j<m, \] with no UMD, Fourier type, or operator-valued multiplier assumption. The proof is based on an exact rational-frequency splitting of $D^j$, the Peetre $K$-functional, and the Hardy--Littlewood maximal operator on Morrey spaces. On the line the estimate has the scale-sharp multiplicative form. On finite intervals we construct a graph-Morrey extension and obtain the same embedding. The weak fine index is optimal for arbitrary Banach couples. Combining the derivative theorem with the exact Morrey trace theorem yields an exact onto trace formula for every $u^{(j)}$, together with mixed Hölder/interpolation embeddings and a trace-powered $p$-to-$q$ Morrey gain. If $E_0\hookc E$, these embeddings become compact both in the same Morrey norm after an arbitrarily small loss of interpolation smoothness and in time-Hölder spaces above the derivative-trace threshold. For positive selfadjoint Hilbert scales we further improve the weak interpolation target to the fractional domain $D(B^{1-j/m})$ and show that this exponent is sharp.

Abstract: We develop an abstract averaging method for equations $Mx=\varepsilon N(x,\varepsilon)$ in Banach spaces. The method applies when the equation can be separated into an averaged compatibility condition and a nonlinear complementary problem, and it allows the principal operator $M$ to be genuinely nonlinear. We prove that every nondegenerate zero of the corresponding averaged equation generates, for all sufficiently small nonzero values of $\varepsilon$, a nearby solution. Under a suitable small Lipschitz condition this solution is locally unique, while in the differentiable case it belongs to a unique local $C^1$ branch. The framework contains the abstract linear Fredholm averaging theory as a special case, but also covers quasilinear problems for which the principal operator has no invertible linearization at the reference state. We also determine the leading asymptotic profile of the solutions. Applications are given to a vector $p$-Laplacian Duffing system and to a nonsmooth problem in a Hilbert space.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: We study the inverse problem of determining a connected, closed Riemannian manifold of dimension at least two from wave observations. The sources are supported in an open set $S$, and the resulting waves are observed on an open receiver set $R$, which may be at a positive distance from $S$. We prove uniqueness up to isometry within the class of manifolds that satisfy a subcritical spectral lower bound: $\|φ|_R\|_{L^2(R)} \ge ce^{-Cλ^θ}\|φ\|_{L^2(M)}$ for every eigenfunction $φ$, where $λ$ is the corresponding eigenvalue, $θ\in[0,1/2)$, and the constants $c,C>0$ do not depend on $φ$. If a bound of this type holds also on $S$, then uniqueness holds within the class of all connected, closed manifolds. By the spectral inequality, the bound always holds with $θ=1/2$. Uniqueness nevertheless fails at this endpoint: in every dimension $n\ge 2$, we construct a pair of nonisometric connected, closed Riemannian $n$-manifolds that produce identical wave observations.

</details>


### [69] [Critical anisotropic $p$-Laplace equations without finite energy: classification and a Schoen-type Harnack inequality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00337)
*Phuong Le*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文在非可逆Minkowski范数生成的各向异性$p$-Laplace方程中，对全范围$1<p<N$完整分类了非负局部弱解：非零解必为各向异性Sobolev气泡，并证明尺度不变的Schoen型Harnack不等式。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Antonini–Ciraolo–Gatti问题A的各向异性版本，去除Chen–Wu–Yan–Yang非能量各向异性分类中对$p$的限制，且在无有限能量、可积性或渐近假设下完成分类。

Abstract: Let $N\ge 2$ and $1<p<N$. Let $H$ be a possibly non-reversible Minkowski norm on $\mathbb{R}^N$, with $H\in C^2(\mathbb{R}^N\setminus\{0\})$ and $D^2(H^2/2)$ uniformly positive definite away from the origin. We classify all nonnegative local weak solutions $u\in W^{1,p}_{\rm loc}(\mathbb{R}^N)$ of \[ -\operatorname{div}\big(H(\nabla u)^{p-1}\nabla H(\nabla u)\big)=u^{p^*-1},\qquad p^*=\frac{Np}{N-p}, \] without any finite-energy, integrability, or asymptotic assumption. Every nonzero solution is an anisotropic Sobolev bubble. Conclusion: 该工作在互补区间 $\sigma>2p$ 上完整建立了临界拟线性 Hartree 方程的尖锐渐近与对称性理论；三种 regime 下解均关于某点径向对称且沿径向变量严格递减，填补了 $\sigma\le2p$ 之外的理论空白。

Abstract: We establish the sharp asymptotic and symmetry theory for the critical quasilinear Hartree equation \[ -Δ_pu=\big(|x|^{-σ}*u^{p^*_σ}\big)u^{p^*_σ-1} \quad\text{in }\mathbb R^N, \qquad p^*_σ=\frac{p(2N-σ)}{2(N-p)}, \] in the complementary range $2p<σ<N$, beyond the quasilinear theory previously available for $σ\le2p$ [Math. Ann., 391(2):2653--2708, 2025]. This range is structurally different: $p^*_σ<p$, so the effective potential obtained by factoring out $u^{p-1}$ becomes singular as $u\to0$. Nevertheless, whenever $p^*_σ>1$, every nonnegative finite-energy weak solution is automatically locally bounded. Our proof provides a quasilinear regularity mechanism adapted to this complementary range, where the potential-theoretic route available for $σ\le2p$ breaks down. For positive finite-energy solutions with $p^*_σ>1$ we moreover uncover a sharp transition governed by the sign of $σ(p-2)+2p$. If this quantity is positive, the classical $p$-harmonic profile persists. At the threshold, \[ u(x)\simeq |x|^{-γ_h}(\log|x|)^{1/(p-p^*_σ)}, \qquad γ_h=\frac{N-p}{p-1}, \] with the matching two-sided gradient rate. Below the threshold, \[ u(x)=A_*|x|^{-γ_*}+o(|x|^{-γ_*}), \qquad γ_*=\frac{2(N-p)(σ-p)}{p(σ-2p)}<γ_h, \] and the rescaled gradients converge uniformly to the corresponding radial profile. This yields a unified construction covering both polynomial and exponential heat kernel decay, general locally Lipschitz nonlinearities, and time-dependent reaction coefficients, without convexity or monotonicity assumptions. Abstract: We study the relation between ground states, criticality, and variational compactness for a family of genuinely multipolar Lp-Hardy weights, 1 < p < n. Removing a geometric interaction term leads to a second variational problem, whose optimal coefficient is controlled by concentration at the poles and at infinity. We prove that a strict gap below the resulting threshold yields strong compactness of normalized minimizing sequences, a positive extremizer, and criticality of the optimally rescaled operator. For 1 < p < 2, this gap holds throughout the full-potential critical set and the optimal truncated coefficient is strictly below 1; for p >2, the coefficient is strictly above 1 in the full-potential attainment region. At fixed parameters strictly inside the quadratic attained region, the optimal value converges to 1 as p tending to 2, with attainment and criticality on both sides. Among others, this model describes the dynamics of dry foams and it also arises as a sharp interface limit of Navier-Stokes/Allen-Cahn systems with non-vanishing mobility. Despite this wide range of applications, global-in-time existence of any kind of weak solutions remains a challenging open problem. In this work, we introduce a novel notion of varifold weak solution in two and three ambient dimensions, based on a sharp energy dissipation principle à la De Giorgi, and we show for the first time the unconditional global-in-time existence of such a weak solution. Abstract: Let $K\subset\mathbb{R}^n$ be a convex body, let $X$ and $Y$ be independent uniform points of $K$, and set $R_K=(|K|/ω_n)^{1/n}$. We consider the distribution function \[
  D_K(ρ)=\mathbb P\{|X-Y|/R_K<ρ\}, \] and its homogeneous counterpart \begin{equation*}
  \mathcal J_ρ(K)=\iint_{K\times K}\mathbf{1}_{\{|x-y|<ρR_K\}}dxdy. \end{equation*} Thus $D_K(ρ)$ is the proportion of ordered pairs of points of $K$ whose distance is less than $ρR_K$, or equivalently the radial distribution function of the normalized covariogram of $K$.

Third, the action-angle variables on \(\mathcal U_N\) are constructed, which establishes the complete integrability of the focusing CMdNLS equation on \(\mathcal U_N\).

Assuming the reduced logarithmic energy bound and vertex logarithmic tightness, we prove that after passing to a subsequence, the normalized maps $v_\varepsilon = U_\varepsilon/ρ_\varepsilon$ converges in $C^k_{\mathrm{loc}}(Ω\setminus A)$ $\forall k\ge0$ away from a finite set of interior vortices $A$. The limiting map is a canonical $S^1$-valued harmonic map $u_*$ containing integer interior vortex factors and fixed fractional corner factors. The renormalized energy is defined by removing discs around the interior vortices and sectors around the vertices and subtracting the corresponding logarithmic divergences. The interior vortex locations of the limiting map are critical points of this renormalized energy.

</details>

Abstract: We study an incompressible visco-morphoelastic system in three dimensions in which the Eulerian effective strain is transported by the Zaremba-Jaumann rate and coupled to a Navier-Stokes momentum balance through a Kelvin-Voigt stress. The strain remains symmetric and splits into a scalar transport-relaxation equation for its trace and a deviatoric equation containing the nonlinear stretching and Jaumann terms. We regularize both equations by diffusion of order $\varepsilon$, which admits a mechanical interpretation as weakly nonlocal remodeling with a characteristic length scale. For fixed $\varepsilon>0$ we construct Leray-Hopf type weak solutions by a truncated Galerkin scheme and remove the truncation a posteriori using a maximum principle for the scalar variable. As $\varepsilon\to0$, the scalar variable converges strongly in $L^p((0,T)\timesΩ)$ for every $1\le p<\infty$, by a DiPerna-Lions commutator argument on bounded Lipschitz domains, allowing the stretching term to be identified in the limit. In contrast, the Jaumann commutator is a product of two weakly convergent sequences, and its limit cannot be identified from the available estimates. We therefore obtain a defect \[\boldsymbol{\mathcal{D}}\in L^2(0,T;(H^2(Ω;\mathbb{S}_0))^\ast)\] in the limiting deviatoric equation. We give two sufficient conditions for $\boldsymbol{\mathcal{D}}=0$. The energy-level cancellation used in related corotational viscoelastic models does not close here because the coefficient of the stretching term is itself an unknown.

Abstract: We establish a global uniqueness result at the critical Sobolev regularity for the isotropic Calderón problem in all dimensions $n\ge 4$. More precisely, for every $n\ge4$ and every bounded Lipschitz domain $Ω\subset\mathbb{R}^n$, any real-valued, uniformly elliptic scalar conductivity $γ\in W^{1,n}(Ω)$ is uniquely determined by its full Dirichlet-to-Neumann map. This settles a long-standing open problem in the field. To prove uniqueness at this endpoint, we develop an averaged trace norm method, which combines complex geometrical optics with trace norm estimates averaged over wave orientations.

Abstract: We establish a class of sharp higher-order inequalities related to the uncertainty principle. In particular, when $N=2$, we give an answer to the higher-order version of Open Problem 9 raised by Maz'ya in [Integr. Equ. Oper. Theory (2018) 90:25]. Second, we establish sharp higher-order Heisenberg uncertainty principles that do not involve higher-order tensors. Abstract: This paper is concerned with establishing estimates on averaged Green's functions for a uniformly elliptic divergence form partial difference equation with random coefficients on the $d$ dimensional integer lattice $\mathbb{Z}^d$. It has previously been shown that the averaged Green's function is point-wise well approximated by the homogenized Green's function at large length scales. Corresponding results also hold for (fractional) derivatives of the averaged Green's function up to but not including the second derivative. Second derivative results have been established when the elliptic equation generates a positive semi-group, as in the diagonal case. Here the second derivative result is shown more generally by using an insight of Bourgain.

</details>

Abstract: We construct solutions to the two-dimensional stationary Prandtl system under a constant adverse pressure gradient exhibiting a family of distinct separation laws. More precisely, for every integer $\ell\ge1$, we construct smooth initial data for which separation occurs at a finite location $x=x^\ast$, with \[ \partial_y u(x,0) =\mathbf C_\ell(x^\ast-x)^{\ell/2}\bigl(1+o(1)\bigr), \qquad x\uparrow x^\ast, \] where $\mathbf C_\ell>0$ depends on the initial data.

This description addresses Open Problem~5 posed by Oleinik and Samokhin~\cite{OleinikSamok-book-99}. We also prove that the square-root separation regime, corresponding to $\ell=1$, is dynamically stable under small admissible perturbations of the initial data in a suitable topology.

Conclusion: 该工作为Guderley内爆激波的非线性稳定性提供了严格数学基础，并展示了"解析估计+计算机辅助验证"相结合处理自相似奇点问题的有效性。

Abstract: We prove nonlinear stability of the Guderley imploding shock under radial perturbations for the three-dimensional compressible Euler equations in the adiabatic range $1<γ\leq\frac{5}{3}$. The perturbed solutions develop an asymptotically self-similar implosion in finite time. Our result allows perturbations with small, nonzero constant pressure in the quiescent region ahead of the shock. The proof combines weighted $L^\infty$ estimates along characteristics with a computer-assisted proof of stability inequalities for the Guderley profile.

Conclusion: 本文提供了处理非齐次退化抛物 $p$-Laplace 方程弱解正则性的新途径，克服了内在尺度化方法中的前向传播估计障碍，并刻画了正等待时间条件在仅有界初值情形下的最优性。

Abstract: For weak solutions to quasilinear degenerate parabolic equations of $p$-Laplace type, a central obstacle in applying the method of intrinsic scaling to prove their Hölder regularity is the derivation of forward-in-time propagation estimate for the spatial measure of level sets. In this paper, we revisit and overcome this difficulty for an inhomogeneous degenerate parabolic $p$-Laplace equation with bounded nonnegative time-independent forcing term and initial data. Our new proof is built upon the temporal Lipschitz estimate that we establish on any time interval separated from the initial time by a positive amount. The strong regularity gain furnished by this estimate allows us to derive the forward propagation of level-set measures directly, thereby bypassing the energy inequality that served as the starting point of the previous approach. Furthermore, we establish space-time $W^{1,\infty}_{x,t}$ estimates away from the initial time and construct counterexamples to demonstrate the sharpness of the positive waiting-time condition under merely bounded initial data.

### [93] [Inverse Energy Cascade and Non-uniqueness for the Advection-Diffusion Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01779)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对流-扩散方程在临界正则性附近解的唯一性/非唯一性问题长期受到关注，本文旨在探究当漂移处于 $L_t^2L_x^\infty$ 以下的对数近临界类时，解的唯一性是否失效，并厘清在能量不等式与能量有界条件下唯一性成立的确切边界。

Method: 构造两类反例：其一是扩散辅助的标量能量逆级联，能量在 $t\to 0$ 时发散；其二是压缩式逆混合，扩散仅起扰动作用，产生初始时刻存在能量跳变的抛物型解。二者在正时间下漂移与标量均光滑。此外考虑 $L^{2,\infty}_tL^\infty_x$ 类漂移及从几乎处处时间出发满足能量不等式的解。

Result: 在 $L_t^2L_x^\infty$ 以下的对数近临界类中构造出非唯一解（两种机制，其一具 $t\to 0$ 能量发散，其二为有界抛物解且初始时刻有能量跳变）；在 $L^{2,\infty}_tL^\infty_x$ 类中，满足能量不等式且能量有界的解具有唯一性，同时构造出能量在 $t\to 0$ 时爆破的非唯一解。

Conclusion: 唯一性在 $L_t^2L_x^\infty$ 以下的对数近临界漂移类中一般不成立，其失效可由逆级联与逆混合两种机制产生；而在 $L^{2,\infty}_tL^\infty_x$ 且能量有界的设定下唯一性恢复，非唯一性仅在能量于初始时刻爆破时出现，由此刻画了唯一性的临界边界。

Abstract: We prove non-uniqueness for the advection-diffusion equation with a drift in a logarithmically near-critical class below $L_t^2L_x^\infty$. The first construction is a diffusion-assisted inverse cascade of scalar energy that diverges as $t \to 0$. The second uses compressed inverse mixing, with diffusion acting perturbatively, and produces a bounded parabolic solution with the energy jump at the initial time. In both constructions the drift and scalar are smooth for positive time. We also consider the drift in $L^{2,\infty}_tL^\infty_x$ and solutions satisfying the energy inequality starting from almost every time. In this setting, we prove uniqueness of solutions with bounded energy, and construct non-unique solution with energy blowing up as time goes to zero.

</details>


### [94] [On a $2x2$ system of conservation laws with discontinuous flux](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01858)
*Felisia Angela Chiarello,Simone Fagioli,Bastien Polizzi,Massimiliano Daniele Rosini*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究带间断通量的一维2x2守恒律系统，对总变差足够小的初值证明弱解整体存在，显式构造Riemann求解器并分析其性质，最后设计Glimm型数值格式复现解析波型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带间断通量的守恒律系统在交通流、多相流等非均匀介质中具有广泛应用背景，但通量间断使得经典守恒律理论（如Lax熵条件、标准Riemann求解器）不再直接适用，因此需要针对该结构建立适定的弱解理论并给出可计算的数值方法。作者希望填补2x2系统中间断通量情形下整体存在性与数值逼近的空白。

Method: 针对一维2x2带间断通量守恒律系统：(1) 显式构造相应的Riemann求解器，给出连接左右状态的波结构；(2) 分析该求解器的主要性质（如波速、熵条件、唯一性等）；(3) 对总变差充分小的初值，利用波分解与Glimm泛函等工具证明弱解的全局存在性；(4) 构造与精确Riemann求解器匹配的Glimm型数值格式。

Result: 证明了在初值总变差足够小时弱解的全局存在性；所构造的Riemann求解器具备预期的良好性质（如波序、熵相容性等）；所提出的Glimm型数值格式能够成功复现解析得到的波型，说明数值结果与理论分析一致。

Conclusion: 该工作为带间断通量的2x2守恒律系统建立了从精确Riemann求解器、弱解整体存在性到Glimm型数值逼近的较完整理论框架，表明间断通量情形下仍可继承经典Glimm方法的有效性，并为相关物理模型的数值模拟提供了可靠基础。

Abstract: We study a one-dimensional $2x2$ system of conservation laws with discontinuous flux. We prove the global existence of weak solutions for initial data with sufficiently small total variation. We construct explicitly the associated Riemann solver and analyze its main properties. Finally, we introduce a Glimm-type numerical scheme tailored to the exact Riemann solver and use it to reproduce the wave patterns obtained analytically.

</details>


### [95] [Compressible steady water waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01883)
*Abba Ramadan,Samuel Walsh*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究二维自由边界等熵Euler方程（含常重力、忽略表面张力）下小振幅与大振幅可压缩水波的存在性，涵盖真空下的周期波、孤立波以及河道中两相问题的锋面型解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Janzen–Rayleigh展开对可压缩水波提供了形式化描述，但缺乏严格的数学基础；本文旨在给出该展开的严格版本，并构造可趋于跨音速流的解曲线。

Method: 采用局部与全局分支（bifurcation）技术，以不可压缩解为起点，将适当定义的马赫数（Mach number）作为分支参数，构造上述各类水波的大族解。

Result: 构造出小振幅与大振幅可压缩水波的解族，给出经典Janzen–Rayleigh展开的严格版本，并获得可极限趋于跨音速流的解曲线。

Conclusion: 该工作为可压缩水波提供了严格的构造性理论，统一处理周期波、孤立波与锋面型解，并揭示了通向跨音速流的解分支结构。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider the existence of small- and large-amplitude compressible water waves. These are solutions of the two-dimensional free boundary isentropic Euler equations subjected to a constant gravitational force and neglecting the effects of surface tension. We study periodic and solitary waves beneath vacuum as well as front-type solutions of the two-phase problem in a channel. Using local and global bifurcation techniques, we construct large families of these waves beginning at an incompressible solution and treating the Mach number (defined in a suitable sense) as the bifurcation parameter. This gives a rigorous version of the classical Janzen--Rayleigh expansion, but also furnishes solution curves that may limit to transsonic flow.

</details>


### [96] [Global regularity and sparsity for regularised optimal transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01928)
*Xiaopeng Cheng,Lukas Koch,Haotian Xiao*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究次二次或熵正则化的正则化最优传输，在边际与支撑域满足 $C^{1,\alpha}$ 假设下，证明势函数具有关于正则化参数一致的全局梯度-Lipschitz 界，并刻画条件支撑在边界附近的球状行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为正则化最优传输提供关于正则化参数一致的正则性估计，并刻画条件支撑的几何尺度，同时以更弱假设改进并独立复现已有的相关结果。

Method: 在边际测度及其支撑域满足 $C^{1,\alpha}$ 正则性假设下，分析次二次或熵正则化最优传输问题，推导势函数的全局梯度-Lipschitz 界并研究条件支撑 $\text{supp}\,\pi_\varepsilon(\cdot|x)$ 的局部几何。

Result: 得到了关于正则化参数一致的势函数全局梯度-Lipschitz 界；证明条件支撑在边界附近（up to the boundary）表现为半径约为 $\varepsilon^{\frac{4}{d(p-1)+2}}$ 的球；在更弱假设下用不同证明得到了已有文献 [9, Theorem 1.2] 的结果。

Conclusion: 该工作独立于并平行于文献 [9] 完成，在更弱假设下给出了一致梯度-Lipschitz 估计与条件支撑的球状尺度刻画，改进了相关已有结论。

Abstract: We study regularised optimal transport with subquadratic or entropic regularisation. Under $C^{1,α}$-assumptions on the marginals and the domains of their support, we prove global gradient-Lipschitz bounds on the potentials, uniform in the regularisation parameter. Moreover, we show that, up to the boundary, the support of the conditional supports $\text{supp}\,π_\varepsilon(\cdot|x)$ behaves like balls of radius $\varepsilon^\frac 4 {d(p-1)+2}$.
  Our work was carried out concurrently and independently of [9]. We obtain the results of [9,Theorem 1.2] under weaker assumptions and with a different proof.

</details>


### [97] [Global existence, uniform boundedness and asymptotic behavior for Gierer--Meinhardt system with critical exponent $qr=(p-1)(s+1)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01968)
*Xianfa Song,Zeen Song*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究具有临界指数的Gierer--Meinhardt系统的全局存在性、一致有界性与渐近行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gierer--Meinhardt系统是刻画生物模式形成的经典反应扩散模型，其临界指数情形在数学上具有特殊困难性，作者希望在该临界条件下刻画解的长时间行为。

Method: 针对临界指数$qr=(p-1)(s+1)$的Gierer--Meinhardt方程组，在Neumann边界条件及连续初值、源项假设下，对任意$d_1,d_2>0$，通过先验估计与分析技巧证明解的性质。

Result: 在给定条件下，对每一对$d_1,d_2>0$，分别得到系统的全局存在性、一致有界性以及渐近行为的结论。

Conclusion: 该工作为临界指数情形的Gierer--Meinhardt系统建立了全局可解性与长期渐近刻画，深化了对该类模型动力学行为的理解。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the following Gierer--Meinhardt system \begin{equation*} \begin{cases} u_t=d_1Δu-a_1u+u^p/v^q+δ_1(x),\quad x\in Ω, t>0,\\ v_t=d_2Δv-a_2v+u^r/v^s+δ_2(x), \quad x\in Ω, t>0,\\ \partial_νu=\partial_νv=0,\qquad\qquad \qquad\qquad\quad x\in \partial Ω, t>0,\\ u(x,0)=u_0(x),\quad v(x,0)=v_0(x),\quad x\in Ω, \end{cases} \end{equation*} where $Ω\subset \mathbb{R}^N$ is a bounded domain, $ν$ is the outer norm vector with respect to the smooth boundary $\partial Ω$, $d_1,d_2, p,q,r>0$, $s>-1$, $p-1<r$, $qr=(p-1)(s+1)$, $δ_1(x)$, $δ_2(x)$, $u_0(x)$ and $v_0(x)$ are continuous functions.
  For every pair $d_1,d_2>0$, we respectively establish the global existence, uniform boundedness and asymptotic behavior for Gierer--Meinhardt system with critical exponent $qr=(p-1)(s+1)$ under certain conditions.

</details>


### [98] [Regularity for the prescribed vertical mean curvature system in the plane](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01970)
*Jonas Hirsch,Connor Mooney,Riccardo Tione*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 证明了平面上预定垂直平均曲率系统 Lipschitz 解的整体正则性，其中余维数任意、平均曲率属于 $L^r\ (r>2)$，加强并推广了作者此前关于 Lawson-Osserman 猜想的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者此前的工作研究了 Lawson-Osserman 猜想相关情形，自然的问题是能否在更一般的设定（任意余维数、更弱的平均曲率可积性）下获得同样的正则性结论，从而深化对预定平均曲率系统解的正则性理论的理解。

Method: 针对平面上预定垂直平均曲率系统的 Lipschitz 解，采用几何测度论与偏微分方程正则性分析方法，在余维数任意、平均曲率仅属于 $L^r\ (r>2)$ 的弱假设下进行估计与证明。

Result: 证明了该系统的 Lipschitz 解处处正则，即在上述一般条件下解无奇异点，从而将原有关于 Lawson-Osserman 猜想的结果加强并推广到任意余维数与更弱的曲率可积性情形。

Conclusion: 该结果确立了平面预定垂直平均曲率系统 Lipschitz 解的处处正则性，推广了既有工作，对平均曲率系统的正则性理论具有重要意义。

Abstract: We prove everywhere regularity for Lipschitz solutions to the prescribed vertical mean curvature system in the plane, where the codimension is arbitrary and the mean curvature is in $L^r, \, r > 2$. This strengthens and extends our previous result on the Lawson-Osserman conjecture, https://arxiv.org/abs/2308.04997.

</details>


### [99] [Sharp Small-Height Estimates for Subcritical Special Lagrangian Equations in Dimension Three](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01986)
*Caiyan Li,Wei Wei*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 证明了三维次临界相位特殊拉格朗日方程连续粘性解的内部Lipschitz估计，并给出尖锐高度阈值 $\sec\Theta$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究三维特殊拉格朗日方程解的正则性，确定Lipschitz估计成立时高度振荡阈值的最优性。

Method: 利用两点极大值原理以及三维特有的代数恒等式。

Result: 若 $\mathrm{osc}_{B_R}u\leq MR^2$，$0\leq M<\sec\Theta$，则 $\mathrm{Lip}_{B_{\rho R}}u\leq CR$；阈值最优：$M=\sec\Theta$ 时存在整解其Lipschitz半范数在每个固定内部球上无界。

Conclusion: 建立了带尖锐高度阈值的三维次临界相位特殊拉格朗日方程内部Lipschitz估计，且阈值不可改进。

Abstract: We prove an interior Lipschitz estimate with a sharp height threshold for continuous viscosity solutions of the three-dimensional special Lagrangian equation with subcritical phase $|Θ|<π/2$. If \[
  osc_{B_R}u\leq MR^2,\qquad 0\leq M<\secΘ, \] then $Lip_{B_{ρR}}u\leq CR$ for some $ρ>0$ and $C>0$ depending only on $|Θ|$ and $M$. The threshold is optimal: at $M=\secΘ$, there are entire real-analytic solutions whose Lipschitz seminorms are unbounded on every fixed interior ball. The proof uses a two-point maximum principle and algebraic identities specific to dimension three.

</details>


### [100] [Global Hölder estimates for linearized Monge-Ampère equations in divergence form with applications to dual semigeostrophic equations and periodic homogenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02071)
*Guoqing Cui,Chong Gu,Nam Q. Le,Ling Wang,Bin Zhou*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文在 $n\geq 3$ 维中为散度型线性化 Monge-Ampère 方程建立了内部与全局 Hölder 估计，并应用于三维半地转系统与 Monge-Ampère 方程的周期均匀化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性化 Monge-Ampère 方程的解的正则性（尤其是 Hölder 估计）在一般维数中尚不完善，需要建立仅依赖 Hessian 行列式上下界与右端向量场有界性的估计。

Method: 在 Monge-Ampère 势的 Hessian 行列式一致有界且右端向量场有界的假设下，利用解的 $L^p$ 范数（任意 $p>1$）推导内部与全局 Hölder 估计；核心技术是对线性化算子 Green 函数梯度在小截面上的 $L^1$ 估计，并给出关于极点的均匀衰减率。

Result: 获得了任意 $p>1$ 下基于 $L^p$ 范数的 Hölder 估计；给出了 Green 函数梯度的均匀 $L^1$ 衰减估计；并据此得到三维周期对偶半地转系统中原始与对偶势时间导数的均匀 Hölder 估计，以及 Monge-Ampère 方程周期均匀化的线性收敛速率。

Conclusion: 该工作将线性化 Monge-Ampère 方程的 Hölder 正则性推广至所有维数，并为相关动力学与均匀化问题提供了定量收敛结果。

Abstract: We establish interior and global Hölder estimates for linearized Monge-Ampère equations in divergence form in all dimensions $n\geq 3$, when the Hessian determinant of the convex Monge-Ampère potential is bounded above and below by positive constants and the vector field on the right-hand side is bounded. The estimates use the $L^p$ norm of the solution for any $p>1$. A key ingredient is an $L^1$ estimate, with appropriate decay rates, for the gradient of the Green's function of the linearized Monge-Ampère operator in small sections, uniform with respect to the pole. As applications, we obtain uniform Hölder estimates for the time derivatives of the primal and dual potentials in the three-dimensional periodic dual semigeostrophic system when the initial density is bounded away from zero and infinity, and establish a linear convergence rate for periodic homogenization of the Monge-Ampère equation.

</details>


### [101] [Geometry and Convergence of Quadratically Regularized Optimal Transport II](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02077)
*Alberto González-Sanz,Marcel Nutz*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究二次成本下的二次正则化最优传输在小正则化参数$\varepsilon$时的稀疏结构，证明最优耦合支集包含Brenier映射图像，并在Brenier图像中心化与$\ell=\varepsilon^{1/(d+2)}$重标度后给出支集截面的椭圆/半空间极限、对偶势的均匀展开，以及对Brenier映射两种近似的精确$L^p$速率与偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典最优传输（Brenier映射）在计算上常因耦合支集为低维结构而难以直接处理；二次正则化（熵型之外的另一类正则）可产生稀疏耦合，但其在$\varepsilon\to0$时的局部几何与近似精度尚缺乏细致刻画。本文旨在量化小正则化下二次正则化最优传输如何逼近经典最优传输。

Method: 针对$\mathbb{R}^d$中光滑边缘密度与二次成本，在$\varepsilon\to0$的小正则化极限下，通过以Brenier图像为中心并按$\ell=\varepsilon^{1/(d+2)}$重标度，分析最优耦合支集的截面极限；利用对偶势的渐近展开与线性Neumann问题确定展开系数。

Result: 支集包含Brenier映射图像；重标度后支集截面在内部收敛到显式椭球、在边界收敛到半空间轮廓；对偶势可在$\ell^2$阶均匀展开（含边界），主系数由共同局部剖面与由线性Neumann问题决定的相反全局修正组成；对Brenier映射的两种近似（对偶势梯度与耦合条件均值）得到精确$L^p$速率$\ell^{1+1/p}$及精确主常数，并识别出内部与边界的主导偏差。

Conclusion: 结果表明二次正则化能对经典最优传输产生准确的稀疏近似，精确刻画了其在小正则化极限下的局部几何、对偶势展开与Brenier映射近似的收敛速率和偏差结构。

Abstract: We study quadratically regularized optimal transport with quadratic cost in the regime of small regularization $\varepsilon$, where the support of the optimal coupling is sparse. For smooth marginal densities in $\mathbb{R}^d$, we show that the support contains the graph of the Brenier map. After centering by the Brenier image and rescaling by $\ell=\varepsilon^{1/(d+2)}$, the sections of the support have explicit ellipsoidal limits for $\varepsilon\to0$, except at boundary points, where the limits are half-space profiles. The optimal dual potentials admit expansions at order $\ell^2$, uniformly up to the boundary. We identify the leading coefficients, which consist of a common local profile and opposite global corrections determined by a linear Neumann problem. Finally, we analyze two approximations to the Brenier map, namely the gradient of the dual potential and the conditional mean of the coupling. We obtain the sharp $L^p$ rate $\ell^{1+1/p}$ with exact leading constants and further identify the leading interior and boundary biases. Taken together, our results illustrate that quadratic regularization induces an accurate sparse approximation of classical optimal transport.

</details>


### [102] [Resonance expansions and local-energy decay estimates for Dirac operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02108)
*Zhuo Chen,Michael Melgaard*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究三维半经典Dirac算子的共振展开与局域长时动力学，建立了指数型截断预解估计、共振展开公式及局部能量衰减的定量阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半经典Dirac算子的共振结构与长时局域能量衰减缺乏系统的定量刻画，尤其是共振展开的时域表现和衰减阈值的几何机制尚未明确。

Method: 对光滑Hermite矩阵值扰动（紧集外解析），证明远离共振集的指数截断预解界与共振自由薄矩形内的多项式界；结合局部O(h^{-3})共振计数上界、鸽巢选取可容许围道、几乎解析泛函演算与Cauchy--Green形变导出谱局域传播子的共振展开。对质量型扰动βV(x)在非捕获能量窗内，利用对数共振自由区与定量FBI变换/逃逸函数论证得到连续截断预解的多项式界与快速局域能量衰减，并用无畸变区的有限时间传播论证精化估计。

Result: 正能共振对大正时贡献、负能共振对大负时贡献；引入最大正叶连接时间T_χ后，证明几何型连续预解估计，并对每个低于共振自由深度常数的固定M得到显式局域能量衰减阈值T_N > T_χ + (N+1)/M；同时给出对完整畸变预解的一致[h log(1/h)]^{-1}界的严格微局域障碍，并识别出对数深度处夹层连续预解的有限飞行时间放大机制。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了三维半经典Dirac算子共振展开与局域长时衰减的统一框架，揭示了能量符号决定时域方向、几何连接时间决定衰减阈值的机制，并量化了对数深度预解估计的极限。

Abstract: We study resonance expansions and localised long-time dynamics for three-dimensional semiclassical Dirac operators. For smooth Hermitian matrix-valued perturbations which are analytic outside a compact set, we first obtain exponential cut-off resolvent bounds away from the resonance set and polynomial bounds in thin resonance-free rectangles adjacent to the real axis. Combining these estimates with a local $\Oc(h^{-3})$ upper bound on the number of resonances, a pigeonhole selection of admissible contours, almost-analytic functional calculus, and a Cauchy--Green deformation, we derive resonance expansions for spectrally localised propagators. Positive-energy resonances contribute for large positive times, whereas negative-energy resonances contribute for large negative times. We then specialise to mass-type perturbations $\mbfβV(x)$ in a nontrapping energy window. Using the logarithmic resonance-free region established in our earlier work together with a quantitative FBI-transform/escape-function argument, we obtain a polynomial bound for the continued cut-off resolvent and deduce rapid local-energy decay. We subsequently refine this polynomial estimate by a finite-time propagation argument in the undistorted region. Introducing the maximal positive-sheet connection time $T_χ$ associated with the spatial cutoff, we prove a geometric continued-resolvent estimate and, for every fixed $M$ below the resonance-free depth constant, derive the explicit local-energy decay threshold $T_N>T_χ+(N+1)/M$. The same analysis gives a rigorous microlocal obstruction to a uniform $[h\log(1/h)]^{-1}$ bound for the full distorted resolvent and identifies a finite-flight-time amplification mechanism for the sandwiched continued resolvent at logarithmic depth.

</details>


### [103] [On Two Species Long Range Segregation in an Annular Domain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02141)
*Howen Chuah*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究环形区域上两族群长程分离椭圆系统的自由边界，证明自由边界由同心球面组成且唯一确定，并推导其半径随参数变化的ODE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该椭圆方程组建模种群的长程分离现象，此前工作仅在二维研究了自由边界正则性、在高维研究了部分正则性，但缺乏对特殊情形（K=2、环形区域、任意维）自由边界几何结构与定量性质的刻画。

Method: 利用解的唯一性与旋转不变性分析自由边界的几何结构；应用\cite{CL2}中导出的自由边界条件证明自由边界唯一确定；研究域、相互作用距离与边界数据对半径的影响；对随参数t变化的单参数族系统，分别在二维和n≥3维推导半径的常微分方程，并给出实例讨论。

Result: 证明自由边界由同心球面组成且被唯一确定；给出半径随域、相互作用距离和边界数据的变化规律，特别地自由边界在相互作用距离趋于零时收敛于相邻模型的自由边界；分别得到二维与n≥3维下自由边界半径的ODE。

Conclusion: 在环形域、K=2的特定情形下，长程分离模型的自由边界具有完全对称的同心球面结构并可唯一确定，其半径可由ODE刻画，且在相互作用距离趋于零时与相邻模型一致。

Abstract: We consider a system of elliptic equations, depending on a small parameter $ε> 0$, which models the long range segregation of populations. The system has been previously studied in \cite{CL2} for the regularity of the free boundary in dimension $2$ and in \cite{ChPaTo26_2} and \cite{ChPaTo26_3} for the partial regularity of the free boundary in higher dimensions. In this paper, we consider the special case with $K = 2$ populations in an annular domain in arbitrary dimensions. Using the uniqueness and the rotational invariance of the solution, we show that the free boundary consist of concentric spheres. Moreover, by an application of the free boundary condition derived in \cite{CL2}, we show that the free boundary is uniquely determined. We also examine how the radius of the free boundary change according to the domain, the interaction distance and the boundary data. In particular, we show that the free boundary converges to that of the adjacent model as the interaction distance tends to zero.
  We also study the one parameter family of the elliptic system, in which the domain, the interaction distance, and the boundary data depends on $t$. We derive an ODE for the radius of the free boundary in dimension $2$ and dimension $n \geq 3$ separately. Several examples are given and discussed.

</details>


### [104] [A Global Wavefront Set Condition for Defining Bony's Paraproduct Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02152)
*Josh Mott,Tim Van Hoose*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文在Coriasco–Johansson–Toft的整体波前集框架下，给出了Besov分布乘法中Hörmander条件的自然推广，并将该微局部分析视角与Bony仿微分演算中的经典抛物积（paraproduct）分解联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布（尤其是Besov分布）的乘法在分析中缺乏良好定义，需要合适的微局部条件来刻画乘积何时存在。已有的Hörmander条件针对经典波前集给出乘法准则，而整体波前集框架能更好地处理非紧或全局设定，因此需要将其推广到该框架并对接已有的仿微分技术。

Method: 采用Coriasco–Johansson–Toft的整体波前集（global wavefront set）框架，建立适用于Besov分布乘法的Hörmander型条件的自然推广；随后通过微局部分析方法，将这一条件与Bony仿微分演算中逐点乘法所对应的经典抛物积分解进行对照与关联。

Result: 得到了一类推广的Hörmander乘法条件，可用于刻划Besov分布乘积的存在性；并揭示该微局部乘法判据与经典抛物积分解在结构上的一致性对应关系。

Conclusion: 整体波前集框架下的乘法条件为分布乘法提供了统一视角，并与经典仿微分演算中的抛物积分解相兼容，从而连接了微局部分析与仿微分两套方法。

Abstract: We establish a natural generalization of the Hörmander condition for the multiplication of Besov distributions using the global wavefront set framework of Coriasco, Johansson, and Toft. Using this condition, we relate the microlocal perspective on multiplication of distributions to the classical paraproduct decomposition of pointwise multiplication arising in the paradifferential calculus of Bony.

</details>


<div id='math.FA'></div>

# math.FA [[Back]](#toc)

### [105] [Hyponormality of Generalized Cesàro Matrices of Every Positive Integer Order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00193)
*Yicen Ma*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文给出广义Cesàro矩阵在ℓ²(ℕ₀)上亚正规的充要条件为α≥0的计算机辅助证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究广义Cesàro矩阵（元素为m(n−j+1)_{m−1}/(n+α+1)_m，j≤n）的亚正规性，判定参数α>-1下该算子何时为亚正规算子。

Method: 核心代数工具是将辅助缺陷算子显式分解为正值对角算子与至多m个秩一算子的和；用离散Gram多项式确定各项符号与系数。对非负参数，用加权估计将正性归结为标量不等式；m≥32768时用一致解析估计，其余阶数与整个参数区间用有限有理证书覆盖，不做参数采样也不截断无穷算子。

Result: 证明当且仅当α≥0时该算子亚正规；给出自交换子的统一加权下界；当−1<α<0时，对每个阶数都能找到使二次型为负的有限支撑向量。

Conclusion: 广义Cesàro矩阵亚正规性完全由α符号决定：α≥0亚正规，−1<α<0非亚正规，且证明覆盖全部参数与阶数，无需采样或截断。

Abstract: For a positive integer $m$ and a real parameter $α>-1$, consider the generalized Cesàro matrix on $\ell^2(\mathbb N_0)$ with entries $m(n-j+1)_{m-1}/(n+α+1)_m$ for $j\le n$.
