We give a computer-assisted proof that this operator is hyponormal if and only if $α\ge0$. The main algebraic ingredient is an explicit decomposition of an auxiliary defect operator into a positive diagonal operator and at most $m$ rank-one operators. Discrete Gram polynomials determine the signs and coefficients of these terms. For nonnegative parameters, weighted estimates reduce positivity to scalar inequalities. A uniform analytic estimate covers every $m\ge32768$, while finite rational certificates cover the remaining orders and entire parameter intervals, without parameter sampling or truncating an infinite operator. The proof also gives a uniform weighted lower bound for the self-commutator. For $-1<α<0$, finite-support vectors give negative quadratic forms for every order.

</details>


### [106] [The Hilbert matrix operator into the Bergman space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00252)
*David Norrbo*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 证明了Hilbert矩阵算子 $H:A^2_{\ln^\alpha}\to A^2$ 在 $\alpha\geq 2$ 时有界，在 $\alpha<2$ 时无界，解决了 Karapetrović 与 Jevtić 提出的公开问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决 B. Karapetrović 和 M. Jevtić 提出的关于 Hilbert 矩阵算子从对数加权 Bergman 空间 $A^2_{\ln^\alpha}$ 到 $A^2$ 的有界性公开问题。

Method: 将对数加权 Bergman 空间的情形转化为更一般的加权序列空间命题：证明 Hilbert 矩阵 $H:\ell_v^p\to\ell_w^p$ 有界，其中权 $v(n)=(\ln n)^p/n$、$w(n)=1/n$；$\alpha=2$ 的情形作为该一般结论的推论。

Result: 当 $\alpha\geq 2$ 时 $H:A^2_{\ln^\alpha}\to A^2$ 有界；当 $\alpha<2$ 时 $H$ 不将 $A^2_{\ln^\alpha}$ 映入 $A^2$（即无界），从而给出 $2$ 为其临界指数。

Conclusion: 该工作完整刻画了 Hilbert 矩阵算子在对数加权 Bergman 空间上的有界性阈值，解决了原有公开问题。

Abstract: We prove that the Hilbert matrix operator $H:A^2_{\ln^α} \to A^2$ is bounded when $α\geq 2$ and that $H$ does not map $A^2_{\ln^α} $ into $A^2$ when $α<2$, solving an open problem posed by B. Karapetrović and M. Jevtić. The boundedness when $α=2$ is shown as a corollary to the more general statement: the Hilbert matrix $H:\ell_v^p \to \ell_w^p$ is bounded, where $v(n) = (\ln n)^p / n$ and $w(n) = 1/n$.

</details>


### [107] [Self-improvement for absolutely Cesáro bounded operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00271)
*Loris Arnold*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文研究了p-绝对Cesàro有界算子的q-绝对Cesàro有界性传递问题,给出了最优的指标范围,并在type s空间、L^p空间上推广了相关结论,同时构造了反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究p-绝对Cesàro有界与q-绝对Cesàro有界之间的蕴含关系,确定最优指标范围,并将正算子的结论推广到更一般的算子类,同时探讨谱与增长估计。

Method: 通过算子理论中的插值与type空间技术,证明p-绝对Cesàro有界蕴含q-绝对Cesàro有界的指标范围;在type s∈(1,2]的Banach空间上给出q<s的对数估计;利用L^p空间结构将正算子结论推广到最终正算子;并构造ℓ^1上的反例算子。

Result: 1) 常数K>1的p-绝对Cesàro有界算子对1≤q<pK/(K-1)是q-绝对Cesàro有界的,且该范围最优(即使对正加权移位)。2) type s∈(1,2]空间中绝对Cesàro有界算子对1≤q<s是q-绝对Cesàro有界的,q=s时有对数估计,并得‖T^n‖=O(n^{1/s-ε})。3) L^p(1<p<∞)上将绝对Cesàro有界到p-绝对Cesàro有界的蕴含从正算子推广到最终正算子。4) 对每个1≤p<∞构造ℓ^1上p-绝对Cesàro有界算子T,σ(T)⊆D∪{1}但limsup‖T^n(I-T)‖=∞,且该构造给出对所有p均p-绝对Cesàro有界的单一算子。

Conclusion: p-绝对Cesàro有界性的指标传递范围被精确刻画为pK/(K-1)且最优;type s空间上给出q<s的完整刻画与多项式增长界;L^p空间上蕴含关系推广至最终正算子;ℓ^1反例表明谱条件不蕴含‖T^n(I-T)‖有界。

Abstract: We prove that every $p$-absolutely Cesàro bounded operator with constant $K>1$ is $q$-absolutely Cesàro bounded for $1\leq q<pK/(K-1)$. This range is optimal, even for positive weighted shifts. On Banach spaces of type $s\in (1,2]$, we prove that every absolutely Cesàro bounded operator is $q$-absolutely Cesàro bounded for every $1\leq q<s$, with a logarithmic estimate at $q=s$. We also prove the polynomial growth bound $\| T^n \| =O(n^{1/s-\varepsilon})$ for some $\varepsilon>0$. On $L^p$-spaces, $1<p<\infty$, we extend the implication from absolute Cesàro boundedness to $p$-absolute Cesàro boundedness from positive to individually eventually positive operators. Finally, for every $1\leq p<\infty$, we construct a $p$-absolutely Cesàro bounded operator $T$ on $\ell^1$ such that $σ(T)\subseteq\mathbb D\cup\{1\}$ but $\limsup_{n\to\infty}\|T^n(I-T)\|=\infty$. We also observe that the construction yields a single operator which is $p$-absolutely Cesàro bounded for every $1\leq p<\infty$.

</details>


### [108] [Dilation Theory for Scaled \(q\)-Numerical Ranges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00443)
*Matias Kaunisto,Ryan O'Loughlin,Jani Virtanen*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文将Berger扩张定理从数值域推广到缩放q-数值域，证明若 W_q(A)/q 含于闭单位圆盘，则 A 存在酉 ρ_q-扩张。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Berger扩张定理刻画了数值域 W(A) 含于闭单位圆盘时算子的酉扩张及其2-谱集性质。作者希望将这一结论推广至更一般的缩放q-数值域，从而建立相应的扩张与谱集刻画。

Method: 通过研究缩放q-数值域 W_q(A)/q，定义扩张常数 ρ_q = max{1, 2|q|/(1+√(1-|q|²))}，并证明当该集合含于闭单位圆盘时算子存在酉 ρ_q-扩张，进而利用扩张结果推导谱集性质。

Result: 证明了 W_q(A)/q 含于闭单位圆盘蕴含 A 的酉 ρ_q-扩张；作为推论，任何包含 Ω_q(A) 的圆盘都是 A 的完全 ρ_q-谱集。当 q=1 时 ρ_q=2，退化为经典的"任何包含 W(A) 的圆盘都是 A 的完全2-谱集"。

Conclusion: 本文成功将Berger扩张定理及其谱集推论统一推广到缩放q-数值域框架，经典结果作为 q=1 的特例被重新得到。

Abstract: We generalise Berger's dilation theorem from numerical ranges to scaled $q$-numerical ranges. Specifically, we prove that if the scaled $q$-numerical range $\frac{W_q(A)}{q}$ is contained in the closed unit disk, then $A$ admits a unitary $ρ_q$-dilation, where \[ ρ_q=\max\left\{1,\frac{2|q|}{1+\sqrt{1-|q|^2}}\right\}. \] As a corollary we show that every disk containing $Ω_q(A)$ is a complete $ρ_q$-spectral set for $A$. At $q=1$, this recovers the classical fact that any disk containing $W(A)$ is a complete $2$-spectral set for $A$.

</details>


### [109] [The Separable Quotient Problem and the Lotz Property in Banach Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00584)
*Gustavo Araújo,Anderson Barbosa,Juan B. Seoane-Sepúlveda*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文在建立多个可分商问题（SQP）新的充分或等价条件的同时，证明每个不具备Lotz性质的无穷维Banach空间都存在可分商，从而揭示了SQP与半群理论之间的意外联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可分商问题（SQP）是Banach空间理论中的经典公开问题，已有条件尚不充分，作者希望从新的角度寻找判定条件，并探索它与其它数学分支（如半群理论）的潜在联系。

Method: 通过建立若干新的充分条件或等价条件来刻画可分商的存在性；并借助半群理论（Lotz性质）这一非经典工具，分析不具备Lotz性质的无穷维Banach空间类。

Result: 证明了每个不具备Lotz性质的无穷维Banach空间都拥有可分商，即该类空间构成SQP的一个新充分类。

Conclusion: SQP与半群理论之间存在此前未被察觉的紧密联系，为研究可分商问题提供了新的框架和工具，也拓展了已知的可分商空间类。

Abstract: In this paper, in addition to establishing several new sufficient or equivalent conditions for the well-known Separable Quotient Problem (SQP), we show that every infinite-dimensional Banach space lacking the Lotz property admits a separable quotient, thereby revealing a surprising connection between the SQP and semigroup theory.

</details>


### [110] [Riesz transform characterization of Hardy spaces associated with Dunkl--Schrödinger operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00703)
*Agnieszka Hejna-Łyżwa*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文研究Dunkl Laplacian关联的Schrödinger算子L的Hardy空间H_L^1，利用L的Riesz变换给出其范数等价刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的Hardy空间可由Riesz变换刻画，但Dunkl框架下带势的Schrödinger算子L的Hardy空间H_L^1的Riesz变换刻画尚待建立，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 结合H_L^1的原子分解、局部Hardy空间理论，以及对热核Dunkl导数的加权积分估计；在势的局部积分条件与半群的衰减条件下（相对立方体的容许覆盖表述）进行证明。

Result: 证明了范数等价：||f||_{H_L^1} ≍ ||f||_{L^1(dw)} + Σ_{j=1}^N ||R̃_j f||_{L^1(dw)}。结果适用于任意根系和非负重数函数。

Conclusion: 建立了Dunkl Schrödinger算子Hardy空间的Riesz变换刻画，推广了经典结果并覆盖一般根系与重数函数。

Abstract: Let $L=-Δ_k+V$ be a Schrödinger operator associated with the Dunkl Laplacian and a nonnegative potential. We characterize the Hardy space $H_L^1$, defined by the maximal operator of the semigroup generated by $-L$, in terms of the Riesz transforms associated with $L$. Under a local integral condition on the potential and a decay condition on the semigroup, formulated relative to an admissible covering by cubes, we prove that \[
  \|f\|_{H_L^1}
  \asymp
  \|f\|_{L^1(dw)}
  +\sum_{j=1}^N
  \|\widetilde{R}_j f\|_{L^1(dw)}. \] The proof combines an atomic characterization of $H_L^1$ with local Hardy space theory and weighted integral estimates for Dunkl derivatives of the heat kernels. The result applies to arbitrary root systems and nonnegative multiplicity functions.

</details>


### [111] [On the Shalit-Shamovich Spectral Radius](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00816)
*Maximilian Tornes*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 谱半径在算子空间结构上是完全等距不变量：若两个算子空间结构在任意算子d元组上的谱半径相同，则恒等映射完全等距；最小谱半径有类似结论，并应用于有界非交换函数代数的完全有界同构问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Shalit与Shamovich引入的谱半径是算子空间上的重要量，但该量能否唯一决定底层算子空间结构此前未知。Scherer、Shalit与Shamovich提出了这一问题，本文旨在回答它。

Method: 设E_1、E_2为C^d上的两个算子空间结构，假设其对应谱半径ρ_{E_1}与ρ_{E_2}在每个d元算子组上取值一致，通过分析谱半径对算子空间矩阵范数的约束，证明恒等映射为完全等距。随后将论证平行推广至最小谱半径，并用于算子空间单位球上有界非交换函数代数。

Result: 证明了主要定理：若两个算子空间结构在所有d元算子组上谱半径相同，则恒等映射完全等距。得到最小谱半径的类似结果，并展示其在有界非交换函数代数完全有界同构问题中的应用。

Conclusion: 谱半径（及最小谱半径）唯一决定C^d上的算子空间结构，从而正面回答了Scherer–Shalit–Shamovich的问题，并为相关非交换函数代数的完全有界同构分类提供了工具。

Abstract: We show that the spectral radius introduced by Shalit and Shamovich uniquely determines the underlying operator space structure: If E_1 and E_2 are operator space structures on C^d such that the corresponding spectral radii ρ_{E_1} and ρ_{E_2} agree on every d-tuple of operators, then the identity map is completely isometric. This answers a question of Scherer, Shalit and Shamovich. Moreover, we obtain an analogous result for the minimal spectral radii, and showcase an application to the completely bounded isomorphism problem for algebras of bounded non-commutative functions on operator space unit balls.

</details>


### [112] [Extreme points of the unit ball and isometries of noncommutative quasi-Banach Marcinkiewicz spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01043)
*Kai Fang,Yi Gao,Jinghao Huang,Fedor Sukochev*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 该文刻画了Marcinkiewicz空间（正）单位球的端点，并据此给出非交换Marcinkiewicz空间上正等距的初等形式，以及若干相关空间上满等距与等距群的分类结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有关于L_p空间、Orlicz空间等上等距结构的研究，但Marcinkiewicz空间（含非交换情形）的单位球端点与等距分类尚不系统；本文旨在填补这一空白并推广到弱L_p空间、Marcinkiewicz序列空间、弱ℓ_p算子理想等。

Method: 首先用自然拟范数刻画Marcinkiewicz空间（正）单位球的端点；以此为基础分析正等距的“初等形式”；再分别研究非交换Marcinkiewicz空间、具等迹原子的原子von Neumann代数上的线性映射、弱L_p空间、Marcinkiewicz序列空间与弱ℓ_p算子理想上的满等距及单参数等距群。

Result: 非交换Marcinkiewicz空间上每个正等距均为初等形式；在原子等迹的原子von Neumann代数上，线性映射为满正等距当且仅当它是保持奇异值函数的Jordan *-同构的限制；并给出弱L_p空间（1<p<∞、σ-有限非原子von Neumann代数）、Marcinkiewicz序列空间、弱ℓ_p算子理想（p>0）的满等距分类及后两者的单参数等距群结构。

Conclusion: 端点刻画为等距结构分析提供了有效工具，所得结论统一并推广了非交换对称空间上等距的已知形式，完整刻画了相关Marcinkiewicz型与弱型空间的满等距及其等距群。

Abstract: We characterize extreme points of the (positive) unit ball of Marcinkiewicz spaces with respect to their natural quasi-norms. Having these results at hand, we show that every positive isometry on a noncommutative Marcinkiewicz space is of elementary form. In particular, a linear mapping on a noncommutative Marcinkiewicz space over an atomic von Neumann algebra with all atoms having the same trace is a positive surjective isometry if and only if it is the restriction of a Jordan $*$-isomorphism which preserves the singular value functions. We also study (not necessarily positive) surjective isometries on weak $L_p$-spaces affiliated a $σ$-finite non-atomic von Neumann algebra for $1<p<\infty$, Marcinkiewicz sequence spaces, and weak $\ell_p$ operator ideals, $p>0$, as well as one-parameter groups of isometries on the latter two.

</details>


### [113] [Sparse Kahane--Salem--Zygmund Forms and Weighted Hardy--Littlewood Inequalities Across the Critical Endpoint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01500)
*Anderson Barbosa,Daniel Núñez-Alarcón,Anselmo Raposo,Joedson Santos*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文研究稀疏Kahane--Salem--Zygmund构造与齐次多项式的加权Hardy--Littlewood不等式。对于基数$n^{d+o(1)}$的支撑集，确定了控制$\ell_{p_1}^n\times\cdots\times\ell_{p_m}^n$上单位模$m$-线性型最小范数的$n$的精确幂次；对角情形下给出了$2\le p\le m$、$2\le r\le\infty$时系数范数与上确界范数问题中缺失的多项式增长指数。随后引入对角加权Hardy--Littlewood泛函，在$s=p\ge m$时与经典Hardy--Littlewood系数范数及其最优常数完全一致。确定了$2\le p\le m$、$1\le q\le2$时的最优对角权重指数、临界线$p=m$上以及区域$q>2$的锐利部分；在$q=\infty$时对所有$2\le p\le m$得到最优权重指数。稀疏系数估计提供了匹配的维数障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究稀疏Kahane--Salem--Zygmund构造与加权Hardy--Littlewood不等式，旨在确定单位模多重线性型最小范数的精确幂次，并填补系数范数与上确界范数问题中多项式增长指数的空缺，同时建立加权Hardy--Littlewood泛函与经典范数的最优常数一致性。

Method: 采用稀疏Kahane--Salem--Zygmund构造方法，针对基数$n^{d+o(1)}$的支撑集分析单位模$m$-线性型的最小范数；引入对角加权Hardy--Littlewood泛函，并在$s=p\ge m$时与经典Hardy--Littlewood系数范数进行最优常数对比；通过对参数区域$2\le p\le m$、$1\le q\le2$、临界线$p=m$及$q>2$的锐利部分进行分类讨论确定最优权重指数。

Result: 对于基数$n^{d+o(1)}$的支撑集，确定了控制$\ell_{p_1}^n\times\cdots\times\ell_{p_m}^n$上单位模$m$-线性型最小范数的$n$的精确幂次；对角情形下得到$2\le p\le m$、$2\le r\le\infty$时系数范数与上确界范数问题中缺失的多项式增长指数。对角加权Hardy--Littlewood泛函在$s=p\ge m$时与经典Hardy--Littlewood系数范数及最优常数完全一致。确定了$2\le p\le m$、$1\le q\le2$时的最优对角权重指数、临界线$p=m$上以及区域$q>2$锐利部分的最优权重指数；在$q=\infty$时对所有$2\le p\le m$得到最优权重指数。稀疏系数估计提供了匹配的维数障碍。

Conclusion: 稀疏Kahane--Salem--Zygmund构造给出了单位模多重线性型最小范数的精确幂次，填补了系数范数与上确界范数问题中对角情形的多项式增长指数空白；所引入的对角加权Hardy--Littlewood泛函在特定参数下与经典范数及最优常数一致，并在多个参数区域确定了最优权重指数，稀疏系数估计为相应结果提供了匹配的维数障碍。

Abstract: We study sparse Kahane--Salem--Zygmund constructions and weighted Hardy--Littlewood inequalities for homogeneous polynomials. For supports of cardinality $n^{d+o(1)}$, we determine the sharp power of $n$ governing the smallest norm of a unimodular $m$-linear form on $\ell_{p_1}^n\times\cdots\times\ell_{p_m}^n$; in the diagonal case, this yields the missing polynomial growth exponent in the coefficient-versus-supremum norm problem for $2\le p\le m$ and $2\le r\le\infty$. We then introduce a diagonal weighted Hardy--Littlewood functional which, on $s=p\ge m$, agrees exactly with the classical Hardy--Littlewood coefficient norm with the same optimal constant. We determine the optimal diagonal weight exponent for $2\le p\le m$ and $1\le q\le2$, on the full critical line $p=m$, and on a sharp part of the region $q>2$; at $q=\infty$ the optimal weight exponent is obtained for every $2\le p\le m$. The sparse coefficient estimates provide the matching dimensional obstructions.

</details>


### [114] [Diagonal operators on Janson-Sobolev and Janson-Sobolev-Hardy spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01628)
*Krystian Kazaniecki,Richard Lechner*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 该文研究Janson-Sobolev与Janson-Sobolev-Hardy空间的Banach空间及算子分解结构，证明由对角算子D决定的一类算子S可几乎投影式地以常数1⁺经D分解，并给出对角算子分解的推论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献对鞅空间的Banach空间结构与算子分解理论已有研究，但对由q-adic滤流、子空间V与重排不变函数空间X共同刻画的Janson-Sobolev型鞅空间的算子分解性质尚未充分刻画，作者希望建立这一类新鞅空间的算子分解理论。

Method: 引入并研究由q-adic滤流、子空间V⊂R₀^{l×q}以及重排不变函数空间X确定的新一类鞅空间（Janson-Sobolev与Janson-Sobolev-Hardy空间）；利用线性泛函λ_U^{k_t}(D)与典范投影Q_{K_t}^B构造算子S=Σλ_U^{k_t}(D)Q_{K_t}^B，并研究其经有界对角算子D的几乎投影式分解，分解常数控制在1⁺。

Result: 主要结果证明：对每个有界对角算子D，由线性泛函与典范投影决定的算子S几乎投影式地经D分解，分解常数为1⁺。作为推论，在典范投影的自然有界性条件下，以及对所有赋予L¹范数的空间，均得到对角算子的分解结果。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了Janson-Sobolev与Janson-Sobolev-Hardy鞅空间的算子分解框架，表明对角算子的分解性质在自然有界性条件下及L¹范数空间情形下均成立，为这类新鞅空间的Banach空间几何研究提供了工具。

Abstract: We study the Banach space and operator factorization structure of Janson-Sobolev and Janson-Sobolev-Hardy spaces. This new class of martingale spaces is determined by a $q$-adic filtration, a subspace $V\subset \mathbb{R}_0^{l\times q}$, and a rearrangement invariant function space $X$. Our main result shows that, for every bounded diagonal operator $D$, the operator $S = \sum_{t=1}^s λ_{\mathcal U}^{k_t}(D)Q_{\mathcal K_t}^{\mathcal B}$ determined by the linear functionals $λ_{\mathcal U}^{k_t}(D)$ and the canonical projections $Q_{\mathcal K_t}^{\mathcal B}$, almost projectionally factors through $D$ with constant $1^+$. As consequences, we obtain factorization results for diagonal operators both under a natural boundedness condition on the canonical projections and for all spaces equipped with the $L^1$-norm.

</details>


### [115] [Chaining, tree and measure for some canonical processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01709)
*Xuanang Hu,Hanchao Wang,Xinglong Wu*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文证明了两条关于规范过程研究中距离族的确定性结果：一条由增长条件导出可容许划分方案，另一条给出参数化分离树表示并与majorizing-measure量比较；关键在于证明不依赖过程分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将规范过程理论中通常依赖概率假设（随机变量、独立性、尾函数、矩估计）的距离不等式，转化为纯粹确定性的组合/度量结构，以揭示本质并扩展适用范围。

Method: 确定性论证：从增长条件构造可容许划分方案；用参数化分离树表示距离族，并与majorizing-measure量比较；对有限指标集给出递归构造；对正则对数凹尾的规范过程验证假设成立。

Result: 分离树估计的一方向对Bernoulli过程成立（无需额外假设）；对有界凸无条件指标集证明反向估计；有限指标集情形下增长论证给出可容许划分与参数化分离树的递归构造。

Conclusion: 距离族的上述表示与划分可由确定性条件刻画，不依赖过程分布；该框架统一并扩展了规范过程的相关估计，并明确了对数凹尾与Bernoulli情形的适用边界。

Abstract: We prove two deterministic results for families of distances arising in the study of canonical processes. The first derives an admissible partition scheme from a growth condition. The second gives a representation in terms of parameterized separation trees and compares it with the corresponding majorizing-measure quantities. The main point is that the proofs do not depend on the distribution of the underlying process: once the initial distance and the family of distances are given, no random variables, independence, tail functions, or moment estimates are used.
  For canonical processes with regular log-concave tails, the assumptions of the abstract results follow from the usual regularity conditions. One direction of the separation-tree estimate also applies to Bernoulli processes without these additional assumptions, and we prove the reverse estimate for bounded convex unconditional index sets. We also give a version of the growth argument for points which, for finite index sets, leads to a recursive construction of admissible partitions and parameterized separation trees.

</details>


### [116] [Ballistic Speed and Potential-Theoretic Recurrence for Homogeneous Open Quantum Random Walks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01844)
*Ameur Dhahri,Chul Ki Ko,Farrukh Mukhamedov,Hyun Jae Yoo*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 该文将有限程开放量子随机游走（OQRW）的均方根弹道输运与势论回复性联系起来，在给定谱假设下证明了周期均匀局部极限定理、Green函数与势核渐近，并给出漂移-维数的暂留/回复分类，还通过h-变换处理可约游走。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望把经典随机游走中"弹道输运—势论回复性"的对应关系推广到开放量子随机游走这一非自伴、非可逆的量子 Markov 半群框架中，从而建立均方根（RMS）弹道速度与暂留/回复性之间的定量联系，并为可约情形提供分解工具。

Method: 针对齐次 OQRW，假设局部信道本原、Fourier 边缘谱为有限个简单周期特征值、无非零平稳 Fourier 模、存在非退化二次谱项；据此建立周期均匀局部极限定理并导出有限集返回界。对中心化情形求强 Green 函数（≥3 维）与势核（1、2 维）渐近。对可约游走，用显式调和 h-变换把每个吸收分量化为 OQRW，并借助精确有限陷阱与稀疏反射壁的非齐次方法处理零速与 TOM 回复。

Result: 通过上述假设得到：RMS 弹道速度等于漂移范数；非零漂移蕴含暂留；中心化游走在有效维数 1、2 中回复，在高维中暂留，且在低维给出原点投影在 TOM 中的回复。可约情形下，平方 RMS 速度等于各分量漂移平方的经验吸收加权均值，但降维漂移分类仍需分量返回估计。一个中心化非对易族在所有维度验证了基本谱假设并给出显式势常数。

Conclusion: RMS 弹道输运与势论回复性在开放量子随机游走中形成一致的对应框架：速度刻画暂留/回复，h-变换给出可约结构的 Green 势分解，非齐次的有限陷阱/反射壁方法补充了零速与 TOM 回复情形。

Abstract: Our study connects root-mean-square ballistic transport to potential-theoretic recurrence for finite-range open quantum random walks. In homogeneous walks with primitive local channels, finitely many simple periodic Fourier peripheral eigenvalues, no nonzero stationary Fourier mode, and a nondegenerate quadratic spectral term, we prove a periodic uniform local limit theorem and recover exponential finite-set return bounds for nonzero drift. The centered case yields strong Green-function asymptotics in at least three dimensions, as well as potential-kernel asymptotics in one and two dimensions. According to these assumptions, the RMS ballistic speed equals the drift norm, nonzero drift indicates transience, and centered walks are recurring in effective dimensions one and two but transitory in higher dimensions. The low-dimensional finding shows a recurrence of the origin projection in TOM. For reducible walks, an explicit harmonic \(h\)-transform converts each absorption component to an OQRW, providing a detailed breakdown of Green occupation potentials. The squared RMS speed is the absorption-weighted mean of squared component drifts, but the reduced drift-dimension classification also needs specific component return estimations. A centered noncommuting family validates the fundamental spectral assumptions in all dimensions and provides explicit potential constants. Exact finite traps and sparse reflecting barriers provide a complementary nonhomogeneous method for zero speed and TOM recurrence.

</details>


### [117] [Non-separable combinatorial Banach spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01919)
*Piotr Borodulin-Nadzieja,Mikołaj Marsy,Kamil Ryduchowski*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 研究不可数密度的组合Banach空间，刻画长c₀与长ℓ₁同构嵌入的条件，讨论固定基数无条件基Banach空间的（可补）万有性，并证明由Suslin树生成的Banach空间不含不可数等边集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合Banach空间（由有限集族诱导）在同构分类与万有空间理论中具有重要地位，但其在不可数密度情形下的结构性质尚缺乏系统刻画，尤其缺少对长c₀、长ℓ₁嵌入及等边集等基本几何/同构不变量存在性的判据。

Method: 在不可数密度框架下研究由有限集族生成的组合Banach空间，建立一般性定理，用以刻画该类空间包含长c₀与长ℓ₁同构副本的条件；并考察具有固定基数无条件基的Banach空间范畴中的（可补）万有空间存在性，结合具体构造实例（如Suslin树生成的Banach空间）进行分析。

Result: 建立了不可数密度组合Banach空间的一般定理，给出了其包含长c₀与长ℓ₁同构副本的刻画；讨论了固定基数无条件基Banach空间的（可补）万有空间存在性并给出若干实例；特别地，证明Suslin树生成的Banach空间不含不可数等边集。

Conclusion: 组合Banach空间在不可数密度下的同构与几何结构与树型组合构造密切相关，长c₀/长ℓ₁嵌入可由一般判据刻画；固定基数无条件基范畴中的万有性存在条件受组合结构制约，而Suslin树类空间构成不含不可数等边集的重要反例。

Abstract: We study combinatorial Banach spaces, i.e. Banach spaces induced by families of finite sets, of uncountable density. We prove some general theorems, for example we characterize when such spaces contain isomorphic copies of long $c_0$ and long $\ell_1$. We discuss the existence of Banach spaces which are (complementedly) universal in the category of Banach spaces with unconditional basis of fixed cardinality. We present some examples, in particular we show that the Banach spaces generated by Suslin trees do not contain uncountable equilateral sets.

</details>


<div id='math.CO'></div>

# math.CO [[Back]](#toc)

### [118] [A Counterexample to Wormald's Conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00086)
*James Alexander Schreib*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文给出了Wormald猜想的一个反例：一个16个顶点的三次图（$K_{3,3}$与一个10顶点桥接图的非交并）不能被划分为两个同构的生成线性森林，其障碍来自奇偶性。通过附加$K_4$可得到所有阶数$16+4t$的反例，该反例是非连通的，连通情形仍未解决，完整族已在Lean中无需自定义公理地验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Wormald猜想是一个关于图的边分解的公开问题。作者旨在通过构造反例来否证该猜想，揭示其在一般情形下的不成立性。

Method: 构造一个16顶点三次图：$K_{3,3}$与一个由两个$K_4$的细分副本构成的10顶点桥接图的非交并。利用奇偶性论证：任何同构生成线性森林划分会迫使每个单色分量为偶数阶，但需要将桥的五顶点一侧划分为此类分量，产生矛盾。通过附加$K_4$副本推广到所有$16+4t$阶。完整族在Lean中形式化验证。

Result: 该16顶点图不存在将边划分为两个同构生成线性森林的方案，从而构成反例；通过附加$K_4$得到无穷族反例（阶数为$16+4t$）。反例是非连通的，连通情形悬而未决。Lean验证成功且无需自定义公理。

Conclusion: Wormald猜想被否证；反例族非连通，连通情形仍开放。所有构造均在Lean中形式化验证，增强结果可靠性。

Abstract: We give a counterexample to Wormald's conjecture. A 16-vertex cubic graph, the disjoint union of $K_{3,3}$ with a 10-vertex bridged graph built from two subdivided copies of $K_4$, has no partition of its edges into two isomorphic spanning linear forests. The obstruction is parity: any such partition would force every monochromatic component to have even order while requiring a five-vertex side of a bridge to be partitioned into such components. Adjoining copies of $K_4$ gives counterexamples in every order $16+4t$. The counterexample is disconnected and leaves the connected case unresolved. The full family is verified in Lean without custom axioms.

</details>


### [119] [Periodic Joint Co-tiles of Independent Tiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00099)
*Hu Tan,Ying Zhang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该论文证明了在 $\Z^d$（$d\geq2$）中，$d-1$ 个独立有限瓦片只要存在联合余瓦片，就存在完全周期的联合余瓦片，从而去掉了 Meyerovitch–Sanadhya–Solomon 周期存在性定理中的性质 $(\star)$，并在 $d=2$ 时恢复 Bhattacharya 定理；进一步给出更一般的周期存在性、周期可铺性刻画及判定算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Meyerovitch–Sanadhya–Solomon 的周期存在性定理需要额外的性质 $(\star)$，限制了其适用范围；作者希望去掉这一附加条件，在更一般的有限族情形下建立周期联合余瓦片的存在性，并推广 Bhattacharya 在 $d=2$ 的结果。

Method: 推广 Greenfeld–Tao 的布尔正规形方法：构造平移轨道闭包中的一个构型，使其在有限指数格子的陪集上的限制要么在秩 $d-1$ 子群下不变，要么由密度为 $1/2$ 的三项仿射小数部分公式给出；铺砌方程将这些限制配对成一个有限二部图，再用二着色把限制替换为常数同时保持所有铺砌方程；由此推出独立伴随下的周期可铺性刻画以及含独立 $(d-1)$-子族的族的判定算法（维数作为输入的一部分）。

Result: 对每个 $d\geq2$，$d-1$ 个独立有限瓦片若存在联合余瓦片则存在完全周期联合余瓦片；更一般地，任何存在联合余瓦片且其指示函数可写为完全周期实函数加上有限个有界实函数（每个在秩 $d-1$ 子群下不变）的有限族均具周期存在性。当 $d=2$ 时恢复 Bhattacharya 定理；得到周期可铺性由独立伴随刻画的结论，以及含独立 $(d-1)$-子族的族的判定算法。

Conclusion: 该工作去除了周期存在性定理中对性质 $(\star)$ 的依赖，把独立有限瓦片的周期联合余瓦片存在性推广到一般有限族，并给出可判定性结果；其布尔正规形与二部图/二着色技术为周期铺砌问题提供了新的组合与代数工具。

Abstract: For every $d\geq2$, we prove that $d-1$ independent finite tiles in $\Z^d$ admit a fully periodic joint co-tile whenever they admit a joint co-tile. Here independence means that the tiles contain the origin and every choice of one nonzero vector from each tile is linearly independent. This removes property $(\star)$ from the periodic existence theorem of Meyerovitch, Sanadhya and Solomon and recovers Bhattacharya's theorem when $d=2$. More generally, we prove periodic existence for any finite family admitting a joint co-tile whose indicator is a fully periodic real function plus finitely many bounded real functions, each invariant under a subgroup of rank $d-1$. Extending the Boolean normal-form method of Greenfeld and Tao, we obtain a configuration in the translation orbit closure whose restrictions to cosets of a finite-index lattice are either invariant under a rank-$(d-1)$ subgroup or given by a three-term affine fractional-part formula with density one half. The tiling equations pair the restrictions given by this formula and determine a finite bipartite graph. A two-coloring replaces those restrictions by constants while preserving every tiling equation. We deduce a characterization of periodic tilability by independent companions and a decision algorithm for families containing an independent $(d-1)$-subfamily, with the dimension as part of the input.

</details>


### [120] [Counting Weight-$k$ Italian Dominating Sets on Trees and Cycles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00108)
*Pingping Shao,Chengye Zhao*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究路径、树、环上各类权重意大利支配函数(IDF)的计数问题，将系数计算归结为受约束的整数组合问题，给出部分闭式表达，并设计计算意大利支配多项式全部系数的O(n²)时间、O(n)空间动态规划算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 意大利支配多项式是支配多项式与罗马支配多项式的自然类比，但此前缺乏对固定图类上其全部系数（而非仅支配数）的系统计算方法。核心动机是建立IDF计数与受约束整数组合之间的显式联系，并据此实现高效精确计算。

Method: 将f(v)=0顶点的邻域和≥2条件改写为亏量变量y(v)=2−f(v)，使d_I(G,2n−j)等价于把j拆成n个每部分≤2的组合数，且满足局部邻接容量约束；由此推导d_I(C_n,2n−j)（j=0,…,5）为三项式系数减去显式枚举的禁用构型的闭式；算法上分别对路径、一般树（后序合并带亏量参数）和环（边界条件化）给出O(n²)时间、O(n)空间动态规划，并附正确性证明与复杂度分析。

Result: 获得d_I(C_n,2n−j)（j=0,…,5）的闭式表达；对路径、一般树、环分别实现O(n²)/O(n)的动态规划算法，可计算多项式全部系数；所有数值结果经暴力枚举验证一致，并提供完整数值表。已有结论γ_I(P_n)=⌊n/2⌋+1与γ_I(C_n)=⌈n/2⌉仅引用而未重证。

Conclusion: 通过将意大利支配多项式系数刻画为受约束整数组合，本文在路径、树、环上实现了IDF全系数的精确高效计数，连接了支配多项式理论与组合计数方法，并为后续图类上的多项式系数计算提供了可推广的DP框架。

Abstract: We study the problem of counting \emph{Italian dominating functions} (IDFs) of each possible weight on paths, trees, and cycles. An Italian dominating function on a graph $G=(V,E)$ is a function $f\colon V\to\{0,1,2\}$ such that every vertex $v$ with $f(v)=0$ satisfies $\sum_{u\in N(v)}f(u)\ge 2$; the \emph{Italian domination polynomial} $D_I(G,x)=\sum_k d_I(G,k)\,x^k$ records the number $d_I(G,k)$ of such functions of weight~$k$. This polynomial is the Italian analogue of the well-studied domination and Roman domination polynomials, and computing its coefficients on a fixed graph class is, in essence, a \emph{constrained integer composition} problem: passing to the deficit variables $y(v)=2-f(v)$, each coefficient $d_I(G,2n-j)$ counts the number of compositions of $j$ into $n$ parts, each at most~$2$, that additionally satisfy a local adjacency capacity constraint. We make this connection explicit and use it to derive closed-form expressions for $d_I(C_n,2n-j)$, $j=0,\ldots,5$, as trinomial coefficients minus explicitly enumerated forbidden configurations. On the algorithmic side, we give dynamic programming algorithms that compute \emph{all} coefficients of $D_I$ on path graphs ($O(n^2)$ time, $O(n)$ space), on general trees ($O(n^2)$ time, $O(n)$ space, via a post-order merge with a deficit parameter), and on cycle graphs ($O(n^2)$ time, $O(n)$ space, via boundary conditioning), together with formal correctness proofs and complexity analyses. The known values $γ_I(P_n)=\lfloor n/2\rfloor+1$ and $γ_I(C_n)=\lceil n/2\rceil$ are recalled with references rather than reproved. All numerical results are verified against brute-force enumeration, and complete tables are provided.

</details>


### [121] [Probability threshold for the stability of $α(G(n,r,s))$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00144)
*M. M. Koshelev,A. M. Raigorodskii*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 研究图 $G(n,r,s)$（顶点为 $[n]$ 的所有 $r$-子集，边连接交集大小为 $s$ 的两顶点）在 $r>2s+2$ 时的独立数精确稳定性概率阈值，改进了 Pyaderkin 关于渐近稳定性的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: $G(n,r,s)$ 是 Erdős–Ko–Rado 型（Kneser 型）图的推广，其独立数稳定性是极值组合与随机图交叉的核心问题。Pyaderkin 此前仅得到独立数的渐近稳定性，本文希望给出更强的精确稳定性阈值。

Method: 通过概率/随机方法分析 $G(n,r,s)$ 的独立数，在条件 $r>2s+2$ 下确定其独立数达到精确稳定的概率阈值。

Result: 确定了 $G(n,r,s)$（$r>2s+2$）独立数精确稳定的概率阈值，强于 Pyaderkin 的渐近稳定性结论。

Conclusion: 将独立数稳定性从渐近层面推进到精确层面，深化了对该类 Kneser 型图随机结构临界行为的理解。

Abstract: Let $G(n,r,s)$ be a graph $(V,E)$ such that $V = \binom{[n]}{r}$ and $E = \{(u, v)| |u \cap v| = s\}. $In this paper we study the probability threshold for the exact stability of the independence number of $G(n, r, s)$ where $r > 2s+2$. We improve the previous result of Pyaderkin that implied asymptotic stability of the independence number.

</details>


### [122] [Laurentness of Svinin's even-order recurrences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00307)
*Qiyue Tang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 证明了 Svinin 偶数阶递推数列的每一项关于初始值均为 Laurent 多项式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Svinin 提出的偶数阶递推数列其 Laurent 性质此前未被确立，而 Laurent 现象是判断递推数列可积性与簇代数结构的重要标志，因此需要给出严格证明。

Method: 通过分析递推关系式，给出每项可表示为初始值的有理函数，并证明其分母为初始值的单项式（即无其他因式），从而得出 Laurent 多项式性质。

Result: 证明了 Svinin 偶数阶递推的每一项都是初始值的 Laurent 多项式。

Conclusion: Svinin 偶数阶递推具有 Laurent 性质，符合可积递推序列的典型特征，为其与簇代数及可积系统的联系提供了支撑。

Abstract: We prove every term of Svinin's even-order recurrences is a Laurent polynomial in the initial values.

</details>


### [123] [Optimal discrepancy for triangles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00375)
*Gleb Smirnov*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 证明平面上任意n点集都存在对所有三角形偏差为O(n^{1/4})的二着色，改进先前上界并匹配经典下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三角形偏差问题中，先前最优上界为O(n^{1/4}√log n)，与经典下界Ω(n^{1/4})之间仍存在对数因子差距，本文旨在消除该差距。

Method: （摘要未给出具体方法，仅陈述结果。）

Result: 证明任意n点平面点集存在二着色，使所有三角形的偏差为O(n^{1/4})。

Conclusion: 上界改进至O(n^{1/4})，与经典下界匹配，从而解决了平面三角形偏差问题（至常数因子）。

Abstract: We prove that every $n$-point set in the plane has a two-coloring with discrepancy $O(n^{1/4})$ for all triangles. This improves the previous bound $O(n^{1/4}\sqrt{\log n})$ and matches the classical lower bound.

</details>


### [124] [The Algebraic Connectivity and Laplacian Spectral Radius of Token Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00500)
*Xiaodi Song,Cristina Dalfó,Miquel Àngel Fiol,Shenggui Zhang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究图与其token图的代数连通度及Laplacian谱半径关系，给出组合/代数证明，并刻画谱半径相等的图类（含树）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有结果指出图的代数连通度≥其token图的代数连通度，且图的Laplacian谱半径≤其token图的谱半径；关于代数连通度相等的猜想此前借助连续Markov链随机游走理论证明。作者希望用组合/代数方法重新证明并推广，同时研究谱半径何时严格小于以及相等情形。

Method: 通过研究图、加边后图及其token图的代数连通度得到若干结果，给出组合/代数证明；讨论Laplacian谱半径小于token图谱半径的条件；刻画与其token图具有相同Laplacian谱半径的图，包括树。

Result: 获得了图、加边图及其token图代数连通度的相关结果并给出组合/代数证明；给出了Laplacian谱半径严格小于token图谱半径的若干条件；刻画了与token图具有相同Laplacian谱半径的图，涵盖树。

Conclusion: 以组合/代数方法处理图与其token图之间的代数连通度与Laplacian谱半径关系，补充了既有基于连续Markov链的证明，并明确了谱半径相等图类的特征。

Abstract: For a graph $G=(V,E)$ of order $n$ and an integer $k$ between $1$ and $\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor$, its token graph $F_k(G)$ is the graph whose vertices consist of the $\binom{n}{k}$ $k$-subsets of $V$, and two vertices of $F_k(G)$ are adjacent whenever their symmetric difference is an edge in $E$. It was found that the algebraic connectivity of a graph is greater than or equal to that of its token graph, while the Laplacian spectral radius of a graph is less than or equal to that of its token graph. Moreover, a conjecture that the algebraic connectivity of a graph coincides with that of its token graph has been proved by using the theory of continuous Markov chains of random walks.
  In this paper, we derive some results about the algebraic connectivities of a graph and the same graph after adding new edges and their token graphs to obtain a combinatorial/algebraic proof. Besides, we provide some conditions under which the Laplacian spectral radius of a graph is less than that of its token graph. Finally, we characterize the graphs that have the same Laplacian spectral radius as their token graphs, including trees.

</details>


### [125] [Seven Coincidences and Sixty Symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00572)
*Ernesto Lupercio,Evgeniya Shaydurova*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 受John Baez关于二项式系数中七个例外巧合的提问启发，本文为A_5的表示构造了七个外幂恒等式，其对称性源于单纯形棱与二十面体的顶点、轴和面及稳定化子特征标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: John Baez提出了关于二项式系数中七组例外巧合的问题，本文以此为动机展开研究。

Method: 构造A_5表示的七个外幂恒等式，利用单纯形棱与二十面体的顶点、轴、面以及稳定化子的特征标来解释其共同对称性；枚举指定维度下所有表示同构类的有序对。

Result: 除平凡作用对以外，1540处的置换对和7140处的复单项对是唯一的；在3003处，Klein十二点除子通过多项式除法、微分与留数分解给出一个非典范交织算子。

Conclusion: A_5表示的外幂恒等式统一于二十面体对称性，并刻画了特定维度下表示对的唯一性及3003处的非典范交织结构。

Abstract: Motivated by John Baez's question about seven exceptional coincidences among binomial coefficients, we construct seven exterior-power identities for representations of $A_5$. Their common symmetry comes from simplex edges and icosahedral vertices, axes, and faces, together with characters of stabilizers. We enumerate all ordered pairs of representation isomorphism classes in the prescribed dimensions. Apart from the pair of trivial actions, the permutation pair at $1540$ and the complex monomial pair at $7140$ are unique. At $3003$, Klein's twelve-point divisor gives a noncanonical intertwiner factored through polynomial division, differentiation, and residues.

</details>


### [126] [Lower bounds for the magnitude of the minimum eigenvalue of graphs with applications to MaxCut and Chowla's cosine problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01657)
*Fredy Yip*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该文改进了Jin等人关于最小特征值绝对值很小的图的递归估计，并将结果应用于谱图论、差异理论和Chowla余弦问题，完整解决了Räty-Sudakov-Tomon猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Jin、Milojević、Tomon和Zhang此前建立了强大的递归估计，联系图的正特征值与绝对值很小的最小特征值。本文旨在改进该估计，以便在谱图论、差异理论和Chowla余弦问题中获得更优结果，并解决Räty、Sudakov和Tomon提出的猜想。Janzer、Tomon和Yip的近期结果可将最小特征值估计直接转化为相应的surplus估计。

Method: 建立Jin等人递归估计的精炼版本，得到改进的估计；利用Janzer、Tomon和Yip的结果将最小特征值估计直接转化为surplus估计；并将这些工具应用于远离所有团的不交并的图。

Result: 完整解决了Räty、Sudakov和Tomon的猜想：对n个顶点、最小特征值为λ_n、surplus为sp(G)的图G，若G与所有团的不交并集ε-远，则|λ_n| ≥ Ω_ε(n^{1/4})且sp(G) ≥ Ω_ε(n^{5/4})；当G与所有团的不交并集n^{-o(1)}-远时，|λ_n| ≥ n^{1/4-o(1)}且sp(G) ≥ n^{5/4-o(1)}；此外，含m条边的K_t-free图的surplus至少为m^{0.614-o(1)}（m趋于无穷）。

Conclusion: 该文通过精炼递归估计并借助特征值与surplus之间的转化工具，不仅改进了谱图论、差异理论和Chowla余弦问题的相关估计，还完整解决了Räty-Sudakov-Tomon猜想，并给出了K_t-free图surplus的下界。

Abstract: Jin, Milojević, Tomon and Zhang established a powerful recursive estimate relating the positive eigenvalues of a graph whose least eigenvalue is small in absolute value. We establish a refinement of this result, yielding improved estimates across spectral graph theory and discrepancy theory, and for Chowla's cosine problem. Recent results of Janzer, Tomon and Yip allow us to directly transfer least eigenvalue estimates to the corresponding surplus estimates.
  Using these tools, we fully resolve a conjecture of Räty, Sudakov and Tomon. We show that, for an $n$-vertex graph $G$ with least eigenvalue $λ_n$ and surplus $\operatorname{sp}(G)$, if $G$ is $ε$-far from all disjoint unions of cliques, then $|λ_n|\geq Ω_ε(n^{1/4})$ and $\operatorname{sp}(G)\geq Ω_ε(n^{5/4})$. Furthermore, we show that when $G$ is $n^{-o(1)}$-far from all disjoint unions of cliques, we have $|λ_n|\geq n^{1/4 - o(1)}$ and $\operatorname{sp}(G)\geq n^{5/4 - o(1)}$. Finally, we show that the surplus of a $K_t$-free graph with $m$ edges is at least $m^{0.614 - o(1)}$ as $m$ tends to infinity.

</details>


### [127] [Stability conditions and subdivisions of Lawrence polytopes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00591)
*Natasha Crepeau*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该文建立了图的稳定性条件、退化集与拟阵单元素扩张之间的对应关系，并用Lawrence多面体的细分构造稳定性条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究节点曲线（nodal curve）的稳定性条件可由其对偶图上的组合数据刻画，需要理解稳定性条件产生的退化集的结构及其分类。

Method: 以图G的对偶图、双连通顶点子集上的整数赋值为稳定性条件；将退化集与图拟阵M(G)的单元素扩张对应；利用有向拟阵单元素扩张与对偶有向拟阵Lawrence多面体细分的双射，从cographic拟阵的Lawrence多面体细分构造稳定性条件；并在退化集偏序中引入下界刻画。

Result: 证明图G的退化集对应图拟阵M(G)的单元素扩张；部分扩张可定向为有向图拟阵的单元素扩张；由cographic拟阵M*(G)的Lawrence多面体细分可构造G上的稳定性条件；当退化集以所有双连通子集为偏序下界时，对应M(G)的可定向扩张。

Conclusion: 退化集与拟阵（有向拟阵）单元素扩张的对应为稳定性条件提供了组合与多面体层面的统一框架，并可用于构造和分类稳定性条件。

Abstract: Let $X$ be a nodal curve, and $G$ be the graph dual to $X$. A stability condition on a graph $G$ is an assignment of integers to the biconnected subsets of vertices of $G$ satisfying some desired properties. Stability conditions yield degeneracy sets, which are certain collections of biconnected subsets of $G$, but degeneracy sets can also be described without a reference stability condition. We show that the degeneracy sets of a graph $G$ correspond to the single-element extensions of the graphic matroid $M(G)$. Some single-element extensions of the graphic matroid $M(G)$ can be oriented to be single-element extensions of the oriented graphic matroid $\mathcal{M}(G)$. Single-element extensions of oriented matroids are in bijection with subdivisions of the Lawrence polytope of the dual oriented matroid. We construct stability conditions on $G$ from subdivisions of the Lawrence polytope of the cographic matroid $\mathcal{M}^\ast(G)$. Finally, we show that any degeneracy set $\mathcal{D}$ of a graph $G$ that has the set of all biconnected subsets of $G$ as a lower bound in the poset of degeneracy sets corresponds to an orientable extension of $M(G)$.

</details>


### [128] [A Pick-type theorem for halfway-lattice polygons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00696)
*Charlene Chu,Yael Karshon*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文类比Pick定理，研究边位于格点“中点处”的幺模多边形，给出了其面积A与内部格点数I和顶点数V的关系式：A = I - V/8 + 1/2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Pick定理刻画了顶点落在格点上的多边形面积与内部格点数I、边界格点数B的关系A = I + B/2 - 1。本文旨在将这一格点几何结果推广到边位于格点中点的多边形情形，探索类似面积公式。

Method: 考虑平面中边恰好位于格点之间中点位置的多边形，并在其同时满足幺模（unimodular）条件的情形下，借助内部格点数I与顶点数V推导面积表达式。

Result: 对该类幺模多边形，面积可表示为 A = I - V/8 + 1/2，其中I为内部格点数，V为顶点数。

Conclusion: 该结果给出了类似Pick定理的面积公式，将面积与内部格点数、顶点数直接联系，是Pick定理在格点中点边多边形上的一种推广。

Abstract: Given a polygon in the plane with vertices on lattice points, Pick's theorem expresses its area A in terms of the number I of lattice points in its interior and the number B of lattice points in its boundary: A = I + B/2 - 1. We consider polygons in the plane whose edges are halfway between lattice points. For such a polygon that is also unimodular, we express its area A in terms of the number I of lattice points in its interior and the number V of its vertices: A = I - V/8 + 1/2 .

</details>


### [129] [$k$-arrangements of pseudolines and pseudocircles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00773)
*Jan Kynčl,Carolina Medina,Gelasio Salazar*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究伪直线$k$-排列中不可避免的构型数量随$k$的增长规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Ramsey意义下，简单$1$-排列中唯一不可避免的构型是循环排列，作者希望探究将该结论推广到一般$k$-排列时，不可避免构型的数量如何随$k$变化。

Method: 通过构造与分析简单$k$-排列（任意两条曲线恰好相交$k$次且均为交叉点，无三线共点），并结合Ramsey型组合论证，对不可避免构型进行计数；对偶数$k$的情形，将方法推广到伪圆$k$-排列。

Result: 证明了对任意$m\ge 3$，大小为$m$的不可避免简单$k$-排列的数量随$k$呈指数增长，且该增长与$m$无关；对偶数$k$，在伪圆$k$-排列上得到类似结论。

Conclusion: 不同于$k=1$时唯一不可避免构型的情形，一般$k$下不可避免构型数量随$k$指数增长，表明不可避免构型的多样性随$k$显著增加，该现象在伪圆情形（偶数$k$）同样成立。

Abstract: A $k$-arrangement of pseudolines is a set of bi-infinite curves in the plane such that any two of them intersect each other in exactly $k$ points, at which they cross, and it is simple if no three curves meet at a common point. Cyclic arrangements are the only simple $1$-arrangements of pseudolines that are unavoidable, in the Ramsey spirit: for each fixed $m\ge 1$, every sufficiently large simple $1$-arrangement of pseudolines has a cyclic subarrangement of size $m$. We show that, for every $m\ge 3$, the number of unavoidable simple $k$-arrangements of pseudolines of size $m$ grows exponentially with $k$, independently of $m$. For even $k$, we prove an analogous result for $k$-arrangements of pseudocircles.

</details>


### [130] [Riordan Arrays and Shifted Hankel Determinants of OEIS A005773](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00775)
*Daniel Yaqubi,Madjid Mirzavaziri*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 研究与Motzkin-prefix序列(OEIS A005773)相关的两个Riordan阵列，通过生成矩阵的边界分解得到Motzkin端点分布的周期三变换及按终高模3计数的精确公式，并导出三阶、四阶Hankel行列式的闭式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motzkin路径及其前缀序列的细化计数与Hankel行列式在组合数论中具重要地位；作者希望借助Riordan阵列与生成矩阵的结构分解，揭示端点分布的周期性并给出高阶Hankel行列式的显式表达。

Method: 利用Riordan阵列理论，通过对其production matrix（生成矩阵）进行简单的边界因子分解(boundary factorization)，建立两个阵列间的联系；同时对移位Hankel矩阵给出统一分解。

Result: 获得Motzkin端点分布的周期三变换，得到按最终高度模3分类的路径精确计数公式；并由移位Hankel矩阵的统一分解，导出第三、第四Hankel行列式Δ_n^(3)与Δ_n^(4)的显式闭式。

Conclusion: Riordan阵列的生成矩阵边界分解是研究Motzkin路径端点分布与Hankel行列式的有效工具，可推广至更高阶Hankel行列式的计算。

Abstract: We study two Riordan arrays associated with the Motzkin-prefix sequence (\href{https://oeis.org/A005773}{OEIS A005773}). By connecting their production matrices through a simple boundary factorization, we obtain a period-three transformation on Motzkin endpoint distributions and exact formulas for paths by final height modulo~$3$. In addition, a unified factorization for the shifted Hankel matrices leads directly to explicit closed forms for the third and fourth Hankel determinants, $Δ_n^{(3)}$ and~$Δ_n^{(4)}$.

</details>


### [131] [Ascending, Bridge, and Unknotting Numbers of Knotted Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00788)
*Bahar Acu,Puttipong Pongtanapaisan,Ritikaa Singhal*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文为虚拟空间图与焊接空间图定义了上升数与桥数，证明了上升数以桥数为下界，并借此比较了不同的交叉变换解结概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虚拟空间图与焊接空间图中已有多种"通过交叉变换解结"的概念，但这些概念之间的关系尚不明确，缺乏统一的比较工具。

Method: 定义虚拟空间图与焊接空间图的上升数（ascending number）与桥数（bridge number），并建立上升数关于桥数的下界不等式。

Result: 证明了上升数可由桥数给出下界，即二者之间存在不等式关系。

Conclusion: 该不等式关系为比较虚拟空间图的不同交叉变换解结概念提供了有效手段。

Abstract: In this paper we define the ascending number and bridge number for virtual and welded spatial graphs. We prove that the former admits a lower bound in terms of the latter. This relationship allows us to compare different notions of unknotting virtual spatial graphs by crossing changes.

</details>


### [132] [Reversing the Mostar line-graph inequality with long pendant paths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00829)
*Kerem Kat*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明在固定根图接上长度t的悬挂路径后，原图与线图Mostar指数之差在每个奇偶类上最终为仿射函数，且存在统一截断；斜率由距离层邻居数决定，非二部核斜率为正。三角链给出最大度3且在任意正圈秩c上满足Mo(L(G))>Mo(G)的无穷图族，解决Alex-Indulal问题3.3。零斜率时用仙人掌分支质量公式判定相等，并刻画固定核长期相等当且仅当c为奇数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Mostar指数在线图与原图之间的差异，关注悬挂路径增长时的渐近行为，并解决Alex-Indulal问题3.3关于Mo(L(G))>Mo(G)的构造问题，同时刻画相等情形。

Method: 对固定根图K接长度t的悬挂路径得K_t，分析Mo(L(K_t))-Mo(K_t)在奇偶类上的仿射性，给出统一截断；用距离层邻居数刻画斜率；构造三角链与二叉树；对零斜率情形用仙人掌分支质量公式判定相等。

Result: 证明差值在每个奇偶类超越尖锐统一截断后恰为仿射；斜率依赖距离层邻居数，非二部核为正；三角链给出任意正圈秩c、最大度3且Mo(L(G))>Mo(G)的无穷图族；零斜率时仙人掌分支质量公式判定相等，相同顶点剖面未必给相同截距；固定核对所有足够长悬挂都相等当且仅当c为奇数；偶数c时二叉树构造在一个最终奇偶类上相等。

Conclusion: Mostar指数在线图与原图之差随悬挂路径增长呈奇偶类仿射规律，非二部核必然为正；解决了Alex-Indulal问题3.3；相等情形由圈秩奇偶与仙人掌分支质量精确刻画，最大度3即可实现。

Abstract: Let $K_t$ be obtained by attaching a pendant path of length $t$ to a fixed rooted graph $K$. We prove that $\mathrm{Mo}(L(K_t))-\mathrm{Mo}(K_t)$, the difference between its line-graph and original Mostar indices, is exactly affine on each parity class beyond a sharp uniform cutoff. The slope depends on distance-level neighbor counts and is positive for every non-bipartite core. Triangle chains give infinitely many graphs with $\mathrm{Mo}(L(G))>\mathrm{Mo}(G)$ at every positive cyclomatic number $c$, with maximum degree three, resolving Alex-Indulal Problem 3.3. At zero slope, a cactus branch-mass formula decides equality; identical vertex profiles need not give identical intercepts. Fixed cores giving equality for all sufficiently long attachments exist exactly when $c$ is odd. Maximum degree three suffices, and at every even $c$ a binary-tree construction gives equality on one eventual parity class.

</details>


### [133] [The least signless Laplacian eigenvalue of $\{C_3,C_5\}$-free graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00875)
*Qi Zhou*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文将Brandt关于无三角形正则图最小无符号拉普拉斯特征值≤4n/25的猜想及相关更强界，推广到无{C3,C5}图情形，证明任意n阶无{C3,C5}图满足qmin(G)<0.0569n，且无需正则性假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Brandt猜想正则无三角形图的最小无符号拉普拉斯特征值上界为4n/25，Balogh等用旗代数证明了对所有无三角形图的更强界15n/94。作者希望考察额外排除五边形（即限制为无{C3,C5}图）后该上界能否进一步显著降低。

Method: 结合七顶点旗不等式与局部Rayleigh约束，在计数论证过程中保留最小特征值信息；并通过精确整数证书验证所需不等式成立。

Result: 证明任意n阶无{C3,C5}图G满足qmin(G)<0.0569n，且不含任何正则性假设。

Conclusion: 额外排除五边形可使最小无符号拉普拉斯特征值的上界进一步收紧至0.0569n，方法借助旗代数与局部Rayleigh约束及整数证书得以严格验证。

Abstract: Brandt [Discrete Math. 183 (1998) 17--25] conjectured that the least signless Laplacian eigenvalue of every regular triangle-free graph of order $n$ is at most $4n/25$. Using flag algebras, Balogh, Clemen, Lidický, Norin and Volec [SIAM J. Discrete Math. 37 (2023) 1173--1179] established the stronger bound $15n/94$ for all triangle-free graphs. We investigate the effect of additionally excluding pentagons and prove that every $\{C_3,C_5\}$-free graph $G$ of order $n$ satisfies $\qmin(G)<0.0569n$, without any regularity assumption. The proof combines seven-vertex flag inequalities with local Rayleigh constraints that retain the least eigenvalue throughout the counting argument. An exact integer certificate establishes the required inequality.

</details>


### [134] [Fair Duels after a False Start](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00880)
*Tanya Khovanova,Tejo Madhavarapu*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 在公平决斗博弈中，两人轮流射击，下一枪分配给目前获胜可能性较低的一方。Cooper与Dutle证明，当成功概率相同且p→0时，射击序列收敛于Thue–Morse序列。本文允许序列先以任意有限前缀开始，再恢复该公平准则。结果表明：除非初始前缀本身是Thue–Morse序列或其补序列的前缀，否则在p→0极限下序列最终周期，且最小周期为2的幂、不超过4^{n+1}，重复块也是Thue–Morse序列的前缀。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓展Cooper–Dutle的公平决斗模型，考察初始有限前缀扰动对射击序列渐近行为的影响，以理解局部初始条件是否改变全局极限结构。

Method: 在公平准则下，依次将下一枪分配给当前获胜可能性较低者；先执行任意长度n的有限前缀，之后恢复公平准则，并研究成功概率p→0时所得无限序列的极限性质与周期性。

Result: 证明初始扰动会根本改变最终行为：除非初始前缀是Thue–Morse序列或其补序列的前缀，否则极限序列最终周期；最小周期为2的幂且至多4^{n+1}，其中n为前缀长度；重复块本身是Thue–Morse序列的前缀。

Conclusion: 初始有限前缀通常破坏原有的收敛到Thue–Morse序列的渐近结构，导致最终周期性，且周期块仍带有Thue–Morse前缀结构；例外情形仅为前缀与Thue–Morse序列或其补序列一致。

Abstract: In the fair dueling game, two players take turns shooting at each other, with the turn order defined by the following fairness criterion: the next shot is assigned to the player who is less likely to have won the duel so far. Cooper and Dutle showed that when both players have the same probability $p$ of a successful shot, the shooting sequence converges to the Thue--Morse sequence as $p \rightarrow 0$. We extend this model by forcing the sequence to begin with an arbitrary finite prefix before reverting to this fairness criterion. We prove that this initial disruption fundamentally alters the sequence's eventual behavior: in the $p \rightarrow 0$ limit, unless the initial prefix is itself a prefix of the Thue--Morse sequence or its complement, the resulting sequence is eventually periodic. Moreover, the least period is a power of 2 that is at most $4^{n+1}$, where $n$ is the length of the prefix, and the repeating block is itself a prefix of the Thue--Morse sequence.

</details>


### [135] [The threshold for fractional clique decompositions of random hypergraphs, via matrix scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00924)
*Tuan Tran*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了超图中分数 $K_r^{(k)}$-分解阈值下的随机稀疏化阈值，其形式为 $p \ge C(\log n/n^{r-k})^{1/(\binom{r}{k}-1)}$，并证明该界在常数因子意义下是紧的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在最小余度约束下，超图经过边独立稀疏化后仍能保持分数团分解性质的概率阈值，并验证图情形中关于阈值阶的猜想。

Method: 采用概率矩阵缩放方法，并结合最小余度与分数分解阈值的条件进行分析。

Result: 证明了对固定 $k\ge2$、$r\ge k+1$ 与 $\varepsilon>0$，任意最小余度至少为 $(\delta^*_{k,r}+\varepsilon)n$ 的 $n$ 顶点 $k$-一致超图，在以概率 $p \ge C(\log n/n^{r-k})^{1/(\binom{r}{k}-1)}$ 独立保留边后，以高概率存在分数 $K_r^{(k)}$-分解；且该 $p$ 的下界在常数因子意义下是最优的。

Conclusion: 该结果为超图分数团分解的随机稀疏化阈值给出了紧阶，推广并确认了图情形的阈值猜想。

Abstract: Let $δ^*_{k,r}$ be the fractional $K_r^{(k)}$-decomposition threshold in minimum codegree. For fixed $k\ge2$, $r\ge k+1$ and $\varepsilon>0$, we prove that every $n$-vertex $k$-uniform hypergraph $G$ with minimum codegree at least $(δ^*_{k,r}+\varepsilon)n$ admits, with high probability, a fractional $K_r^{(k)}$-decomposition after retaining each edge independently with probability $p \ge C(\log n/n^{r-k})^{1/(\binom{r}{k}-1)}$. The bound on $p$ is sharp up to a constant factor, confirming the conjectured threshold order in the graph case. The proof develops a probabilistic matrix-scaling approach.

</details>


### [136] [Zeros and interlacing for multiset Eulerian-Narayana polynomials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00966)
*Philip B. Zhang,Tongyuan Zhao*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 证明了多重集Eulerian–Narayana多项式的实根性猜想（2021年由Lin、Ma、Ma、Zhou提出），包括去除因子t后所有零点的单重性与负性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lin、Ma、Ma、Zhou（2021）提出多重集Eulerian–Narayana多项式的实根性猜想，该问题涉及多项式的实根性、零点单重性与负性等经典组合性质，尚待解决。

Method: 采用森林分解（forest decomposition）与Lagrange反演，导出微分公式及微分算子的一阶因子分解；利用该因子分解分析非恒定gamma系数与有序gamma零点，并通过因子分解获得全非负的系数转移矩阵。

Result: 证明非恒定gamma系数与有序gamma零点均单调不减，并给出由Narayana多项式和Eulerian多项式刻画的相应界；转移矩阵为全非负；相邻列生成多项式的零点严格交错。

Conclusion: 完整证明了多重集Eulerian–Narayana多项式的实根性猜想，并通过算子因子分解统一处理了gamma系数单调性、转移矩阵全非负性与零点交错等一系列性质。

Abstract: We prove the real-rootedness conjecture of Lin, Ma, Ma, and Zhou (2021) for multiset Eulerian-Narayana polynomials, including simplicity and negativity of all zeros after removing the factor t. Using a forest decomposition and Lagrange inversion, we derive a differential formula and a first-order factorization of the differential operator. We use these factors to show that the nonconstant gamma-coefficients and ordered gamma-zeros are nondecreasing, with corresponding bounds given by the Narayana and Eulerian polynomials. The same factorization gives totally nonnegative coefficient transition matrices. We also prove strict interlacing of the zeros of generating polynomials arising from adjacent columns of the transition matrices.

</details>


### [137] [Degree-square Turán problem for two self-converse tournaments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01047)
*Zhuoran Han,Yaojun Chen*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文确定了 $F$ 为传递竞赛图 $TT_r$ 或 5 顶点正则竞赛图 $\text{RT}_5$ 时的极值函数 $\operatorname{ex}_2^+(n,F)$，后者证实了 Iľkovič 的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ai 等人提出：对哪些自反竞赛图 $F$ 可以确定 $\operatorname{ex}_2^+(n,F)$？$TT_r$ 和 $\text{RT}_5$ 均为自反竞赛图，Iľkovič 已解决 $TT_4$ 的情形，本文旨在解决更一般的情形。

Method: 在有向图极值理论框架下，研究避免固定有向图 $F$ 的 $n$ 顶点有向图中出度平方和的极值，针对 $F=TT_r$ 与 $\text{RT}_5$ 分别进行分析与刻画。

Result: 确定了 $F$ 为 $TT_r$ 或 $\text{RT}_5$ 时 $\operatorname{ex}_2^+(n,F)$ 的精确值，其中 $\text{RT}_5$ 情形证实了 Iľkovič 的猜想。

Conclusion: 本文推广了 Iľkovič 关于 $TT_4$ 的结果至一般传递竞赛图，并解决了 $\text{RT}_5$ 情形，推动了自反竞赛图极值问题的研究。

Abstract: For a fixed digraph $F$, let $\operatorname{ex}_2^+(n,F)$ be the maximum of $\sum_{v\in V(D)}d_D^+(v)^2$ over all $n$-vertex $F$-free digraphs. Ai et al. [arXiv:2606.03520, 2026] asked for which self-converse tournament $F$ one can determine $\operatorname{ex}_2^+(n,F)$. Let $TT_r$ denote a transitive tournament on $r$ vertices and $\text{RT}_5$ denote a regular tournament on 5 vertices. Note that both $TT_r$ and $\text{RT}_5$ are self-converse. Iľkovič [arXiv:2609.05042, 2026] determined $\operatorname{ex}_2^+(n,F)$ for $F=TT_4$. In this paper, we determine $\operatorname{ex}_2^+(n,F)$ for $F$ being $TT_r$ or $\text{RT}_5$, and the latter confirms a conjecture due to Iľkovič.

</details>


### [138] [A Hadamard Cube of Order 22](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01065)
*Amin Bahmanian,Sho Suda*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文构造并明确给出了一个22阶的三维Hadamard矩阵，通过列出其22个$22\times22$二维切片来展示该实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维Hadamard矩阵的存在性构造较为困难，此前缺乏22阶的具体实例，作者旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 显式构造法：直接给出一个三阶张量形式的三维Hadamard矩阵，并以22个大小为$22\times22$的二维切片逐一列出其元素。

Result: 成功得到一个22阶三维Hadamard矩阵的具体实例。

Conclusion: 该结果给出了22阶三维Hadamard矩阵的显式构造，为相关存在性研究与构造方法提供了具体例证。

Abstract: We give an explicit three-dimensional Hadamard matrix of order $22$. The example is presented by listing its twenty-two $22\times22$ two-dimensional slices.

</details>


### [139] [Descent Tops and Pinnacles on 2143- and 3421-Avoiding Permutations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01077)
*Yue Dong,Lily Li Liu,Tongyuan Zhao*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 证明了Burstein关于descent-top集合分布猜想中的2143-3421情形，构造出显式双射，并据此推出Dumont第一类排列上2143与3421的Wilf等价性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Burstein猜想涉及descent-top集合在两个avoidance class上的分布关系，其中2143-3421情形此前未被证明；Burstein与Jones还猜想在Dumont第一类排列上2143与3421 Wilf等价。本文旨在解决这两项问题。

Method: 在最大Cartesian树上构造对合，并结合极大递减串末项的交换操作，在两个avoidance class之间建立显式双射；在中间avoidance class上证明交换过程终止且各方向输出与选择无关，由此给出显式逆映射；随后将该双射限制到Dumont第一类排列上。

Result: 双射保持pinnacle set，并反转pinnacle值的从左到右顺序；交换过程可终止且输出唯一，从而得到显式逆；限制到Dumont第一类排列后，证明2143与3421在该类上Wilf等价。

Conclusion: 证明了Burstein猜想的2143-3421情形，并验证了Burstein-Jones关于Dumont第一类排列上2143与3421 Wilf等价的猜想。

Abstract: We prove the 2143-3421 case of Burstein's conjecture on the distribution of descent-top sets by constructing an explicit bijection between the two avoidance classes. The bijection also preserves the pinnacle set and reverses the left-to-right order of the pinnacle values. We combine an involution on maximum Cartesian trees with exchanges of the final entries of maximal decreasing runs. On an intermediate avoidance class, we prove that the exchange procedures terminate and that the output in each direction is independent of the choices made. This gives an explicit inverse. Restricting the bijection to Dumont permutations of the first kind proves the conjecture of Burstein and Jones that 2143 and 3421 are Wilf-equivalent on this class

</details>


### [140] [Upper Bounds on the Turán Density of Hypergraphs Associated with the Projective Plane over a Finite Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01084)
*Subhankar Dash,Kaushik Majumder*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 利用L^p方法与射影平面关联结构，给出了t-页书B_t[PG(2,q)]的Turán密度上界，并改进了PG(2,q)自身的上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定射影平面(q+1)-图及其书形的Turán密度上界，并改进已知结果。

Method: 采用L^p方法，结合有限射影平面PG(2,q)的关联结构进行分析。

Result: 证明B_t[PG(2,q)]的Turán密度上界为(q(q+1)^{-1})^{1/(qt)}；将PG(2,q)的Turán密度上界由1- C(q^2,q)^{-1}改进为(q(q+1)^{-1})^{1/q}。

Conclusion: 该估计一致地改进了射影平面图及其书形的Turán密度上界。

Abstract: Let $\PG(2,q)$ denote the projective plane over the finite field $\mathbb{F}_q$ where $q\geq2$ is a prime power. For a positive integer $t$, let $B_{t}[\PG(2,q)]$ denote the $t$-page book obtained from $t-$many copies of the $(q+1)-$graph $\PG(2,q)$, sharing a common edge. Employing the $\mathsf{L}^p-$method and using the incidence structure of the projective plane, we establish that the upper bound of Turán density of $B_{t}[\PG(2,q)]$ is $(q(q+1)^{-1})^{\frac{1}{qt}}$. For the $(q+1)-$graph $\PG(2,q)$, the previously known upper bound on its Turán density was $1-\binom{q^2}{q}^{-1}$. As a consequence of our estimate, this upper bound is improved to $(q(q+1)^{-1})^{\frac{1}{q}}$.

</details>


### [141] [Perfect matching covers of claw-free cubic graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01137)
*Huaibin Zhou,Xiumei Wang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明Berge猜想在无桥无爪三次图上成立，并给出四个完美匹配覆盖边数下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Berge猜想断言每个无桥三次图可被五个完美匹配覆盖；5-圈双覆盖猜想等价于无桥无爪三次图可被四个完美匹配覆盖。本文旨在无爪情形下推进对Berge猜想的验证。

Method: 针对无桥无爪三次图的结构分析，结合完美匹配与覆盖理论，构造可覆盖大部分边的四个完美匹配。

Result: 证明Berge猜想对无桥无爪三次图成立；每个此类图G存在四个完美匹配覆盖至少299/315|E(G)|条边。

Conclusion: 该结果在无爪三次图类中验证了Berge猜想，并给出了四个完美匹配覆盖比例的定量下界。

Abstract: The Berge conjecture [Proc. London Math. Soc., 1979] asserts that every bridgeless cubic graph can be covered by five perfect matchings. The 5-cycle double cover conjecture, proposed independently by Preissmann (1981) and Celmins (1984), asserts that every bridgeless graph admits five even subgraphs such that every edge belongs to exactly two of them. Hakobyan and Mkrtchyan [Ars Math. Contemp., 2019] proved that the 5-cycle double cover conjecture holds if and only if every bridgeless claw-free cubic graph can be covered by four perfect matchings. In this paper, we prove that the Berge conjecture holds for bridgeless claw-free cubic graphs. Moreover, we show that every bridgeless claw-free cubic graph $G$ admits four perfect matchings that cover at least $\frac{299}{315}|E(G)|$ edges.

</details>


### [142] [A Proof of the Third Borwein Conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01156)
*Yicen Ma*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该论文研究多项式 $S_n(q)=\prod_{j=1}^n\prod_{s=1}^4(1-q^{5j-s})$ 的系数符号，证明了当 $n\ge1750$ 时第三 Borwein 猜想所预测的符号模式成立；结合 $1\le n\le1749$ 的有限验证，得到对所有正整数 $n$ 的猜想符号模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第三 Borwein 猜想关注该类多项式系数的符号模式，长期以来缺乏对所有 $n$ 的完整证明。论文旨在通过解析方法在足够大的 $n$ 上建立符号模式，并用有限算术验证覆盖小度数情形，从而完成整个猜想。

Method: 采用有效的四峰分析（four-peak analysis）估计系数；在合并振幅中保留残数类 $3$ 与 $4$ 的本质抵消，作为精确因子 $e^{-5z}$，导出移位鞍点方程 $d-5n=n^2\beta(t)$；使用两层分拆注入（two-layer partition injection）给出连接大度数分析与小度数的线性边界；所有连续参数估计均带显式常数，其有限算术比较以有理证书形式给出；并描述精确整数验证方法。

Result: 证明了对每个 $n\ge1750$ 及每个系数，第三 Borwein 猜想所预测的符号模式成立；结合作者报告的 $1\le n\le1749$ 有限验证完成，得到对所有正整数 $n$ 的猜想符号模式。补充系数记录覆盖 $1\le n\le500$，报告的全范围计算另行标识。

Conclusion: 解析定理与有限验证相结合，完整验证了第三 Borwein 猜想的符号模式；方法上通过四峰分析、精确抵消因子与移位鞍点方程，以及分拆注入边界，提供了带显式常数和有理证书的可验证证明框架。

Abstract: We study the coefficients of $S_n(q)=\prod_{j=1}^n\prod_{s=1}^4(1-q^{5j-s})$. An effective four-peak analysis gives the sign pattern predicted by the third Borwein conjecture for every $n\ge1750$ and every coefficient. The essential cancellation in residue classes $3$ and $4$ is retained as an exact factor $e^{-5z}$ in the combined amplitude, leading to the shifted saddle point equation $d-5n=n^2β(t)$. A two-layer partition injection provides the linear boundary needed to join this analysis to small degrees. All continuous parameter estimates have explicit constants; their finite arithmetic comparisons are supplied as rational certificates. We also describe exact integer verification. Combining the analytic theorem with the author's reported completion of the finite verification for $1\le n\le1749$ gives the conjectured sign pattern for every positive integer $n$. The available supplementary coefficient record covers $1\le n\le500$; the reported full-range computation is identified separately.

</details>


### [143] [A proof of Lehmer's permutation conjecture for neighbor-swap graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01240)
*Tom Verhoeff*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该文证明了Lehmer (1965)提出的猜想：任意重集的排列在邻接交换图上都存在"非完美哈密顿遍历"，并证明了Verhoeff (2017)重述后的猜想——非utter词图N(S)的哈密顿性（除两个例外族），从而完整解决Knuth《计算机程序设计艺术》中悬而未决的研究问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lehmer (1965)猜想每个重集的排列都可通过相邻交换实现非完美哈密顿遍历；该问题被Knuth列为未解研究问题。Verhoeff (2017)将其重构为N(S)图（非stutter词）的哈密顿性问题，并指出两个例外族：奇数重数的二元签名和(2k,1,1)的排列只有哈密顿路径而无回路。本文旨在证明这一重构猜想，进而证明Lehmer原猜想。

Method: 核心结构是将所有词按"多米诺内容"划分为若干超立方体类：多米诺内部的交换使同一多米诺内容的词类构成一个超立方体，而stutter词恰好是0维类。当所有重数均为偶数时，通过沿生成树粘合各超立方体的哈密顿回路，无需有限检查。恰好一个奇数重数的情形归约到全偶情形与Stachowiak (1992)定理；两个及以上奇数重数也由该定理处理。唯一的有限构件是两个显式回路（28词与84词）。所有构造用Python实现并与暴力图对照验证，证明在Lean 4上基于Mathlib形式化。

Result: 证明了Verhoeff重述的猜想，从而证明了Lehmer猜想本身。证明结构上以超立方体划分为核心，多数情形通过生成树粘合或归约处理，不依赖穷举；仅需两个显式小回路（28词与84词）作为有限构件。实现经Python暴力验证，形式化于Lean 4/Mathlib。

Conclusion: 该文完全解决了Lehmer的邻接交换遍历猜想这一长期未解问题，给出了统一的构造性证明框架，并辅以计算验证与机器形式化，验证了Verhoeff重构猜想的正确性及两个例外族的刻画。

Abstract: In 1965, D. H. Lehmer conjectured that the permutations of every multiset admit an imperfect Hamiltonian traversal by adjacent swaps: a walk in the neighbor-swap graph that visits every word, with some words visited twice in order to reach a neighbor and return. The question is posed as an unsolved research problem in Knuth's Art of Computer Programming. Verhoeff (2017) chose the stutter words, in which every domino is a double, as the words to be reached this way, and reformulated the conjecture as the Hamiltonicity of the graph $N(S)$ on the non-stutter words, with two exceptional families --- binary signatures with an odd multiplicity, and the permutations of $(2k,1,1)$ --- that admit a Hamiltonian path but no cycle. This article proves the reformulated conjecture, and with it Lehmer's conjecture. The key structure is a partition of the words into hypercubes: the swaps inside dominoes turn each class of words with the same domino contents into a hypercube, and the stutters are exactly the $0$-dimensional classes. When every multiplicity is even, Hamiltonian cycles of the hypercubes are glued along a spanning tree, with no finite check. The case of exactly one odd multiplicity reduces to the all-even case and to a theorem of Stachowiak (1992), the one inherited Hamiltonicity input, which also settles two or more odd multiplicities. The only finite ingredients are two explicit cycles, of 28 and 84 words. Every construction is implemented in Python and checked against brute-force graphs, and the proof is formalized in Lean 4 over Mathlib.

</details>


### [144] [A unified framework for existing and new constructions of Neumaier graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01273)
*Aida Abiad,Wouter Castryck,Maarten De Boeck,Antonina Khramova,Thijs van Veluw*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文利用有限环给出Neumaier图的统一构造，得到三个含无穷多严格Neumaier图的新族，并刻画其强正则性条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Neumaier图（含正则团的非完全边正则图）中严格Neumaier图（非强正则）的构造与分类是代数图论的重要问题，已有结果零散，需要一个统一的构造框架来整合并推广。

Method: 基于有限环构造图，结合范数函数、有限域扩张以及分圆数（cyclotomic numbers）等工具，分析所得图的正则团与边正则性质，并刻画其何时成为强正则图。

Result: 构造统一了若干已知结果并给出三个含无穷多严格Neumaier图的新族；来自有限域扩张范数函数的族包含由经典hyperoval产生的*-Tits型广义四边形共线图；当分圆数阶至多为七时，四类环即可在同构意义下得到该构造的所有严格Neumaier图，且这四类环均已用于构造Neumaier图。

Conclusion: 有限环框架为严格Neumaier图提供了统一且富有成效的构造途径，深化了对其结构与分类的理解。

Abstract: A Neumaier graph is a non-complete edge-regular graph containing a regular clique; it is called strictly Neumaier if it is not strongly regular. In this paper we present a construction using finite rings that unifies several known results and yields three new families, each containing infinitely many strictly Neumaier graphs. We characterize when the resulting graphs are strongly regular. The family obtained from norm functions of finite field extensions includes the collinearity graphs of generalized quadrangles of \(*\)-Tits type arising from the classical hyperoval. We further show that, when the cyclotomic numbers involved have order at most seven, four types of rings suffice to obtain, up to isomorphism, all strictly Neumaier graphs arising from our construction. As a consequence of our results, each of these four types has now been used to construct Neumaier graphs.

</details>


### [145] [Intersecting integer partitions: star bounds and counterexamples at every scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01747)
*Yury Person,Thomas Schweser*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究整数分拆的t-相交族，证明在n≥Ak³时规范t-星形最大，并给出Borg猜想在各相交尺度上的反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 整数分拆的t-相交极值问题类比集合的相交族理论，但相关结论尚不完整，尤其Borg猜想是否成立未知，需要确定最大t-相交族的结构与阈值条件。

Method: 将分拆问题转化为集合框架，应用Kupavskii的弱散布定理（weak-spread theorem）进行分析。

Result: 当n≥Ak³（A>24固定，k充分大，1≤t<k一致）时，由至少含t个1的分拆构成的规范t-星形是最大t-相交族；同时，对充分大的k和每个1≤d<k，取d/4≤t≤d及n=⌊tk²/3⌋，可构造严格大于规范星形的t-相交族，从而在所有相交尺度上反驳Borg猜想。

Conclusion: 规范t-星形在大n（n≥Ak³）条件下最优，但Borg猜想一般不成立，存在跨越所有相交尺度的反例。

Abstract: Two integer partitions $t$-intersect if they have at least $t$ common parts, counted with multiplicity. We study the largest $t$-intersecting families of integer partitions of $n$ into exactly $k$ positive parts. The canonical $t$-star consists of the partitions containing at least $t$ ones. Applying Kupavskii's weak-spread theorem, we prove that this star is largest whenever $n\ge Ak^3$, for every fixed $A>24$ and all sufficiently large $k$, uniformly over $1\le t<k$. We also give counterexamples to Borg's conjecture at every intersection scale: for all sufficiently large $k$ and for every $1\le d<k$, one may choose $d/4\le t\le d$ and $n=\lfloor tk^2/3\rfloor$ so that a $t$-intersecting family is strictly larger than the canonical star.

</details>


### [146] [Automorphisms of pentagonal geometries with block size 3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01390)
*Grahame Erskine,Terry S. Griggs*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究 k=3 的五边形几何 PENT(3,r) 的自同构，确定具有循环或反向自同构的谱为 r ≡ 1 或 10 (mod 12)，并发现 584 个两两不同构的 PENT(3,7) 并按自同构群阶进行分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 五边形几何 PENT(k,r) 的存在谱已知，但其自同构问题尚未被研究。作者希望在 k=3 的情形下开启对这类结构自同构的系统研究。

Method: 以 k-uniform、r-regular 的偏线性空间为框架，定义 PENT(k,r) 并要求与给定点不共线的点集构成一条线。针对 k=3，考察循环自同构与反向自同构的存在条件，并借助计算/枚举手段对 PENT(3,7) 进行同构判定与自同构群阶分类。

Result: 具有循环或反向自同构的 PENT(3,r) 的谱为 r = 1 或 10 (mod 12)；同时给出 584 个两两不同构的 PENT(3,7)，并按自同构群阶完成分类。

Conclusion: k=3 时五边形几何的自同构谱被完整刻画为 r ≡ 1,10 (mod 12)，且 PENT(3,7) 被枚举并分类，为后续一般 k 的自同构研究奠定基础。

Abstract: A pentagonal geometry PENT(k,r) is a k-uniform, r-regular partial linear space with the property that the set of points not collinear with a given point x is itself a line in the geometry. The existence spectrum of these geometries is known; we begin the study of automorphisms of these structures in the case k=3. We determine the spectrum of pentagonal geometries PENT(3,r) with either a cyclic or reverse automorphism to be r = 1 or 10 (mod 12). In addition, we show that there are 584 pairwise non-isomorphic PENT(3,7)s and classify them by the orders of their automorphism groups.

</details>


### [147] [Recurrent two-dimensional sequences over three and four letters simulating von Neumann constructions of finite ordinals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01753)
*Xiaofang Jiang,Mihai Prunescu,Bogdan Dumitru*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 研究三类递归双序列，其着色图编码有限序数构造的镜像版本，显式描述单元格、矩形嵌套与盒子装配规则，并证明其非k-自动性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对有限序数构造（两种初始符号变体及从单一初始符号出发的冯·诺依曼构造）的镜像版本进行统一、显式的组合刻画，并探讨其序列的自动性。

Method: 采用递归双序列与着色图表示：三字母序列实现两初始符号与三初始符号变体，四字母序列实现单初始符号的冯·诺依曼构造；显式刻画单元格并确定矩形的有序嵌套。四字母情形区分普通盒与虚拟盒并证明装配规则；证明沿反对角线用归纳法进行；对一初始符号与三初始符号的构造，将递归归约为有限局部构型集合并用计算机验证。

Result: 获得嵌套盒子数量的公式（含算术项表示）；证明这三类序列对任意整数k≥2均不是k-自动的。

Conclusion: 三类递归双序列分别对应有限序数构造的镜像版本，其盒子嵌套具有可刻画的结构与计数公式，且均不具有自动性。

Abstract: We study three recurrent double sequences whose colored diagrams encode mirrored versions of the finite-ordinal construction. Two sequences use three letters and realize variants with two and three initial symbols. A four-letter sequence realizes the von Neumann construction from one initial symbol. For each sequence we describe its cells explicitly and identify the ordered nesting of its rectangles. For four letters, we distinguish ordinary and virtual boxes and prove their assembly rules. The proofs proceed by induction along antidiagonals. For the constructions with one and three initial symbols, we reduce the recurrence to finite sets of local configurations and verify those sets by computer. We derive formulas for the numbers of nested boxes, including arithmetic-term representations, and we prove that none of the three sequences is $k$-automatic for any integer $k \geq 2$.

</details>


### [148] [An improved lower and upper bound of the k-limited domination number](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01442)
*Gordana Radić*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文延续 $k$-limited domination（受限支配）研究，利用 $k$-capacitated domination 改进 $\gamma_k^L(G)$ 的一般下界，并刻画达到 $\lceil n/(k+1)\rceil$ 的图；同时在 $k<\delta(G)$ 条件下用 $k$-limited packing number 精化上界，刻画达到 $n-k$ 的图，统一并推广了 $k=1$ 的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典支配允许支配集顶点支配任意多外部顶点，缺乏容量约束。$k$-limited domination 为每个支配顶点施加最多支配 $k$ 个集合外顶点的限制，但此前仅对特例 $k=1$ 有较完整结论，一般 $k$ 的下界、上界及极值图刻画尚不充分。本文旨在推广与统一这些结果。

Method: 引入并使用 $k$-capacitated domination 概念改进 $k$-limited domination number $\gamma_k^L(G)$ 的一般下界；借助 $k$-limited packing number，在 $k<\delta(G)$ 条件下精化已知上界。

Result: 改进了 $\gamma_k^L(G)$ 的一般下界；刻画了满足 $\gamma_k^L(G)=\lceil n/(k+1)\rceil$ 的图；在 $k<\delta(G)$ 时精化了上界，并刻画了满足 $\gamma_k^L(G)=n-k$ 的图。

Conclusion: 结果推广并统一了 $k=1$ 情形的既有研究，在所考虑的假设下给出了 $k$-limited domination number 达到极值的图的完整刻画。

Abstract: We continue the study of $k$-limited domination in graphs, a domination variant in which each vertex of a dominating set may dominate at most $k$ vertices outside the set. This concept extends classical domination by incorporating capacity constraints on dominating vertices.
  We improve the general lower bound for the $k$-limited domination number $γ_k^L(G)$ by employing the concept of $k$-capacitated domination. As a consequence, we characterize graphs satisfying $γ_k^L(G)=\lceil \frac{n}{k+1} \rceil$. We also refine the known upper bound for graphs with $k<δ(G)$ using the $k$-limited packing number. Under this condition, we describe graphs attaining $γ_k^L(G)=n-k$. These results extend and unify previous investigations for the case $k=1$ and provide a complete characterization of graphs attaining the extreme values of the $k$-limited domination number under the considered assumptions.

</details>


### [149] [Tight degeneracy bounds in online Ramsey games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01516)
*Wen Chen,Qizhong Lin,Shixi Song*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了在q色在线Ramsey游戏中，对于任意q≥2和d≥1，Builder能够迫使一个单色d-退化图H出现，同时绘制的图退化度至多为d，且该界是紧的，正面解决了Conlon、Fox和Sudakov提出的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线Ramsey游戏中，Builder需要迫使单色目标图H出现，但希望其绘制的辅助图尽可能稀疏。此前的开放问题关注：对d-退化图H，Builder能否在保持绘制图退化度不超过d的条件下获胜。

Method: 利用在线Ramsey游戏框架，针对d-退化图H设计Builder策略，使得每步绘制边所形成的图退化度控制在d以内，从而迫使Painter产生单色H副本。

Result: 对任意q≥2和d≥1，Builder能以绘制图退化度至多d的方式迫使任意d-退化图H的单色副本出现；且界d是紧的。

Conclusion: 该工作正面解决了Conlon、Fox和Sudakov提出的问题，给出了退化度意义上的最优上界，完善了对在线Ramsey游戏中目标图稀疏性与绘制图稀疏性关系的理解。

Abstract: In the $q$-color online Ramsey game, Builder and Painter play on an infinite independent set of vertices. At each step, Builder draws an edge and Painter immediately assigns it one of $q$ colors. Builder aims to force a monochromatic copy of a fixed graph $H$. We prove that, for every $q \ge 2$ and $d \ge 1$, Builder can force a monochromatic copy of any $d$-degenerate graph $H$ while drawing a graph of degeneracy at most $d$. The bound $d$ is tight, and this resolves in the affirmative a problem of Conlon, Fox and Sudakov.

</details>


### [150] [On $k$-limited domination: complexity and Sierpiński graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01584)
*Dragana Božović*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了k-Limited Dominating Set问题的NP完全性，并确定了Sierpiński图的k-limited支配数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回答文献中关于1-Limited Dominating Set问题计算复杂性的开放问题，并研究Sierpiński图的k-limited支配数。

Method: 通过NP完全性归约证明1-Limited Dominating Set及一般k-Limited Dominating Set（即使在平面图上）的NP完全性；对Sierpiński图S(n,m)进行组合分析，确定其k-limited支配数的精确值。

Result: 1-Limited Dominating Set问题为NP完全，回答了开放问题；对任意正整数k，k-Limited Dominating Set问题即使在平面图上仍为NP完全；确定了所有n≥1, m≥2时S(n,m)的γ_1^L精确值，以及平面族S(n,3)的k-limited支配数。

Conclusion: k-limited支配问题的判定版本在一般图及平面图上均为NP完全，但在Sierpiński图等特定图族上可得到支配数的精确表达，体现了该参数在复杂性上的难解性与在结构化图上的可计算性。

Abstract: A dominating set $D$ of a graph $G$ is called $k$-limited if every vertex of $D$ has at most $k$ neighbors outside $D$. The minimum cardinality among all $k$-limited dominating sets of $G$ is the $k$-limited domination number, denoted by $γ_k^L(G)$.
  In this paper, we prove that the $1$-Limited Dominating Set problem is $\mathsf{NP}$-complete, answering an open question posed in the literature. We further show that, for every positive integer $k$, the $k$-Limited Dominating Set problem is $\mathsf{NP}$-complete even when restricted to planar graphs. In addition, we study the $k$-limited domination number of Sierpiński graphs. We determine the exact value of $γ_1^L(S(n,m))$ for all integers $n\ge 1$ and $m\ge 2$, and obtain the $k$-limited domination number for the planar family $S(n,3)$.

</details>


### [151] [A negative Schur coefficient for products of two chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01617)
*Kai Zhang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 证明了链乘积 m×n 在 n≥4 且 m≥3n-1 时不是 Schur 正的，并显式给出一个负系数；结合已有结果覆盖 n≥3, m≥n+5 及 n=2, m≥8 的情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 判定链乘积（即矩形格上的偏序集）的 Schur 正性是一个组合表示论问题。此前仅知宽度为二、三的情形，对一般宽度的链乘积，其 Schur 正性边界尚不清楚，需要给出否定性的构造与判据。

Method: 改进 Li、Qiu、Yang、Zhang 的链划分方法：利用 rank capacity 论证强制产生 n-2 条长链，并将剩余三条链的计数转化为“固定行坐标的中间区间”与两个有界边界区域的分离计数，从而定位并计算一个具体负系数的值。

Result: 当 n≥4 且 m≥3n-1 时，m×n 不是 Schur 正的；写 m=n+k，负系数以 (2n+k-1, 2n+k-3, …, k+5, k, k, 4) 为指标，其值等于 (n-2)! 乘以关于 k 的三次多项式。

Conclusion: 结合该论文的定理以及宽度二、三的已知情形，链乘积 m×n 在 n≥3, m≥n+5 以及 n=2, m≥8 时均不是 Schur 正的，明确了这些乘积失去 Schur 正性的范围。

Abstract: We prove that the product of chains $\mathbf{m}\times\mathbf{n}$ is not Schur positive whenever $n\ge4$ and $m\ge3n-1$. Writing $m=n+k$, we exhibit a negative coefficient indexed by $(2n+k-1,2n+k-3,\ldots,k+5,k,k,4)$ and evaluate it explicitly as $(n-2)!$ times a polynomial of degree three in $k$. Our argument refines the chain-partition method of Li, Qiu, Yang, and Zhang: rank capacity forces $n-2$ long chains, and the remaining three chains are counted by separating a middle interval with fixed row coordinates from two bounded boundary regions. Together with their theorem and the known cases of widths two and three, this shows that these products are not Schur positive for $n\ge3$, $m\ge n+5$, and for $n=2$, $m\ge8$.

</details>


### [152] [$K$-$k$-Schur functions are $k$-Schur positive](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01708)
*Haojun Bai,Peter L. Guo*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 证明了每个K-k-Schur函数在k-Schur函数基底下具有非负整数线性组合展开，从而解决了Lam-Schilling-Shimozono猜想，证明依赖于将k-Schur与K-k-Schur函数分别实现为Catalan函数与Katalan函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: k-Schur函数与K-k-Schur函数分别是A型仿射Grassmannian同调与K-同调中Schubert类的对称函数代表。Lam、Schilling和Shimozono猜想每个K-k-Schur函数在k-Schur函数基下具有非负整数系数展开，这一正性猜想是该领域的核心未决问题。

Method: 证明的关键在于将k-Schur函数实现为Catalan函数，将K-k-Schur函数实现为Katalan函数，并借助这两类函数之间的组合关系建立非负展开。

Result: 证明了每个K-k-Schur函数都是k-Schur函数基下的非负整数线性组合，即该展开的所有系数均为非负整数。

Conclusion: 该结果证实了Lam-Schilling-Shimozono猜想，并表明Catalan函数与Katalan函数的实现是研究k-Schur理论与K-同调正性问题的有效工具。

Abstract: The $k$-Schur functions (resp., $K$-$k$-Schur functions) are the symmetric function representatives of Schubert classes in the homology (resp., $K$-homology) of the affine Grassmannian of type $A$. We prove that every $K$-$k$-Schur function is a nonnegative integer linear combination in the basis of $k$-Schur functions. This settles a conjecture by Lam, Schilling and Shimozono. The proof relies on realizations of $k$-Schur and $K$-$k$-Schur functions as Catalan and Katalan functions, respectively.

</details>


### [153] [Cheeger constants and bipartiteness ratios of Cayley graphons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01745)
*Mugdha Mahesh Pokharanakar*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 在一定假设下，连通Cayley图子（Cayley graphon）的Cheeger常数可由其二分比（bipartiteness ratio）以及其Laplacian谱顶端与2之间的间隙从上方界定；由此证明：若连通Cayley图子C(G,γ)是二分图，则存在G的指数为2的子群H，使得H与γ的支集不相交。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Cayley图子这一图极限/加权图对象的谱与割性质之间的关系，特别是Cheeger常数（等周/扩张性质）与二分性度量之间的联系，并将其用于刻画Cayley图子的二分结构。

Method: 在特定假设下，建立Cheeger常数与二分比的上界不等式，以及与Laplacian谱最大特征值与2之差的界；随后利用该不等式推导二分Cayley图子的群论结构结论。

Result: 得到了Cheeger常数的两个上界：一个由二分比控制，另一个由Laplacian谱顶端与2的谱间隙控制；并证明二分连通Cayley图子C(G,γ)必存在指数为2的子群H，其与γ的支集不相交。

Conclusion: 该工作将图的Cheeger型不等式推广到Cayley图子，并给出二分性的群论刻画：二分性对应于存在指数2子群避开生成权重支集，从而沟通了谱间隙、等周常数与群结构。

Abstract: We show, under a certain assumption, that the Cheeger constant of a connected Cayley graphon is bounded from above in terms of its bipartiteness ratio, and also in terms of the gap between the top of the spectrum of its Laplacian and $2$. Using this result, we prove that if a connected Cayley graphon $C(G,γ)$ is bipartite, then there exists an index two subgroup $H$ of $G$ that avoids the support of $γ$.

</details>


### [154] [On $1$-limited and $(1,2)$-domination in cubic graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01796)
*Goran Radić,Aleksandra Tepeh*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了1-Limited Dominating Set问题即使限制在2-连通平面三次图上仍是NP-完全的，从而补全了k-Limited Dominating Set对所有固定正整数k的复杂度结果，并确定了整个Goldberg族的1-limited支配数，为若干(1,2)-支配与诱导环的猜想和界提供了无限族三次图证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: k-Limited Dominating Set对所有固定正整数k的复杂度结果尚不完整，且(1,2)-支配与诱导环存在若干公开猜想和界缺乏构造性证据，尤其在三次图这一重要图类上。

Method: 通过归约证明1-Limited Dominating Set在2-连通平面三次图上为NP-完全；并对Goldberg族三次图进行结构分析，精确计算其1-limited支配数。

Result: 1-Limited Dominating Set在2-连通平面三次图上NP-完全，补全了k-Limited Dominating Set的复杂度图谱；得到Goldberg族全部图的精确1-limited支配数。

Conclusion: 该工作完成了k-Limited Dominating Set的复杂度刻画，并提供了支持(1,2)-支配及诱导环相关猜想与界的无限族三次图。

Abstract: A dominating set $D$ of a graph is called $1$-limited if every vertex of $D$ has at most one neighbor outside $D$, while a $(1,2)$-dominating set is a dominating set in which every vertex of the set has at least two neighbors within the set. These two notions coincide on cubic graphs. We prove that the decision problem 1-Limited Dominating Set is $\mathsf{NP}$-complete even when restricted to $2$-connected planar cubic graphs, thereby completing the known complexity results for $k$-Limited Dominating Set for all fixed positive integers $k$. We also determine the exact $1$-limited domination number of the entire Goldberg family. This provides a further infinite family of cubic graphs supporting several open conjectures and proposed bounds concerning $(1,2)$-domination and induced cycles.

</details>


### [155] [Matroid liftability and Oxley--Wang derived matroids, with connections to scene analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01854)
*Sean Dewar,Emiliano Liwski,Signe Lundqvist,Fatemeh Mohammadi*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文将liftability概念从paving matroids推广到具有可实化截断的matroids，引入generic realisability概念，建立基于Oxley-Wang derived matroids的统一框架，并应用于paving matroids的刻画及Whiteley场景分析理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Liftability最初仅用于paving matroids，用于系统构造matroid variety理想中的多项式。作者希望将其推广到更一般的matroids（具有可实化截断者），并寻找一个统一框架来研究liftability及相关的realisability性质，同时与Whiteley的场景分析理论建立联系。

Method: 将liftability推广到具有可实化截断的matroids；引入generic realisability（同时蕴含realisability与liftability）；研究liftability与generic realisability在固定截断的matroid格中weak order下的行为；利用generic matroid实化的Oxley-Wang derived matroid建立统一框架，对liftability与generic realisability进行刻画；用matroid实化空间的coordinate sections对generic Oxley-Wang derived matroids的基给出代数描述；并与matroids的slack realisation spaces建立联系。

Result: 给出了liftability与generic realisability基于Oxley-Wang derived matroids的刻画；给出了generic Oxley-Wang derived matroids基的代数描述；得到了liftable与generically realisable paving matroids的组合刻画；通过Oxley-Wang derived matroids给出了Whiteley关于允许非平凡（sharp）scenes的超图刻画的新证明；计算了一类特殊sparse paving matroids的generic Oxley-Wang derived matroid。

Conclusion: 该框架将liftability研究统一到Oxley-Wang derived matroids之下，推广了原有paving matroids的结果，并为Whiteley场景分析理论提供了新视角与证明，同时刻画了一类sparse paving matroids。

Abstract: Liftability was first introduced for paving matroids, where it was used to systematically construct polynomials in the ideals of the associated matroid varieties. In this paper, we generalise the notion of liftability to matroids with realisable truncations. We additionally introduce the notion of generic realisability of a matroid; a strong notion of realisability that implies both realisability and liftability of the matroid. We study the behaviour of liftability and generic realisability under the weak order of matroids within the lattice of matroids with fixed truncation. We develop a unified framework for studying liftability of matroids through Oxley--Wang derived matroid of generic matroid realisations. We characterise liftability and generic realisability in terms of Oxley--Wang derived matroids. Moreover, we give an algebraic description of the bases of the generic Oxley--Wang derived matroids in terms of coordinate sections of matroid realisation spaces and relate our constructions to slack realisation spaces of matroids.
  As an application of our framework, we obtain a combinatorial characterisation of liftable and generically realisable paving matroids and connect it with Whiteley's theory of scene analysis. In particular, our approach gives a new proof, via Oxley--Wang derived matroids, of Whiteley's characterisation of hypergraphs admitting non-trivial (sharp) scenes. Finally, we compute the generic Oxley--Wang derived matroid for a special class of sparse paving matroids.

</details>


### [156] [An explicit basis for the extended Catalan arrangement of type Dl](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01866)
*Weili Guo,Yupeng Wang,Zixuan Wang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文受Kawanoue基于有限和公式的构造启发，为Dl型扩展Catalan配置锥的导子模构造了一个显式齐次基。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受Kawanoue利用有限和公式构造导子模的启发，并希望揭示Feigin-Wang-Yoshinaga所构造整基中参数选择的几何动机。

Method: 采用有限和公式（finite-sum formulas）构造Dl型扩展Catalan配置锥导子模的显式齐次基，并考察该基在无穷远处的限制。

Result: 所构造的基在无穷远处的限制与Feigin-Wang-Yoshinaga的整基在相差非零常数意义下一致。

Conclusion: 该结果统一了两种构造，揭示了Feigin-Wang-Yoshinaga整基参数选择背后的几何动机。
