Abstract: Motivated by Kawanoue's construction with the finite-sum formulas, we construct an explicit homogeneous basis for the derivation module of the cone of the extended Catalan arrangement of type Dl. We further show that the restriction of this basis at infinity coincides, up to a nonzero constant, with the integral basis constructed by Feigin-Wang-Yoshinaga. This identifies the geometric motivation behind the parameter choices.

</details>


### [157] [Balanced weightings and permutation matchings applied to the monoid of order-preserving mappings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01912)
*Peter M. Higgins*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文给出有限半群组合D-类具有置换匹配的充要条件：其结构矩阵存在平衡加权（行和、列和分别恒定的非负权重），并据此证明n元链上保序自映射幺半群具有置换匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有结果依赖正则性假设来保证置换匹配的存在，作者希望弱化这一假设，寻找更一般的充要刻画，并推广到具体的重要幺半群（保序自映射幺半群）。

Method: 将D-类的置换匹配问题转化为其结构矩阵的平衡加权存在性问题；用Birkhoff–von Neumann定理替代Hall引理作为核心工具，得到充要条件，再将结论应用于n元链的保序自映射幺半群。

Result: 证明了D-类具有置换匹配当且仅当其结构矩阵容许平衡加权；由此推出n元链上全体保序自映射构成的幺半群具有置换匹配。

Conclusion: 以平衡加权刻画置换匹配是有效且更弱的条件，Birkhoff–von Neumann定理可替代Hall引理，该框架能覆盖非正则情形并适用于保序自映射幺半群。

Abstract: We give a necessary and sufficient condition for a combinatorial D-class of a finite semigroup to have a permutation matching: that its structure matrix admit a balanced weighting, that is, non-negative weights with constant row sums and constant column sums. This weakens the regularity hypothesis, the role of Hall's lemma being taken up by the Birkhoff--von Neumann theorem. We then apply it to show that the monoid of all order-preserving self-maps of an n-element chain, has a permutation matching.

</details>


### [158] [Improved upper bounds on the list chromatic number of $K_t$-minor-free graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01946)
*Yangyan Gu,Rongxing Xu*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了每个 $K_t$-minor-free 图是 $O(t\log\log t)$-choosable，改进此前 $O(t(\log\log t)^6)$ 的界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 是否每个 $K_t$-minor-free 图都是 $O(t)$-choosable 仍是公开问题。此前 Postle 给出 $O(t(\log\log t)^6)$ 的界，Delcourt 与 Postle 指出结合他们关于色数 $O(t\log\log t)$ 的方法可得到 $O(t(\log\log t)^2)$ 的选择数界，本文旨在改进这一界。

Method: 先证明每个 $n$ 个顶点的 $K_t$-minor-free 图的 choice number 为 $O(t\log(2+n/t))$，然后以此为核心工具，沿 Delcourt 与 Postle 提出的思路进行论证。

Result: 得到 $K_t$-minor-free 图的 choice number 上界 $O(t\log\log t)$。

Conclusion: 每个 $K_t$-minor-free 图是 $O(t\log\log t)$-choosable，将已知上界从 $O(t(\log\log t)^2)$ 进一步改进至 $O(t\log\log t)$。

Abstract: It remains open whether every $K_t$-minor-free graph is $O(t)$-choosable. Postle proved that every $K_t$-minor-free graph has choice number $O(t(\log\log t)^6)$. At the end of an earlier version of a paper establishing an $O(t\log\log t)$ bound on the chromatic number of $K_t$-minor-free graphs, Delcourt and Postle remarked that their methods, combined with Postle's earlier techniques, yield an $O(t(\log\log t)^2)$ bound on the choice number. In this paper, we first prove that every $n$-vertex $K_t$-minor-free graph has choice number $O(t\log(2+n/t))$. Using this bound as a key ingredient, we follow the approach outlined by Delcourt and Postle to prove that every $K_t$-minor-free graph is $O(t\log\log t)$-choosable.

</details>


### [159] [Matchings and shape-Wilf-Equivalence of sets of patterns of length three II: Quadruples and Quintuples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01996)
*Sucharita Biswas,Umesh Shankar,Sivaramakrishnan Sivasubramanian*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文在已有三元模式分类基础上，完成了四元组和五元组形状-Wilf等价类的分类，并引入Dyck路方法研究含阶梯板的Ferrers板，给出避免横截数的显式公式及匹配计数的双射解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前作已完成长度三的三元模式形状-Wilf等价分类，但四元组、五元组模式集具有三元编码方法无法捕捉的结构特征，需要新的组合工具；同时需补全长度三模式子集的完整分类（除空集与全集）。

Method: 采用含阶梯板的Ferrers板的Dyck路方法，在关联Dyck路上引入区分单元及相关结构参数；结合Bloom–Elizalde的保模式双射研究对应的避免完美匹配。

Result: 获得新的形状-Wilf等价关系，并对若干类给出固定Ferrers板上避免横截数的显式公式；对匹配计数序列导出递推关系，并给出大Dyck路、Schröder路和Dyck路上的双射解释。

Conclusion: 与三元模式姊妹篇一起，完成了长度三模式子集（除平凡的空集与全集外）形状-Wilf等价类的完整分类，并揭示了分类之外的额外组合结构。

Abstract: Building on our classification of shape-Wilf-equivalence classes for triples of patterns of length three, we complete the classification for quadruples and quintuples. The larger pattern sets exhibit structural features that are not captured by the encoding methods used for triples and require additional combinatorial tools. Our main new ingredient is a Dyck-path approach to Ferrers boards containing the staircase board. By introducing distinguished cells and related structural parameters on the associated Dyck paths, we obtain new shape-Wilf-equivalences and, for several classes, explicit formulas for the number of avoiding transversals on a fixed Ferrers board. We further study the corresponding pattern-avoiding perfect matchings through the pattern-preserving bijection of Bloom and Elizalde. Besides deriving recurrence relations, we give bijective interpretations of several matching enumeration sequences in terms of grand Dyck paths, Schröder paths, and Dyck paths. These constructions provide additional combinatorial structure beyond the classification itself. Together with the companion paper on triples, the present work completes the classification of shape-Wilf-equivalence classes for subsets of patterns of length three, apart from the trivial empty and full pattern sets.

</details>


### [160] [Linear circumference in vertex-transitive graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02053)
*Jie Ma,Ziyuan Zhao*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该论文证明每个连通点传递图包含长度至少为 cn 的环（c 为绝对常数），并对大度数情形给出接近哈密顿的线性下界 (1-d^{-1/100})n。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lovász 的哈密顿性猜想断言每个连通点传递图含有哈密顿环，但长期未被解决。本文旨在朝此猜想迈出第一步，给出首个线性长度的环下界。

Method: 证明结合了 DeVos 与 Mohar 关于点传递图的结构性结果，以及一个用于寻找长环的通用框架，该框架本身可能具有独立价值。

Result: 证明了存在绝对常数 c>0，使任意 n≥3 个顶点的连通点传递图含长度至少 cn 的环；且度数 d 足够大时，含长度至少 (1-d^{-1/100})n 的环。

Conclusion: 这是 Towards Lovász 哈密顿性猜想的首个线性界结果，为后续研究提供了基础与工具。

Abstract: We prove that there is an absolute constant $c>0$ such that every connected vertex-transitive graph $G$ on $n \ge 3$ vertices contains a cycle of length at least $cn$. Moreover, every such graph with sufficiently large degree $d$ contains a cycle of length at least $(1-d^{-1/100})n$. This gives the first linear bound towards Lovász's Hamiltonicity conjecture. The proof combines a structural result of DeVos and Mohar on vertex-transitive graphs with a general framework for finding long cycles, which may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [161] [Linear arboricity conjecture for infinite graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02065)
*Leandro Aurichi,Rodrigo Santos Monteiro,Caio Fernando Rodrigues*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 将线性荫度猜想推广到有限最大度的无限图，证明有限与无限版本等价，并引入拓扑线性荫度概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性荫度猜想原仅针对有限图，作者希望将其推广至无限图并建立有限与无限情形的联系，同时通过拓扑约束研究更精细的分解性质。

Method: 定义线性荫度 $\la(G)$ 为将边集分解为最大度不超过2的线性森林的最少个数；将线性荫度猜想推广至有限最大度无限图；引入拓扑线性荫度 $\latop(G)$，要求线性森林不含拓扑圆；并研究其与线性荫度的差异。

Result: 证明线性荫度猜想的有限版本与无限版本等价；证明拓扑线性荫度与线性荫度相差至多1；证明每个围长至少为 $2k$ 的 $2k$-正则图其拓扑线性荫度至多为 $k+1$。

Conclusion: 通过有限-无限等价性将线性荫度猜想统一处理，并通过拓扑线性荫度在围长条件下给出了紧致的上界估计。

Abstract: The linear arboricity $\la(G)$ of a graph $G$ is the least cardinality of linear forests, that is, forests of maximum degree at most $2$, into which its edge set $E(G)$ can be decomposed. The Linear Arboricity Conjecture asserts that $\la(G)\leq\lceil(Δ(G)+1)/2\rceil$ for every finite graph $G$, where $Δ(G)$ denotes the maximum degree of $G$. We extend this conjecture to infinite graphs of finite maximum degree and prove that its finite and infinite versions are equivalent. We introduce topological linear arboricity $\latop (G)$ by requiring the linear forests to contain no topological circle, and show that it differs from linear arboricity by at most one. Finally, we prove that every $2k$-regular graph of girth at least $2k$ has topological linear arboricity at most $k+1$.

</details>


### [162] [Typical intersecting families at $n=2k+1$ and $n=2k+2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02119)
*Lina Li*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文确定了 $2k+1$ 与 $2k+2$ 元集合上 $k$-一致相交族的典型结构：$n=2k+2$ 时几乎所有相交族平凡并给出计数渐近式，$n=2k+1$ 时几乎都非平凡且接近满星，并给出带显式二阶项的计数公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决 Balogh、Garcia、Li 与 Wagner 关于 $k$-一致相交族典型结构的猜想，并补充 Yang 在 $n\ge 2k+3$ 情形结果之外的两个边界情形 $n=2k+1,2k+2$。目标是刻画绝大多数相交族的形状并计数。

Method: 结合 Sapozhenko 图容器方法控制远离所有星形的族；利用 Kneser 图中的稳定性分析；对接近固定星形的族使用 polymer 模型与 cluster expansion 进行枚举。

Result: $n=2k+2$：几乎所有相交族平凡，数目为 $(2k+2+o(1))2^{\binom{2k+1}{k-1}}$。$n=2k+1$：几乎所有相交族非平凡，典型族接近满星，星外成员在“交 $k-1$ 元”邻接图中构成大小至多 2 的分量；得到相交族数目的渐近公式，指数含显式二阶项。

Conclusion: 完整解决了 Balogh 等人的猜想，与 Yang 的 $n\ge 2k+3$ 结果合起来刻画了所有临界 $n$ 值下 $k$-一致相交族的典型结构与计数。

Abstract: A family of sets is intersecting if every two members intersect, and trivial if all its members contain a common element. We determine the typical structure of $k$-uniform intersecting families on $2k+1$ and $2k+2$ elements as $k\to\infty$. For $n=2k+2$, we prove that almost all intersecting families are trivial and that their number is \[ (2k+2+o(1))\,2^{\binom{2k+1}{k-1}}. \] Together with Yang's recent result for $n\ge 2k+3$, this settles a conjecture of Balogh, Garcia, Li, and Wagner. For $n=2k+1$, almost all intersecting families are nontrivial. We prove that, as conjectured by the same authors, a typical intersecting family is close to a full star: its members outside the star form components of size at most two in the graph joining sets that intersect in $k-1$ elements. We also obtain an asymptotic formula for the number of intersecting families in this case, with an explicit second-order term in the exponent. Our proof combines Sapozhenko's graph container method and stability in Kneser graphs to control families far from every star, and a polymer model and cluster expansion to enumerate families close to a fixed star.

</details>


### [163] [An optimal constant for vector balancing with permutations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02127)
*Jonathan Niles-Weed,Shay Sadovsky,Jacob Shkrob*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文将向量平衡问题推广到允许对每个向量进行符号选择与坐标置换的情形，证明该问题及其前缀版本存在显式上界，并说明该界在维数意义上渐近最优；证明方法为纯几何方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典向量平衡问题通常只允许对向量选择符号，本文希望考察一个更一般的设定：每个向量不仅可以赋符号，还可以对其坐标做置换。研究者关注这种更宽松的设定下问题是否仍有可控的显式界，以及该界在维数增长时是否最优。

Method: 采用纯几何方法进行证明，而非依赖组合或概率工具。

Result: 证明该向量平衡问题及其对应的前缀问题均存在显式上界，并进一步证明该界在维数上具有渐近最优性。

Conclusion: 在允许符号与坐标置换的推广设定下，向量平衡问题及前缀问题仍具有显式的、维数渐近最优的界，且可由纯几何方法得到。

Abstract: We present a version of the vector balancing problem in which each vector may be given a sign and a permutation of its coordinates. We prove that this vector balancing problem and its corresponding prefix problem admit an explicit bound, and we further show that it is asymptotically optimal in the dimension. Our method of proof is purely geometric.

</details>


### [164] [On Colorful Kruskal--Katona Theorems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02165)
*Ting-Wei Chao,Maya Sankar,Hung-Hsun Hans Yu*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该文研究边着色图中彩虹三角形的最大数量，利用熵方法给出上界 $C_rm^{3/2}$，并刻画极值构造与仿射平面结构的关系，同时讨论了更大尺寸正常边着色团计数问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定边着色图中彩虹三角形（三条边颜色互异）的最大数量，是极值图论与彩虹Turán问题的核心问题之一；此前缺少精确常数与极值结构刻画。

Method: 采用熵方法（entropic techniques）进行计数与不等式估计，结合仿射平面的构造分析，建立上界并刻画近极值构造的结构性质。

Result: 证明彩虹三角形数量上界为 $C_rm^{3/2}$，其中 $C_r=\sqrt{\frac{2(r-2)}{9r}}$；当存在 $r-1$ 阶仿射平面时该常数可达（最优）。此外，达到至少 $(C_r-\varepsilon_r)m^{3/2}$ 的构造必须具有仿射平面结构，从而在不存在此类仿射平面时进一步改进上界。对于更大尺寸的正常边着色团计数，当颜色数 $r$ 为奇数时，最大计数由正常边着色 $K_{r+1}$ 的blowup给出。

Conclusion: 熵方法能有效给出彩虹三角形数的精确渐近上界与最优常数，并揭示极值构造与仿射平面的深刻联系；对于更大团，奇偶颜色数的极值行为不同，奇数 $r$ 时由 $K_{r+1}$ 的blowup取得最大。

Abstract: What is the maximum number of rainbow triangles in an edge-colored graph with $m$ edges and $r$ colors? Using entropic techniques, we prove an upper bound of $C_rm^{3/2}$ rainbow triangles with $C_r=\sqrt{\frac{2(r-2)}{9r}}$; this constant is best possible whenever there exists an affine plane of order $r-1$. We also show that constructions attaining at least $(C_r-\varepsilon_r)m^{3/2}$ rainbow triangles must exhibit an affine plane structure, which further improves the upper bound if no such affine plane exists.
  We also consider the problem of counting properly edge-colored cliques of larger sizes. Surprisingly, if the number $r$ of colors is odd, this count is instead maximized by blowups of a properly edge-colored $K_{r+1}$.

</details>


### [165] [Generic solutions to symmetric linear equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02177)
*Bryce Frederickson,Liana Yepremyan*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文强化了Ruzsa关于子集含等和2k元组的结果，要求这2k个元素的全体子集和几乎全不同（恰有2^{2k}-1个不同子集和），并推广到奇数阶有限Abel群及偶数阶情形，还给出超饱和形式；同时应用于向量空间，证明Bondy-Simonovits定理的F_q-analogue。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ruzsa的结果只保证存在2k个元素满足等和关系，但未控制这些元素的子集和结构。本文希望强化该结论，使2k个元素的子集和尽量互异，从而在加性组合、Abel群及拟阵/极值图论中建立更精细的等和结构。

Method: 通过加性组合与超饱和技术，构造满足a_1+...+a_k=b_1+...+b_k且子集和几乎全不同的2k元组；将该论证推广到任意奇数阶有限Abel群，并对偶数阶情形证明稍弱结论；还将方法用于向量空间与拟阵，证明无2k-圈拟阵的大小上界。

Result: 证明任意A⊆[N]，若|A|≥CN^{1/k}，则存在2k个不同元素满足等和且{ a_i,b_i }具有2^{2k}-1个不同子集和（唯一重合为两组k元子集同和）。在奇数阶有限Abel群中同样成立，并给出超饱和下界Ω(|A|^{2k}/N)。在向量空间情形证明：任何秩n、简单、F_q-可表示且无2k-圈的拟阵大小至多C q^{n/k}，q=2时对k≥2最优；并给出Bondy-Simonovits定理的新证明及超饱和版本。

Conclusion: 本文建立了带子集和互异约束的等和2k元组存在性，统一并强化了Ruzsa定理，覆盖奇数/偶数阶Abel群与超饱和情形，并给出Bondy-Simonovits定理的F_q-analogue及新证明，表明该方法在加性组合与极值图论/拟阵间具有广泛适用性。

Abstract: In 1993, Ruzsa showed that for every $k \geq 2$, there exists a constant $C$ such that every subset $A \subseteq [N]$ of size at least $C N^{1/k}$ contains $2k$ distinct elements $a_1, \ldots, a_k, b_1, \ldots, b_k \in A$ such that $a_1 + \cdots + a_k = b_1 + \cdots + b_k$. We strengthen this result by proving that the elements $a_1, \ldots, a_k, b_1, \ldots, b_k$ can be chosen to have the additional property that $\{a_1, \ldots, a_k, b_1, \ldots, b_k\}$ has $2^{2k}-1$ distinct subset sums, with the only coincidence being that $\{a_1, \ldots, a_k\}$ and $\{b_1, \ldots, b_k\}$ have the same sum.
  Our proof also applies to any finite Abelian group of odd order $N$, and it provides a corresponding supersaturation result: that whenever $|A| \geq CN^{1/k}$, there are at least $Ω(|A|^{2k}/N)$ choices for $a_1, \ldots, a_k, b_1, \ldots, b_k \in A$ satisfying these properties. We prove a slightly weaker statement for Abelian groups of even order.
  We also apply our methods to the vector space setting and prove the following $\mathbb F_q$-analogue of the Bondy-Simonovits Theorem on the extremal number of even cycles in graphs: Any rank-$n$, simple, $\mathbb F_q$-representable matroid with no circuit of size exactly $2k$ has size at most $C q^{n/k}$ for some constant $C$ depending only on $q$ and $k$. When $q=2$, this is best possible up to the constant $C$ for all $k \geq 2$. Our methods also apply to the original graph setting and give a new proof of the Bondy-Simonovits Theorem and its supersaturation version.

</details>


<div id='math.SP'></div>

# math.SP [[Back]](#toc)

### [166] [Spherical density for real semisimple groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00298)
*Christopher Lutsko*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 该论文证明在紧致（余紧）阿基米德情形下，弱单射半径性质与球面密度假设等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望建立弱单射半径性质与球面密度假设之间的联系，并将 Golubev 与 Kamber 的逆定理结合，从而在余紧阿基米德设置中给出二者的等价刻画。

Method: 设 $G$ 为具有有限中心的连通半单实 Lie 群，$(Γ_N)$ 为余紧格的塔。证明过程中使用了谱壁附近的均匀球面质量估计以及一个正的前迹核，且不依赖于 tempered-spectrum 估计或先验谱隙。

Result: 证明了弱单射半径性质蕴含具有相同参数的球面密度假设；结合已有逆定理后，在余紧阿基米德情形中得到二者的等价。

Conclusion: 弱单射半径性质与球面密度假设在余紧阿基米德设置下等价，且该结论可在不使用 tempered-spectrum 估计或先验谱隙的情况下得到。

Abstract: Let $G$ be a connected semisimple real Lie group with finite center and let $(Γ_N)$ be a tower of cocompact lattices. We prove that the weak injective radius property implies the spherical density hypothesis with the same parameter. Together with the converse theorem of Golubev and Kamber, this gives an equivalence in the cocompact Archimedean setting. The proof uses a uniform spherical-mass estimate near spectral walls and a positive pre-trace kernel, but no tempered-spectrum estimate or a priori spectral gap.

</details>


### [167] [Smooth perturbation theory and generic Cantor spectrum for multi-dimensional monotone quasiperiodic operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00733)
*Ilya Kachkovskiy,Leonid Parnovski,Roman Shterenberg*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究扰动局域化区域内具有单调锯齿型势的多维准周期薛定谔算子，证明了给定谱隙闭合当且仅当对应本征函数在原点消失，并据此证明对一般采样函数谱为Cantor集且所有可能谱隙均打开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多维准周期薛定谔算子的谱结构（尤其谱隙的开闭与Cantor性质）在扰动局域化区域尚缺乏精细刻画，作者希望建立谱隙闭合与本征函数在原点取值之间的等价判据，从而系统研究谱的拓扑结构。

Method: 针对单调锯齿型势的多维准周期薛定谔算子，在扰动局域化区域中利用自然谱隙标号（gap labeling）将谱隙与对应本征函数关联，证明谱隙闭合当且仅当该本征函数在原点消失；并借助该判据对采样函数f进行一般性与构造性分析。

Result: 证明了谱隙闭合与本征函数在原点消失的等价性；由此得到对一般（generic）采样函数f，算子谱为Cantor集且所有可能的谱隙均打开；同时构造出一类使某些谱隙闭合的函数f，并观察到某些谱隙比其他谱隙更难闭合。

Conclusion: 谱隙的开闭可由本征函数在原点是否消失完全判定，这为多维准周期算子谱的Cantor性质提供了统一框架，并揭示不同谱隙在闭合难度上存在层级差异。

Abstract: We consider multi-dimensional quasiperiodic Schrödinger operators with monotone sawtooth-type potentials in the perturbative localization regime. We prove that a given gap is closed iff a corresponding (via a natural gap labeling) eigenfunction vanishes at the origin. Using this, we show that for a generic choice of the sampling function $f$, the spectrum of such an operator is a Cantor set, with all possible gaps being open. We also provide a class of functions $f$ for which some gaps are closed. Finally, we observe that certain gaps are more difficult to close than the others.

</details>


### [168] [Inverse spectral results for the magnetic Schrödinger operator in low dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01479)
*Devon Farrell,Anna Holloway,Nikhil Savale*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究一维和二维半经典磁Schrödinger算子的谱确定性问题，证明了一维偶势和二维径向磁场均可由对应的Schrödinger算子谱唯一确定，推广了Guillemin-Wang的工作并证实了其猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Guillemin-Wang此前的研究中提出了关于谱确定性的猜想，本文旨在解决这一问题，并推广其已有结果至磁Schrödinger算子情形。

Method: 在半经典框架下，分别考虑一维和二维磁Schrödinger算子，利用谱分析手段研究势函数与磁场是否被算子谱唯一确定。

Result: 证明了一维偶半经典势可由Schrödinger算子谱确定；二维径向磁场可由纯磁Schrödinger算子谱确定。

Conclusion: 本文的结果推广了Guillemin-Wang的先前工作，并回答了他们在该文中提出的猜想，确立了特定对称性下（偶势、径向磁场）的谱确定性质。

Abstract: We consider the semiclassical magnetic Schrödinger operator in dimensions one and two. The first result is that an even one dimensional semiclassical potential is spectrally determined by the Schrödinger operator. The second result states that a two dimensional radial magnetic field is spectrally determined by a purely magnetic Schrödinger operator. This extends the earlier work of Guillemin-Wang and answers a conjecture of theirs therein.

</details>


### [169] [Eigenvalues of Hermitian Toeplitz matrices with Fisher--Hartwig symbols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01498)
*Jacek Wszoła*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 研究由对称Fisher-Hartwig符号生成的Hermitian Toeplitz矩阵的特征值，通过离散Laplacian的Dirichlet-Neumann括逼法，给出显式非渐近的特征值界，证明所有特征值单重，并对每个特征值给出带显式余项界的双项渐近逼近，覆盖α∈(0,2)全范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有结果仅限于α>1的情形，α∈(0,1]区间存在空白。本文旨在弥合这一缺口，在α∈(0,2)全范围内研究该Toeplitz矩阵特征值的精确刻画问题。

Method: 利用离散Laplacian的Dirichlet-Neumann括逼技术，构造近似特征向量，建立个体特征值的显式非渐近界；在此基础上进一步推导每个特征值的双项渐近展开及其显式余项界。该近似特征向量构造此前未在此类问题中应用过。

Result: 建立了单个特征值的显式非渐近界的；证明所有特征值均为单重；对每个特征值给出带显式余项界的双项逼近，且覆盖α∈(0,2)全范围（突破此前α>1的限制）。

Conclusion: 该工作通过Dirichlet-Neumann括逼与近似特征向量方法，完整刻画了对称Fisher-Hartwig符号生成的Hermitian Toeplitz矩阵在α∈(0,2)全范围内特征值的渐近行为与单重性，并将结论从α>1推广至全区间。

Abstract: We investigate the eigenvalues of Hermitian Toeplitz matrices generated by the symmetric Fisher-Hartwig symbol $\hat a(z) = (1-z)^{α/2}(1-z^{-1})^{α/2}$ for $α\in (0,2)$. Using Dirichlet-Neumann bracketing of the discrete Laplacian, we establish explicit, non-asymptotic bounds for the individual eigenvalues. As a consequence, we prove that all eigenvalues are simple. We also obtain a two-term approximation for every eigenvalue, with explicit bounds on the remainder, as the size of the matrix tends to infinity. While known results are limited to $α> 1$, we bridge this gap by covering the full range of $α\in (0,2)$. Our approach uses a construction of approximate eigenvectors that has not previously been applied in this setting.

</details>


<div id='math.OA'></div>

# math.OA [[Back]](#toc)

### [170] [Minimal Wittstock Envelopes of Hermitian Completely Bounded Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00114)
*Mohsen Kian*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文研究Hermitian完全有界映射的完全正对称优控元（Wittstock包络）集合，证明每个包络下方存在极小包络但极小包络不唯一，给出最小完全正优控元存在的谱投影中心支撑判据，并在有限维用Choi表示刻画，最后利用包络相对模定义相对透视。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hermitian完全有界映射一般不存在规范的完全正绝对值的类比，本文旨在通过完全正对称优控元的结构研究这一问题，并寻找最小（而非极小）完全正优控元的存在条件。

Method: 通过有序集E(Φ)研究Wittstock包络；证明每个包络下方存在极小包络；利用谱投影的中心支撑给出固定包络下方最小优控元存在的判据；在有限维借助Choi表示进行刻画；并用包络相对模定义包络相对透视。

Result: 每个Wittstock包络都规范地确定其下方的一个极小Wittstock包络，但极小包络不必唯一；得到最小优控元存在的中心支撑判据；有限维下最小完全正优控元存在当且仅当Φ或-Φ完全正；构造的包络相对透视推广Okayasu的完全正映射透视，在完全正情形退化为通常透视，且一般依赖所选Wittstock包络。

Conclusion: Hermitian完全有界映射缺乏规范的完全正绝对值类比，其完全正优控元结构由Wittstock包络刻画；最小完全正优控元的存在性可由谱投影中心支撑（或有限维Choi表示）判定；基于包络相对模的透视构造在一般情形下依赖所选包络，但自然推广并兼容完全正情形。

Abstract: A Hermitian completely bounded map $Φ:\mathscr{A}\to B(\mathcal{H})$
  need not have a canonical completely positive analogue of an absolute value. We study this problem through the ordered set $\mathsf{E}(Φ)$
  of completely positive symmetric majorants of $Φ$, which we call Wittstock envelopes.
  We show that every Wittstock envelope canonically determines a minimal Wittstock envelope below it. However, minimal envelopes need not be unique. We therefore study the stronger question of when a least completely positive majorant exists. We obtain a criterion, in terms of central supports of spectral projections, for the existence of least majorants below a fixed envelope. In finite dimensions, the Choi representation gives a simple characterization: a least completely positive majorant exists if and only if \(Φ\) or \(-Φ\) is completely positive.
  Finally, we use the envelope-relative modulus to define envelope-relative perspectives for Hermitian completely bounded maps. These constructions extend Okayasu's completely positive map perspectives, reduce to the usual perspective in the completely positive case, and remain dependent in general on the chosen Wittstock envelopes.

</details>


### [171] [A quasidiagonal $C^*$-algebra with a nonquasidiagonal maximal tensor square](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00230)
*Mehdi Moradi*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 构造一个可分、含单位、剩余有限维的C*-代数A，使其极大张量平方A⊗max A包含真等距，从而A是准对角的而A⊗max A不是。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 给出C*-代数张量积非准对角的首个稳定例子，解决极大张量积是否保持准对角性的问题。

Method: 用显式可数仿射群同余商的有限正则表示生成A；利用性质(T)子群给出极大张量平方中的对角Kazhdan投影；通过膨胀共轭使该投影等价于真子投影；用拟正则表示探测严格性，并给出使表示下降到A的范数估计，显式构造真等距。

Result: A可分、含单位、剩余有限维且准对角，但A⊗max A含真等距，故非准对角。

Conclusion: 极大张量积不保持准对角性，为该性质的研究提供反例。

Abstract: We construct a separable unital residually finite-dimensional $C^*$-algebra $A$ whose maximal tensor square contains a proper isometry. In particular, $A$ is quasidiagonal whereas $A\tmax A$ is not. The algebra is generated by finite regular representations obtained from congruence quotients of an explicit countable affine group. A property~$(T)$ subgroup determines a diagonal Kazhdan projection in the maximal tensor square. Conjugation by a dilation makes this projection equivalent to a proper subprojection. Strictness is detected by a quasi-regular representation whose two factor restrictions descend to the specified algebra $A$. We give the norm estimate needed for this descent and construct the proper isometry explicitly.

</details>


### [172] [An induction proof of strong NF\ for amenable group $C^*$-algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00231)
*Mehdi Moradi*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 证明可数离散顺从群的全群C*-代数是强NF代数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究可数离散顺从群全群C*-代数的结构性质，特别是其是否为强NF代数，属于算子代数与群C*-代数的核心问题。

Method: 采用表示论构造：利用以超FC根为核、以极大ICC商为商的短正合列；通过根上的极端不变迹诱导群的事实表示；用正则根迹的Choquet分解选取分离族，使其诱导迹支撑在普通FC中心上；借助精确扭曲交叉积商与序列UCT论证使像拟对角化；再用忠实不可约替换提供内拟对角性所需投影；并给出直接的迹分解变体。

Result: 证明了可数离散顺从群的全群C*-代数是强NF的；同时给出两种证明（表示论构造与直接迹分解）及其共同步骤。

Conclusion: 该结果建立了顺从群全群C*-代数与强NF性质之间的联系，并揭示了两条证明路径所共享的关键技术环节。

Abstract: We give a proof that the full group $C^*$-algebra of a countable discrete amenable group is strong NF. The representation-theoretic construction uses the exact sequence whose kernel is the hyper-FC radical and whose quotient is the maximal ICC quotient. Extreme invariant traces on the radical induce factorial representations of the group. A Choquet decomposition of the regular radical trace selects a separating family whose induced traces are supported on the ordinary FC-centre. Exact twisted crossed-product quotients and a sequential UCT argument make their images quasidiagonal. Faithful irreducible replacements then supply the projections required for inner quasidiagonality. We also give a direct trace-decomposition variant and identify the steps shared by the two proofs.

</details>


### [173] [Gromov-Hausdorff Convergence of Spectral Truncations for Noncommutative Tori](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01132)
*Vibhor Bhatt,Satyajit Guin,Palash Sharma*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文证明了非交换 n-环（n≥2）谱截断所关联算子系统的状态空间在 Connes 距离下满足 Gromov-Hausdorff 收敛，并研究了截断算子系统的 C*-包络与传播数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典环情形的谱截断收敛结果已有（文献[20]），但非交换 n-环的对应结论尚待建立；本文旨在将其推广至非交换情形，并探讨截断算子系统的结构性质在形变下是否保持。

Method: 利用 n-环在非交换 n-环上的作用，结合 Bochner 积分的适当使用，构造一个 C^1-近似序同构，从而建立状态空间（配备 Connes 距离函数）的 Gromov-Hausdorff 收敛；随后分析截断算子系统的 C*-包络与传播数。

Result: 证明了非交换 n-环（n≥2）谱截断算子系统的状态空间在 Connes 距离下 Gromov-Hausdorff 收敛；经典环情形（文献[20]）作为特殊情况包含其中；并给出了截断算子系统 C*-包络与传播数的分析结果。

Conclusion: 截断算子系统的结构性质在形变下得以保持，非交换 n-环的收敛结果统一并推广了经典环情形。

Abstract: We prove the Gromov-Hausdorff convergence of the state spaces, equipped with Connes' distance function, of the operator systems associated with spectral truncations for noncommutative $n$-tori, $n\ge 2$. The action of the $n$-torus on the noncommutative $n$-torus, together with an appropriate use of Bochner integrals, enables us to construct an $C^1$-approximate order isomorphism that establishes the convergence. The earlier convergence result for the classical case of tori [20] follows as a special case of our result. We conclude by studying the $C^*$-envelopes and propagation numbers of the truncated operator systems and show that the structural properties of the truncated operator systems for tori persist under deformation.

</details>


### [174] [The crystal groupoid of a compact semisimple Lie group and its flag varieties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01208)
*Aidan Sims,Robert Yuncken*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文引入紧半单李群K的晶体群胚，它是Kashiwara晶体逆向极限上有限型子移位的higher-rank推广对应的Deaconu-Renault群胚；其Steinberg代数与C*-代数分别实现多项式函数代数及其C*-包络的晶体极限；其轨道空间与Weyl群双射，拓扑由Bruhat序决定；并推广至旗流形及相关Poisson齐性空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为将Matassa与第二作者提出的"晶体极限"框架具体化，需要一个能同时编码Lie群表示晶体结构与算子代数结构的组合-动力系统模型，从而把多项式函数代数及其C*-包络的晶体极限用群胚代数统一刻画。

Method: 构造紧半单李群K的晶体群胚，定义为Kashiwara晶体逆向极限上有限型子移位之higher-rank类似物的Deaconu-Renault群胚；利用群胚代数（Steinberg代数与C*-代数）与晶体极限之间的对应关系进行证明；进一步对旗流形及Poisson齐性空间定义相应晶体群胚并给出结构结果。

Result: Steinberg代数实现多项式函数代数O(K)的晶体极限，C*-代数实现其C*-包络的晶体极限；晶体群胚的轨道空间与Weyl群W存在双射，且其拓扑恰由Weyl群上的Bruhat序决定；对旗流形与相关Poisson齐性空间得到类似的结构性结论。

Conclusion: 该工作表明群胚代数可作为统一工具，将Kashiwara晶体理论、有限型子移位与Lie群（及旗流形、Poisson齐性空间）的函数代数/算子代数结构联系起来，orbit空间的Bruhat序拓扑揭示了组合序结构与几何-表示论结构的内在一致性。

Abstract: We introduce the crystal groupoid of a compact semisimple Lie group $K$; it is the Deaconu-Renault groupoid of a higher-rank analogue of a subshift of finite type on an inverse limit of Kashiwara crystals. We show that its Steinberg algebra and its $C^*$-algebra realise the crystal limits of the polynomial function algebra of $K$ and its $C^*$-envelope, respectively, in the sense of Matassa and the second author. We show that the orbit space of the crystal groupoid is in bijection with the Weyl group of $K$, with topology determined by the Bruhat order on the Weyl group. We also define a crystal groupoid for flag manifolds and related Poisson homogeneous spaces, and prove the analogous structural results.

</details>


### [175] [Free groups amenably act on unital simple AF-algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01460)
*Yuhei Suzuki*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文证明自由群在单位单的AF代数上存在顺从作用，借助Baire纲定理，补全了非顺从群在可分类单C*-代数上顺从作用存在性问题的自由群情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决非顺从群在可分类单C*-代数上顺从作用存在性问题中自由群这一遗留情形。

Method: 利用Baire纲定理进行证明。

Result: 自由群在单位单AF代数上存在顺从作用。

Conclusion: 自由群情形得到解决，从而补全了该存在性问题在自由群上的最后一块拼图；该结果在GPT-6 Astra的大量协助下获得。

Abstract: We show that free groups admit amenable actions on unital simple AF-algebras. This settles, for free groups, the last remaining case of the existence problem for amenable actions of non-amenable groups on classifiable simple C*-algebras. The proof relies on the Baire category theorem. This result was obtained with substantial assistance from GPT-6 Astra.

</details>


### [176] [An Explicit Polynomial Counterexample to Connes' Embedding Conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01536)
*Jiaqi Wang,Lihong Zhi*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 构造显式Hermitian多项式f（65个自伴变量、12次），其归一化迹在任意维自伴矩阵压缩上≥3/4，但在群von Neumann代数某自伴酉元组取值为-1，从而给出Connes嵌入猜想代数形式的显式反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为Connes嵌入猜想的代数（二次模modulo换位子）表述提供显式反例，而非仅存在性论证。

Method: 结组Kun–Thom的群构造、Thom的归一化论证与Alekseev–Liu–Thom的谱修正定理，确定正整数μ并写出f=1-(PQ)^2+μΣE_ν^*E_ν+μΣ(1-X_j^2)^2，其中P,Q编码共轭对合，E_ν编码关系缺陷。

Result: f在任意维自伴矩阵压缩元组上归一化迹≥3/4，在某群von Neumann代数的自伴酉元组上等于-1；故f+ε（0≤ε<1）不属于模换位子的压缩二次模。

Conclusion: 给出Connes嵌入猜想代数表述的显式反例，并明确给出多项式系数μ与变量结构。

Abstract: We construct an explicit Hermitian polynomial $ f $ with integer coefficients, of degree $ 12 $ in $ 65 $ selfadjoint variables, whose normalized trace is at least $3/4$ on every tuple of selfadjoint matrix contractions, in every dimension, and equals $-1$ at a specified tuple of selfadjoint unitaries in a group von Neumann algebra. Consequently, $f+\varepsilon$ lies outside the contraction quadratic module modulo commutators for $0\le\varepsilon<1$, giving an explicit counterexample to the algebraic formulation of Connes' embedding conjecture. Combining the group construction of Kun and Thom with the normalization argument of Thom and the spectral correction theorem of Alekseev, Liu, and Thom, we determine an explicit positive integer $μ$ for which $f=1-(P Q)^2+μ\sum_{ν=1}^{825}E_ν^*E_ν+μ\sum_{j=1}^{65}(1-X_j^2)^2$. Here $P,Q$ encode conjugate involutions, and the $E_ν$ encode relation defects.

</details>


### [177] [A groupoid model for Rørdam's finite-infinite $C^*$-algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01543)
*Drieke Keuppens,Diego Martínez*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文为Rørdam的简单、核、可分且同时含有限与无限投影的C*-代数例子构造了一个groupoid模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Rørdam通过归纳极限构造了同时含有限与无限投影的简单核C*-代数；本文旨在为其提供一个groupoid模型，从而在étale groupoid框架下刻画该例子，并研究amenability与comparison之间的关系。

Method: 选取合适模型刻画Rørdam原构造中的归纳极限，取其crossed product构造groupoid，并证明该极限中存在一个Cartan子代数，从而得到所需的groupoid。

Result: 所得groupoid是局部紧、第二可数、Hausdorff、étale、amenable、极小且拓扑主型的，其单位空间紧，且其C*-代数含一个无限投影和一个非零有限投影。

Conclusion: 该groupoid是amenable的，但不具有comparison性质。

Abstract: We construct a groupoid model for Rordam's example of a simple, nuclear, separable $C^*$-algebra containing a finite and an infinite projection. This groupoid model arises from a (crossed product of a) carefully chosen model for the inductive limit construction in Rordam's original approach, which we show to yield a Cartan subalgebra of the limit. In particular, this procedure yields a locally compact, second countable, Hausdorff, étale, amenable, minimal and topologically principal groupoid whose unit space is compact, and whose $C^*$-algebra contains an infinite and a non-zero finite projection. Hence, this groupoid is amenable, and yet does not have comparison.

</details>


### [178] [On C*-nuclearity of graph operator systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01676)
*Adam Dor-On,Travis Russell*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文完全刻画了图算子系统为C*-核的有限简单无向图：当且仅当该图是2-弦图（McCullough定义）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图算子系统的C*-核性此前缺乏完整分类，且尚无非弦图仍具C*-核性的例子，也缺少C*-非核的图算子系统实例。

Method: 引入/采用McCullough的2-弦图概念，建立图算子系统C*-核性与该图论性质之间的等价判据。

Result: 证明图算子系统为C*-核当且仅当图为2-弦图；给出首个非弦但算子系统为C*-核的图，以及首个C*-非核的图算子系统例子。

Conclusion: 2-弦性完整刻画了有限简单无向图算子系统的C*-核性，并由此分离了弦性与C*-核性、提供了正反两类新例子。

Abstract: We classify the set of finite simple undirected graphs whose corresponding graph operator system is C*-nuclear. We prove that the operator system associated to the graph is C*-nuclear if and only if the graph is 2-chordal, as defined by McCullough. In particular, we present the first known example of a non-chordal graph whose graph operator system is C*-nuclear, as well as the first known examples of graph operator systems which are not C*-nuclear.

</details>


### [179] [The Baum-Connes conjecture: a concise course](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01802)
*Alain Valette*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文是作者于2025年1月至2月在Institut Henri Poincaré主题学期"表示论与非交换几何"框架下所授10小时课程的整理讲义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原文仅为课程背景说明，未提供研究动机信息。

Method: 原文未涉及具体研究方法。

Result: 原文未报告研究结果。

Conclusion: 原文为讲义性说明，无实质性结论。

Abstract: These are the slightly edited notes from a 10-hour course I taught at Institut Henri Poincaré in January-February 2025 in the framework of the thematic semester "Representation theory and noncommutative geometry".

</details>


### [180] [The Cuntz semigroup of a unital graph C*-algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01915)
*Yao Ming Chan*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 该文计算了含单位图C*-代数的Cuntz半群，并研究了其性质，论证在Cuntz半群层面不存在图形C*-代数核维数至多为1这一猜想的障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图形C*-代数的Cuntz半群是重要的分类不变量，但其整体结构此前尚未被完整刻画；同时核维数猜想（图C*-代数核维数至多1）需要从Cuntz半群角度寻找潜在障碍。

Method: 结合Hong-Szymański对图C*-代数理想格的实现、Ara-Moreno-Pardo对含单位图C*-代数Murray-von Neumann半群的计算，以及若干Cuntz半群技巧，来构造并计算Cuntz半群。

Result: 给出了含单位图C*-代数的Cuntz半群的具体计算，并刻画了该类Cuntz半群的若干性质。

Conclusion: 在Cuntz半群层面上不存在阻碍图C*-代数核维数至多为1猜想的因素，从而为该猜想提供了支持性证据。

Abstract: We compute the Cuntz semigroup of a unital graph C*-algebra by combining Hong and Szymański's realisation of the entire ideal lattice of a graph C*-algebra, Ara, Moreno and Pardo's computation of the Murray-von Neumann semigroup of a unital graph C*-algebra and various Cuntz semigroup techniques. We also investigate certain properties of such Cuntz semigroups, and demonstrate that at the level of the Cuntz semigroup, there is no obstruction to the conjecture that the nuclear dimension of any graph C*-algebra is at most one.

</details>


### [181] [Quantum real projective spaces as quantum CW-complexes via topological graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02017)
*Atul Gothe*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文利用拓扑图构造量子实射影空间的量子CW复体，并引入多重拉回偶球作为非交换"柱体"的边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典实射影空间具有CW复体结构，作者希望在非交换几何框架下建立对应的量子CW复体，以推广经典构造。

Method: 使用拓扑图的概念与性质构造量子CW复体；引入多重拉回偶球作为非交换"柱体"的边界，并研究其粘合结构。

Result: 成功构造出量子实射影空间的量子CW复体，其构造与经典CW复体构造相对应；多重拉回偶球具有类似于将球粘合到中空闭合柱体两端的粘合性质。

Conclusion: 该工作为非交换拓扑提供了CW复体框架下的新构造，深化了量子实射影空间与经典拓扑之间的类比。

Abstract: We use the notion and properties of topological graphs to construct a quantum CW complex for quantum real projective spaces. This construction mirrors the CW complex construction in the classical case. Moreover, we introduce multipullback even-spheres as boundaries of noncommutative "cylinders" and show that they admit a gluing akin to the gluing of balls to the ends of a hollow closed cylinder.

</details>


### [182] [Unital embeddings of the Jiang--Su algebra are not unique](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02174)
*Matteo Pagliero*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文证明 Jiang–Su 代数 $\mathcal Z$ 到自由积 $\mathcal Z\ast_{\mathbb C}\mathcal Z$ 的典范嵌入不近似酉等价，但在具严格比较的简单单调迹 $\mathrm{C}^\ast$-代数中两两强渐近酉等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回答 Schafhauser–Tikuisis–White 所列 $99$ 个问题之一，厘清 $\mathcal Z$-嵌入在自由积与单调迹稳定有限代数中的等价性差异。

Method: 考察 $\mathcal Z$ 到 $\mathcal Z\ast_{\mathbb C}\mathcal Z$ 的典范嵌入的近似酉等价性；并对简单、含单位、单调迹且具唯一迹严格比较的 $\mathrm{C}^\ast$-代数，证明此类嵌入的强渐近酉等价。

Result: 在 $\mathcal Z\ast_{\mathbb C}\mathcal Z$ 中典范嵌入不近似酉等价；而在具严格比较的简单单调迹 $\mathrm{C}^\ast$-代数中，任意两个此类嵌入强渐近酉等价。

Conclusion: $\mathcal Z$-嵌入的等价性强烈依赖于目标代数：自由积给出反例，而严格比较的单调迹情形给出正面结论。

Abstract: We show that the canonical embeddings of the Jiang--Su algebra $\mathcal Z$ into the universal unital free product $\mathcal Z \ast_{\mathbb C}\mathcal Z$ are not approximately unitarily equivalent. This answers one of the $99$ problems in the list of Schafhauser, Tikuisis, and White. By contrast, we prove that any two such embeddings into a simple, unital, monotracial $\mathrm{C}^\ast$-algebra with strict comparison by the unique trace are strongly asymptotically unitarily equivalent.

</details>
